śrīḥ
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ॥
śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
pratiṣṭhākāṇḍam
tatra prāsādādiphalam |
tatra yamaḥ |
kṛtvā devakulaṃ śubhraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya ca devatām ॥
vidhāya vividhaṃ citraṃ tallokaṃ vindati dhruvam ॥
viṣṇuḥ [91, 10-12] |
yasya devasyāyatanaṃ karoti tasya lokam āpnoti ॥ sudhāsiktaṃ kṛtvā yaśasā virājate ॥ citraṃ kṛtvā gandharvalokam āpnoti ॥
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
yas tu devālayaṃ bhānor daruśailamayaṃ tathā  ॥
kārayen mṛnmayaṃ vāpi tasya puṇyaphalaṃ haret (re) ॥
[2]        ahany ahani yajñena yajato yan mahāphalam  ॥
tat phalaṃ labhate bhānor yaḥ kārayati mandiram  ॥
saptalokamayo bhānus tasya yaḥ kurute gṛham  ॥
pratiṣṭhāṃ samavāpnoti sa naraḥ sāptalaukikīm ॥
praśastadeśabhūbhāge praśastaṃ bhavanaṃ raveḥ  ॥
kārayitvākṣayāṃl lokān sa naraḥ pratipadyate ॥
iṣṭakācayavinyāso yāvad varṣāṇi tiṣṭhati  ॥
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi tatkartur divi saṃsthitiḥ ॥
tathā |
bhaktyā vittānusāreṇa yaḥ kuryād ālayaṃ raveḥ  ॥
āgneyaṃ yānam āruhya modate sa hi bhānunā ॥
alpe mahati vānalpaṃ phalam ādya[āḍhya?]daridrayoḥ ॥
mṛnmayāt koṭiguṇitaṃ phalaṃ syād dārubhiḥ kṛte  ॥
koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ phalaṃ syād iṣṭakāmaye ॥
dviparārddhaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ śailaje tu vidur budhāḥ  ॥
mṛcchailayoḥ samaṃ jñeyaṃ puṇyam āḍhyadaridrayoḥ ॥
yatra tatra gataḥ kuryād bālabhāve 'rkamandiram  ॥
so 'rkalokam avāpnoti vimānavaram āśritaḥ ॥
[3]        apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇaṃ sarvakāmasukhapradam  ॥
tatrārūḍho mahārāja modate śāśvatīḥ samāḥ ॥
uṣitvā bhāskarapure pūjyamānas tu daivataiḥ  ॥
kramād āgatya loke ‘smin rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ ॥
kārayec citraśāstrajñaiś citrakarmārkamandire  ॥
vicitraṃ yānam āruhya citrabhānor gṛhaṃ vrajet ॥
yāvat sa devarūpāṇi gṛharūpāṇi lekhayet  ॥
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi sūryaloke mahīyate ॥
pratimāṃ lakṣaṇavatīṃ yaḥ kārayati mānavaḥ  ॥
divākarasya tal lokam akṣayaṃ pratipadyate ॥
ye bhaviṣyanti ye 'tītā ākalpā[t] puruṣāḥ kule  ॥
tāṃs tārayati saṃsthāpya devasya pratimāṃ raveḥ ॥
tathā |
mṛddārviṣṭakaśailāḍhyaṃ yaḥ kuryād brahmago gṛham  ॥
trisaptakulasaṃyukto brahmaloke mahīyate ॥
mṛnmayāt koṭiguṇitaṃ mṛddārviṣṭakanirmite  ॥
aiṣṭikād dviguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ kṛte śailamaye gṛhe ॥
krīḍamāno 'pi yaḥ kuryāc chālāṃ yo brahmaṇo nṛpaḥ  ॥
brahmaloke sa labhate vimānaṃ sarvakāmadam ॥
tathā |
sarvayajñatapodānatīrthavedeṣu yat phalam |
tat phalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ labhed vedhaḥpratiṣṭhayā ॥
[4]        kajajaṃ sthāpayed yas tu kṛtvā śālāṃ manoramām  ॥
sarvāṅgopacitāṃ puṇyāṃ puṇyaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet ॥
mahādeva uvāca ॥
vasvaci(svasvavi)ttānusāreṇa yaḥ karoti śivālayam  ॥
tasyāhaṃ deham āśritya tiṣṭhāmi satataṃ śubhe ॥
tatra hemamayaṃ rājā prāsādaṃ yas tu kārayet  ॥
tṛṇācchannaṃ daridras tu samaṃ tad dvayam apy uta ॥
darbhastambair daridro 'pi bhāvena kurute gṛham  ॥
so 'pi rudratvam āpnoti kulaṃ cāpi samuddharet ॥
mṛddāraveṣṭakāśailaṃ yaḥ kuryāt tu śivālayam  ॥
trisaptakulasaṃyuktaḥ śivaloke mahīyate ॥
mṛnmayāt koṭiguṇitaṃ phalaṃ dārviṣṭakāmaye  ॥
aiṣṭakād dviguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ śaileye syāc chivālaye ॥
a[śme]ṣṭikasamāyuktaṃ yo 'pi kuryāc chitrālayam  ॥
vidhūya pāpasaṃghātaṃ sa gacchec chaṅkarālayam ॥
krīḍayā(mā)no 'pi yaḥ kuryād bālabhave(bhāvā)c chivālayam  ॥
rudralokaṃ sa labhate vimānaṃ sa(sā)rvakāmikam ॥
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇe sarvakāmasukhapradam  ॥
tatroṣitvā mahābhogī krīḍamānaḥ surāsuraiḥ ॥
punar āgamya loke 'smin rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ  ॥
[5] vāmanapurāṇe [97.46] |
prahlāda uvāca ॥
yaḥ kārayed vai mandiraṃ mādhavasya
puṇyāṃl lokān sa jayec chāśvatān vai  ॥
datvārāmān phalapuṣpābhipannān
bhogān bhuṅkte kāmataḥ svargasaṃsthaḥ ॥
pitāmahasya purataḥ kulāny aṣṭau tu yāni ca  ॥
tārayed ātmanā sārddhaṃ viṣṇor mandirakārakaḥ ॥
nṛsiṃhapurāṇe [32.12,14] |
yaḥ kuryāc chobhanaṃ veśma narasiṃhasya bhaktimān  ॥
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokam avāpnuyāt ॥
pratimāṃ lakṣaṇopetāṃ narasiṃhasya kārayet  ॥
sarvapāpāni saṃtyajya sa tu viṣṇupure vaset ॥
pratiṣṭhāṃ narasiṃhasya yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi  ॥
niṣkāmo naraśārdūla dehabandhāt pramucyate ॥
[sakāmo narasiṃhasya puraṃ prāpya pramodate |
manvantarāṇy anekāni labdhvā sāṅkhyaṃ vimucyate ॥ ]
narasiṃhaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yaḥ pūjām ācaren naraḥ  ॥
tasya kāmāḥ prasiddhyanti putrapautra (ā?)dayo bhuvi ॥
vidhivat sthāpayed yas tu kārayitvā janārdanam ॥
na jātu nirgamas tasya viṣṇulokāt kadācana ॥
[6] ādityapurāṇe |
lepanācchādanaṃ caiva yaḥ karoti punar navam  ॥
devasyāyatane kṛtvā na bhavet kīṭajaṃ bhayam ॥
devīpurāṇe [34.1-6] |
īśvara uvāca ॥
devyā gṛhaṃ tu yaḥ śakra kārāpayati (rayatya) śobhanam ॥
sarvopakaraṇair yuktaṃ śaṅkaṣapagamānvitam  ॥
tasmin ghaṇṭāṃ dhvajaṃ chatraṃ vitānaṃ darpaṇāni ca ॥
datvā murajavaṃśādi nṛtyaṃ saṅgītakāni ca  ॥
devīśāstrārthavettāraṃ pūjakaṃ tu niveśayet ॥
evaṃ prakurute yaś ca tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu  ॥
daśa pūrvān parāṃs tāvad ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam ॥
uddhṛtya svakulaṃ pāpād brahmaloke mahīyate  ॥
gacched bhagavatī yatra parā paramapūjitā ॥
tatra kalpāntaraṃ yāvad bhuktvā bhogān manoramān ॥
punaḥ kālād ihāyātaḥ pṛthivyām ekarāḍ bhavet ॥
pra (prā) sādādikaraṇavidhiḥ ॥
devīpuraṇe |
brahmovāca ॥
devī rudras tathā viṣṇur brahmādyāḥ surasattamāḥ  ॥
pratiṣṭhāpyāḥ śubhe sthāne anyathā te bhayāvahāḥ ॥
[7]        garbhādilakṣaṇādhātrī gandhasvādena yā bhavet  ॥
varṇena ca suraśreṣṭha sā mahī sarvakāmadā ॥
matsyapurāṇe ॥
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi prāsādavidhinirṇayam ॥
vāstau parīkṣite samyak vāstudehe vicakṣaṇaḥ  ॥
vāstūpaśamanaṃ kuryāt samidbhir balikarmaṇā ॥
jīrṇoddhāre tathodyāne tathā gṛhaniveśane  ॥
navaprāsādabhavane prāsādaparivartane ॥
dvārābhivartane tadvat prāsādeṣu gṛheṣu ca  ॥
vāstūpaśamanaṃ kuryāt pūrvam eva vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
ekāśītipadaṃ lekhyaṃ vāstumadhye tu piṣṭakaiḥ  ॥
homas trimekhale kāryaḥ kuṇḍe hastapramāṇake ॥
yāvaiḥ kṛṣṇatilais tadvat samidbhiḥ kṣīravṛkṣakaiḥ  ॥
pāla(lā)śaiḥ khadirair vāpāmārgodumbarasaṃbhavaiḥ ॥
kuśadūrvāmayair vāpi madhusarpiḥsamanvitaiḥ  ॥
kāryas tu pañcabhir bilvair bilvabījair athāpi vā ॥
homānte bhakṣyabhojyais tu vāstudeśe baliṃ haret  ॥
tadvad viśeṣanaivedyam idaṃ dadyāt krameṇa tu ॥
īśakoṇe ghṛtānnaṃ tu śikhine vinivedayet  ॥
odanaṃ sotya(jjva)laṃ dadyāt parjanyāya ghṛtānvitam ॥
jayantāya dhvajaṃ pītaṃ paiṣṭaṃ kūrmaṃ ca saṃnyaset  ॥
indrāya pañcaratnāni paiṣṭaṃ ca kuliśaṃ tathā ॥
[8]        vitānakaṃ ca sūryāya dhūmraśakraṃ tathaiva ca  ॥
satyāya ghṛtagodhūmān masyān [matsyān?] dadyād bhṛśāya ca ॥
śaṣkulīś cāntarīkṣāya dadyāt saktūṃs tu vāyave  ॥
lājāḥ pūṣṇe tu dātavyā vitathe caṇakodanam ॥
gṛhakṣatāya madhvannaṃ yamāya piśitaudanam  ॥
gandhaudanaṃ ca gandharve bhṛṅge meṣasya bhṛṅgikām ॥
mṛgāya yāvakaṃ dadyāt puṣpadantāya pāyasam  ॥
kuśastambena sahitaṃ dadyāt padmaṃ ca vāruṇe ॥
paiṣṭaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ dadyād asurāya surā matā  ॥
ghṛtaudanaṃ ca śoṣāya godhāṃ vai pāpayakṣmaṇe |
ghṛtalaḍḍukāṃs tu rogāya nāge puṣpaṃ phalānvitam  ॥
sarpirmukhyāya dātavyaṃ mudgaudanam ataḥ param ॥
bhallāṭasthānake dadyāt somāya ghṛtapāyasam  ॥
nāgāya śālukaṃ paiṣṭam adityai lopikās tathā ॥
[9]        dityai tu pūrikāṃ dadyād ity evaṃ bāhyato baliḥ  ॥
kṣīraṃ yamāya dātavyam āyavatsāya vai dadhi ॥
savitre laḍḍukān dadyāt samarīcān kuśādanam  ॥
savitur guḍapuṣpaṃ ca jayāya ghṛtacandanam ॥
vivasvate punar dadyād raktacandanapāyasam  ॥
haritālaudanaṃ dadyād budhāya ghṛtapāyasam ॥
ghṛtaudanaṃ tu mitrāya rudrāya ghṛtapāyasam  ॥
āmaṃ pakvaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ deyaṃ syād rājayakṣmaṇe ॥
pṛthvīdharāya māṃsāni kūṣmāṇḍāni ca dāpayet  ॥
śarkarāpāyasaṃ dadyād ayamle (aryamṇe) punar eva tu ॥
pañcagavyaṃ yavāṃś caiva tilākṣatahaviś carūn  ॥
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ brahmaṇe vinivedayet ॥
evaṃ saṃpūjitā devāḥ śāntiṃ kurvanti te sadā  ॥
sarvebhyaḥ kāñcanaṃ dadyād brahmaṇe gāṃ payasvinīm ॥
rākṣasīnāṃ balir deyas tv api yādṛk tathā śṛṇu  ॥
madyo(dyau)danaṃ ghṛtaṃ padmakesaraṃ haviṣānvitam ॥
īśānabhāgam āśritya carakyai vinivedayet  ॥
māṃso(sau)danaṃ sarudhiraṃ haridraudanam eva ca ॥
[10]        āgneyīṃ diśam āśritya vidāryai vinivedayet  ॥
dadhyodanaṃ sarudhiram asthikhaṇḍaiś ca saṃyutam ॥
pītaraktaṃ baliṃ dadyāt pūtanāyai tu rākṣasīm  ॥
vāyavyai pāparākṣasyai matsyamāṃsaṃ surārasam ॥
pāyasaṃ vāpi dātavyaṃ svannāmnā sarvataḥ kramāt  ॥
namaskārāntayuktena praṇavādyena sarvataḥ ॥
tataḥ sarvoṣadhī(dhi)snānaṃ yajamānas tu kārayet  ॥
prāsādabhavanodyānaprārambhe parivartane ॥
puraveśmapraveśeṣu sarvadoṣāpanuttaye  ॥
iti vāstupaśa[ma]naṃ kṛtvā sūtreṇa veṣṭayet  ॥
rakṣoghnapāvamānena sūktena bhavanādikam ॥
kṛtamaṅgalavādyena kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam  ॥
anena vidhinā yas tu pratisaṃvatsaraṃ budhaḥ ॥
gṛhe vāyatane kuryān na sa duḥkham avāpnuyāt  ॥
na ca vyādhibhayaṃ tasya na ca bandhudhanakṣayaḥ ॥
jived varṣaśataṃ svarge kalpam ekaṃ vasen naraḥ  ॥
evaṃ vāstubaliṃ kṛtvā bhajet ṣoḍaśabhāgikam ॥
[11]        tasya madhye caturbhāgaṃ tasmin gartaṃ tu kārayet  ॥
bhāgaṃ dvādaśakaṃ sārddhaṃ tatas tu parikalpayet ॥
[caturdikṣu tathā jñeyaṃ nirgamaṃ tu tato budhaiḥ  ॥ ]
caturbhāgena bhittīnām ucchrayaḥ syāt pramāṇataḥ  ॥
dviguṇaḥ śikharocchrāyo bhittyucchrāyapramāṇataḥ ॥
śikharārddhasya cārddhena vidheyā tu pradakṣiṇā  ॥
caturdikṣu tathā jñeyo nirgamasya(stu) tathā budhaiḥ ॥
garbhasūtradvayaṃ cāgre vistaro maṇḍapasya tu  ॥
āyataḥ syāt tribhir bhāgair bhadrayuktaḥ suśobhanaḥ ॥
pañcabhāgena saṃbhajya garbhamānaṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ  ॥
bhāgam ekaṃ gṛhītvā tu ghā(prā)grīvaṃ kalpayed budhaḥ ॥
garbhasūtrasamo bhāgād agrato mukhyamaṇḍapaḥ  ॥
etat sāmānyam uddiṣṭaṃ prāsādasyeha lakṣaṇam ॥
tathānyaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi prāsādaṃ liṅgamānataḥ  ॥
liṅgaṃ pūjāpramāṇena kartavyā pīṭhikā budhaiḥ ॥
pīṭhikārddhena bhāgaḥ syāt tanmānena tu bhittayaḥ  ॥
bāhyabhittipramāṇena utsedhas tu bhavet tataḥ ॥
bhittyucchrāyāt tu dviguṇaḥ śikharasya samucchrayaḥ  ॥
śikharasya caturbhāgāt kartatryā ca pradakṣiṇā ॥
[12]        pradakṣiṇāyās tu samas tv agrato maṇḍapo bhavet  ॥
tasya cārddhena kartavyas tv agrato mukhamaṇḍapaḥ ॥
prāsādau nirgatau kāryau kapolau garbhamānataḥ  ॥
arddhabhittyuchrayāt tasya mañjarī tu (-rīstu) prakalpayet ॥
maṇḍapasyārddhabhāgena śukanāsāṃ prakalpayet  ॥
ūrddhvaṃ (?) tathārddhabhāgena vedībandho bhaved iha ॥
vedyāś copari taccheṣaṃ kaṇṭhaś cāmaramārakaḥ  ॥
evaṃ vibhajya prāsādaṃ śobhanaṃ kārayed budhaḥ ॥
'        athānyac ca pravakṣyāmi prāsādasyeha lakṣaṇam  ॥
garbhamānapramāṇena prāsādaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
vibhajya navadhā garbhaṃ madhye liṅgasya pīṭhikā |
pādāṣṭakaṃ tu ruciraṃ pārśvataḥ parikalpayet ॥
mānena tena vistāro bhittīnāṃ tu vidhīyate  ॥
pādaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ kṛtvā bhittīnām ucchrayo bhavet ॥
sa eva śi[kha]rasyāpi dviguṇaḥ syāt samucchrayaḥ  ॥
caturdhā śikharaṃ bhajya arddhabhāgadvayasya ca ॥
śukanāsaṃ prakurvīta tṛtīye vedikā matā  ॥
kaṇṭham āmalasāraṃ tu caturthe parikalpayet ॥
[13]        kapolayos tu saṃhāro dviguṇo 'sya vidhīyate  ॥
śobhanaiḥ patravarṇābhair aṅgakaiś ca vibhūṣitaḥ ॥
prāsādo ‘yaṃ tṛtīyas tu mayā tubhyaṃ niveditaḥ  ॥
sāmānyam aparaṃ tadvat prāsādaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
tribhedaṃ kārayet kṣetre yatra tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ  ॥
evaṃ kṛtvātimānena brāhme bhāvavinirgatām ॥
nemīṃ pādena vistīrṇāṃ prāsādasya samantataḥ  ॥
garbhaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ kṛtvā nemyā mānaṃ bhaved iha ॥
sa eva bhitter utsedho dviguṇaḥ śikharo mataḥ  ॥
prāggrīvaḥ pañcabhāgena vistāras tasya cocyate ॥
kārayet suṣiraṃ tasya prākārasya tribhāgataḥ  ॥
prāgrīvaṃ tasya mānena kāme tasya viśeṣataḥ ॥
kuryād vā pañcabhāgena prāggrīvaṃ karṇamūlataḥ  ॥
sthāpayet kanakaṃ tadvad garttānte karṇamūlataḥ ॥
evaṃ ca trividhaṃ kuryāj jyeṣṭhaṃ madhyaṃ kanīyasam  ॥
liṅgamānānubhedena rūpabhedena vā punaḥ ॥
ete sāmānyataḥ proktā nāmataḥ śṛṇutādhunā  ॥
merumandarakailāsā[:] kṛtasiṃhamṛtā(gā)s tathā ॥
[14]        vimānachandrakas tadvac caturasras tathaiva ca  ॥
aṣṭāsraḥ ṣoḍaśāstraś ca vartulaḥ sarvabhadrakaḥ ॥
siṃhaś ca nandanaś caiva nandivarddhanakas tathā  ॥
haṃso vṛṣaḥ suparṇeyaḥ padmako 'tha samudrakaḥ ॥
prāsādā nāmataḥ proktā vibhāgaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ  ॥
śataśṛṅgaś caturdvāro bhūmikāṣoḍaśocchritaḥ ॥
nānāvicitraśikharo meruḥ prāsāda ucyate  ॥
mandaro dvādaśaḥ proktaḥ kailāso navabhūmikaḥ ॥
vimānacchandas tadvad anekaśikharānanaḥ  ॥
sa cāṣṭabhūmikas tadvat saptabhir nandivarddhanaḥ ॥
vi(viṃ)śāṇḍakasamāyukto nandanaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ  ॥
ṣoḍaśāsrasamāyukto nānārūpasamanvitaḥ ॥
anekaśikharas tadvat sarvatobhadra ucyate  ॥
candraśālāsamopeto vijñeyaḥ pañcabhūmikaḥ ॥
valabhīcchandas tadvac chukanātsa(sa)trayānvitaḥ |
vṛṣasyocchrāyatas tulyo maṇḍalaś cāsravarjitaḥ ॥
siṃhaḥ siṃhakṛtir jñeyo gajo gajasamas tathā  ॥
kumbhaḥ kumbhākṛtis tadvad bhūmikaḥ kanakocchrayaḥ  ॥
[15]        aṅgulīpuṭasaṃsthāno pañcāṇḍakavibhūṣitaḥ  ॥
ṣoḍaśāsraḥ samantāt tu vijñeyaḥ sa samudrakaḥ ॥
pārśvayoś candraśobhaś ca ucchrayo bhūmikādvayam  ॥
tathaiva padmakaḥ prokta ucchrāyo bhūmikātrayam ॥
ṣoḍaśāsraḥ sa vijñeyo vicitraśikharaḥ śubhaḥ  ॥
mṛgarājas tu vikhyātaś candraśālavibhūṣitaḥ ॥
prāggrīveṇa viśālena bhūmikā sukhavad unnataḥ  ॥
anekacandraśālas tu gajaḥ prāsāda iṣyate ॥
paryastagṛharājo vai garuḍo nāma nāmataḥ  ॥
saptabhūmyuchrayas tadvac candraśālā upāsitā ॥
bhūmikāstu ṣaḍā(ḍa)śīti bāhyataḥ sarvato bhavet  ॥
kartavyo garuḍas tadvad uchrāyād daśabhūmikaḥ ॥
padmakaḥ ṣoḍaśāstras tu bhūmidvayam athādhikaḥ  ॥
padmatulyapramāṇena śrīvṛkṣaka iti smṛtaḥ ॥
pañcāṇḍako dvibhūmiś ca garbhe hastacatuṣṭayam  ॥
vṛṣo bhavati nāmnāyaṃ prāsādaḥ sārvakāmikaḥ ॥
saptakāḥ pañcakāś caiva prāsādās te yathocitāḥ  ॥
siṃhāsyena samā jñeyā ye cānye tatpramāṇakāḥ ॥
[16]        candraśālaiḥ samopetāḥ sarve prāggrīvasaṃyutāḥ  ॥
aiṣṭikā dāvāś caiva śailā vā syuḥ satoraṇāḥ ॥
meruḥ pañcāśahastaḥ syān mandaraḥ pañcahīnakaḥ  ॥
catvāriṃśat tu kailāsaś catustriṃśad vitānakaḥ ॥
nandivardhanakas tadvad dvātriṃśat samudāhṛtaḥ  ॥
triṃśadbhir nandanaḥ proktaḥ sarvatobhadrakas tathā ॥
vartulaḥ padmakaś caiva viṃśaddhasta udāhṛtaḥ  ॥
gajaḥ siṃhaś ca kumbhaś ca valabhīchandakas tathā ॥
ete ṣoḍaśahastaḥ (-stāḥ) syuś catvāro devavallabhā |
kailāso mṛgarājaś ca vitānacchandako gajaḥ ॥
ete dvādaśahastāḥ syur eteṣāṃ siṃhanandanaḥ  ॥
garuḍo ’ṣṭakaro jñeyo haṃso daśa udāhṛtaḥ ॥
evam ete pramāṇena kartavyāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ  ॥
yakṣarākṣasanāgānāṃ mātṛhastaḥ praśasyate ॥
tathā mervādayaḥ sapta jyeṣṭhaliṅgena sukhāvahāḥ  ॥
śrīvṛkṣakādayaś cāṣṭau madhyamas ta udāhṛtāḥ ॥
tathā haṃsādayaḥ pañca kanyase(ke) śubhadā matāḥ  ॥
valabhīcchandake gaurī jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇī ॥
[17]        varadābhayadā tadvat sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ  ॥
gṛhe tu ratnamukuṭā utpalāgandha(ṅgada)dhāriṇī ॥
varadābhayadā vāpi pūjayet tāṃ sabhartṛkām  ॥
tapovanābhyāsagatāṃ pūjayet tu punaḥ punaḥ ॥
devyā(vyo) vināyakas tadvad valabhīcchandake śubhāḥ  ॥
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi maṇḍapānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam ॥
maṇḍapapravarān vakṣye prāsādasyānurūpataḥ  ॥
trividhā maṇḍapāḥ kāryā jyeṣṭhamadhyakanīyasaḥ ॥
nāmatas tān pravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ dvijasattamāḥ  ॥
puṣpakaḥ puṣyabhadraś ca suvrato 'mṛtacandanaḥ ॥
kauśilyo (ko-) buddhisaṃkīrṇo gajabhadro jayāvahaḥ  ॥
śrīvatso vijayaś caiva vāstukīrtiḥ śrutidharaḥ  ॥
yajñabhadro viśālaś ca saṃśliṣṭaḥ śatrumardanaḥ ॥
bhāgapañco nandanaś ca mānavo mānabhadrakaḥ  ॥
sugrīvo harṣaṇaś caiva karṇikāraḥ padarddhikaḥ  ॥
siṃhaś ca śyāmabhadraś ca śubhadaś ca tathaiva ca  ॥
saptaviṃśatir ākhyātā lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
[18]        stambhā yatra catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ puṣpakaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ  ॥
dvāṣaṣṭiḥ puṣpabhadras tu ṣaṣṭiḥ suvrata ucyate ॥
aṣṭapañcāśakaḥ(ka)stambhaḥ kathyate 'mṛtacandanaḥ  ॥
kauśalyo (śikaḥ) ṣaṭpañcāśac catuḥpañcāśatā punaḥ ॥
nāmnā tu buddhisaṃkīrṇo dvihīno jayabhadrakaḥ  ॥
jayāvaho dvipañcāśac chrīvatsas tu dvihīnakaḥ ॥
vijavas tu dvihīnaḥ syād vāstu triṃśad dhi mānataḥ  ॥
dvābhyām eva prahīnaś(ṇaś) ca tataḥ śrutidharo 'paraḥ ॥
catvāriṃśac chrutidharo tadvihīno viśālakaḥ  ॥
ṣaṭtriṃśakas tu saṃśliṣṭo dvihīnaḥ śatrumardanaḥ ॥
dvātriṃśadbhāgapañcas tu triṃśannandanamānavaḥ  ॥
aṣṭāviṃśaḥ(śo) mānabhadraḥ sugrīvas tadvihīnakaḥ ॥
dvātriṃśadva(ddha)rṣaṇo jñeyaḥ karṇikāraś ca viṃśatiḥ  ॥
padarddhiko 'ṣṭādaśabhir dviṣṭaḥ (ṣṭau ) siṃha ucyate ॥
[19]        dvihīnaḥ śyāmabhadras tu śubhadaś ca nigadyate  ॥
śyāmabhadraḥ kacit prokto dvādaśastambhasaṃyutaḥ ॥
maṇḍapāḥ kathitāś caite yathā lakṣaṇānvitāḥ  ॥
trikoṇaṃ vṛttam arddhendum aṣṭakoṇaṃ dvirāṣṭakam ॥
catuṣkoṇaṃ tu kartavyaṃ saṃsthānaṃ maṇḍala(pa)sya tu  ॥
rāṣṭraṃ ca vijayaś caiva āyurvarddhanam eva ca ॥
putralābhaḥ śriyaḥ puṣṭis trikoṇādikramād bhavet  ॥
evaṃ tu śubhadāḥ proktā anyathā tu bhayāvahāḥ ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ kṛtvā makhadvāraṃ prakalpayet  ॥
vistārā[d] dviguṇochrāyaṃ tattribhāgaḥ kaṭir bhavet ॥
vistārārddho bhaved garbho bhittayo 'nyāḥ samantataḥ  ॥
garbhapādena vistīrṇaṃ dvāraṃ triguṇam āyatam ॥
tathā dviguṇavistīrṇamukhas tadvad udumbaraḥ  ॥
vistārapādapratimaṃ bāhulyaṃ śākhayoḥ smṛtam ॥
tripañcasaptanavabhiḥ śākhābhir dvāram iṣyate  ॥
kaniṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ yathāyogaṃ prakalpayet ॥
vistārād dvigugocchrāyaṃ catvāriṃśadbhir unnatam |
dhanyam uttamam āyuṣyaṃ dhanadhānyakam eva ca ॥
[20]        śataṃ vāśītisahitaṃ cāntarnirgamanaṃ bhavet  ॥
adhikaṃ daśabhis tadvat tathā ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śatam ॥
śatamānaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu navatyāśītibhis tathā  ॥
daśa dvārāṇi caitāni krameṇoktāni sarvadā ॥
anyāni varjanīyāni mānasodvegadāni tu  ॥
dvāravedhaṃ tu tattvena sarvavāstuṣu varjayet ॥
vṛkṣakoṇabhramidvārāstambhakūpadhvajādibhiḥ  ॥
śvabhreṇa kulyayāviddhaṃ na dvāraṃ śubhadaṃ bhavet ॥
kṣayaś ca durgatiś caiva pravāsaḥ kṣudbhayaṃ tathā  ॥
daurbhāgyaṃ nidhanaṃ rogo daridryaṃ kalahaṃ tathā  ॥
virodhaś cārthanāśaś ca sarvavedhād bhavet kramāt  ॥
pūrveṇa phalino vṛkṣāḥ kṣīravṛkṣās tu dakṣiṇe ॥
paścimena jalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ padmotpalavibhūṣitam  ॥
uttare saralais tālaiḥ śubhāḥ syuḥ puṣpavāṭikāḥ ॥
sarvatas tu jalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sthiram asthiram eva ca  ॥
sarvataś cāpi kartavyaṃ parikhāvalayādikam ॥
yāmye tapovanasthānam uttare mātṛkāgṛham |
mahānasaṃ tathāgneye nairṛte ca vināyakāḥ ॥
vāruṇe śrīnivāsaś ca vāyavye gṛhamālikā |
uttare yajñaśālās tu nirmālyasthānam uttare ॥
[21]        vāruṇe somadaivatye balinirvāhaṇaṃ smṛtam  ॥
purato vṛkṣabhasthānam eṣa (śeṣe) syā[t] kusumāyudham ॥
jale vā pītavasanaṃ viṣṇuṃ tajjalaśāyinam  ॥
evam āyatanaṃ kuryād uccamaṇḍalasaṃyutam ॥
ghaṇṭāvitānakasutoraṇacitrayuktaṃ
nityotsavapramuditena janena sārdham |
yaḥ kārayet suragṛhaṃ vidhivad dhvajāṅkaṃ
śrīstaṃ na muñcati sadā divi pūjyate ca ॥
evaṃ gṛhārcanavidhāv api śaktitaḥ syāt
saṃsthāpanaṃ sakalavedasumantrayuktam |
govastrakāñcanahiraṇyadharāpradānaṃ
deyaṃ gurudvijavareṣu tathānnadānam ॥
atha digudakavedhādicihnam ॥
devīpurāṇe ॥
brahmovāca ॥
purasya yāmyabhāge tu na syāt sūryo na keśavaḥ  ॥
nairṛtye vā viśeṣeṇa yajamāno 'tra dakṣiṇe ॥
tatra devā na kartavyāḥ sūryaśaṃkarakeśavāḥ  ॥
yajamāno vrate(je)n nāśaṃ samānte bandhusaṃyutaḥ ॥
[22]        tanmadhye nāyakaḥ(kaṃ) kuryād yatra devaḥ svayaṃ bhavet  ॥
na sūryakeśavau madhye yatra liṅgadvayaṃ dvijāḥ ॥
evaṃ kṛte bhaved vyādhiḥ kartṛrāṣṭre nṛpāya ca  ॥
puram udvasate śīghraṃ ṣaṇmāsān mriyate kṛtī ॥
vidhānaṃ śubhadaṃ śakra kathayāmi śivoditam  ॥
nāyakaṃ sanmukhe (-khaṃ) kuryān nṛtyamānaṃ ca tāṇḍave ॥
caturbhujaṃ caikadaṃṣṭraṃ tryakṣaṃ paraśudhāriṇam  ॥
akṣasūtradharaṃ śakra nāmahastābhayapradam ॥
vidyāmantraiḥ pratiṣṭhā tu kartavyā nāyakasya tu  ॥
atha vā nairṛteḥ sūryaḥ sthāpitaḥ paścimena vai ॥
yajamāne bhaved duḥkham arthahānir bhaved dhruvam  ॥
mātaraḥ pūrvataḥ kuryān nṛtyamānottarā matāḥ ॥
valabhīcchandakaḥ(-ke ) śakra mātṛmantraiḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ  ॥
ajñānāt sthāpitaḥ kaiścil liṅgaṃ rākṣasavāyave ॥
digbhāge vai śakra tatra yatra lakṣmī[ḥ sukhāvahā  ॥
kartavyā tena vidhinā sarvāśubhavināśinī ॥
devīmadhye yathā kaiścin nāyakaḥ sthāpito bhavet  ॥
tadā kāryā umā devī tasya pīṭhe sureśvara ॥
tasya vāmagataḥ kāryo nāyako vighnanāyakaḥ  ॥
evaṃ kṛte bhavec chāntiḥ kartuś ca sthāpakasya ca ॥
anyathā jāyate doṣaḥ pūrvokto yaḥ paroditaḥ  ॥
tathā |
sūryo vahnir yāmye vā nairṛte vā hariḥ kvacit ॥
[23]        na viṣṇoḥ paścime somo vāyavye kārayec chivam  ॥
kṛte vinaśyate kartā tatsthānaṃ codvasaṃ bhavet ॥
devo na pūjyate tatra tasmin sthāne svake śubhe  ॥
vistareṇa pravakṣyāmi yena śāntiḥ śubhaṃ bhavet ॥
nagarāṇāṃ purāṇāṃ ca devatānāṃ purādiṣu  ॥
vimārgeṣu śivaḥ śastaḥ sanmukhaḥ saukhyabhāgaśaḥ ॥
sarvataḥ saumya durgātipuraṃ nandati rakṣibhiḥ  ॥
tasmād durgā prakartavyā nagareṣu purādiṣu ॥
mahālakṣmyādikāḥ śakra kṣemakārī sacaṇḍikā  ॥
maṅgalā maṅgalārūpamahīsvagni [-hiṣaghnī] śubhānanā ॥
yathāsthānavibhāgasthāḥ sarvalokasukhāvahāḥ  ॥
tathā |
yatra tatra sthitaṃ liṅgam ucchritaṃ śubhadaṃ bhavet  ॥
varṇāśramanṛpādīnāṃ tasmāt kāryaṃ samucchritam ॥
na nindāṃ kārayet śakra deva kaḥ śivasannidhau  ॥
kṛte vinaśyate rājā rāṣṭrabhaṅgaś ca jāyate ॥
tathā |
prāsādamātrā jagatī kāryā śakra na cānyathā  ॥
kanya(nīya)sānāṃ madhyamānāṃ (-dhyānāṃ) sarvatraiva viśeṣataḥ ॥
uttamānāṃ tu liṅgānāṃ tatrasthānāṃ sureśvara  ॥
prāsādāt dviguṇā kāryā jagatī triguṇā tathā  ॥
umeśaviṣṇuvighneśā eṣāṃ yā jagatī bhavet ॥
[24]        na tasya lakṣaṇaṃ samyak kiṃtu vedhe parityajet  ॥
tathā |
sarvamaṅgalā(la)jagatyavi(dhi) kāra(-re) |
pūrvāmukhe tu prāsāde garbhā īśāna nara ( gāḥ ) śubhāḥ ॥
āgneyasthā umā śakra nairṛtyāṃ vijayā śubhā  ॥
śrīś ca vāyavyabhāge tu madhye vai sarvamaṅgalā ॥
etat te kathitaṃ śakra pañcāyatanalakṣaṇam  ॥
pratyaṅmukhe ca prāsāde ye devā jagatīsthitāḥ ॥
samāsena pravakṣyāmi vāyavyaṃ tu sarasvatī  ॥
devārāmataḍāgādipurodyānagṛhāṇi ca ॥
tathā dvādaśage jīve aṣṭame vātha bhāskare |
pratiṣṭhā kāritā viṣṇor mahābhayakarī matā ॥
caturtho ravijo yadvad bhūmipaḥ surapūjita  ॥
garte puraśilānyāse varjanīyā[:] sukhārthibhiḥ ॥
devīdevī surādhyakṣa bha(ja)gatyāṃ sarvamaṅgalā  ॥
navānyāḥ paṭhitāḥ śakra devamukhyāḥ puroditāḥ ॥
[25]        mara(mahā)rājagṛhād rājā haṭṭabhaṭṭāraka bhave kṛte ॥
tadvat toyaṃ gṛhād devyā dvārabhedaṃ na kārayet ॥
prāsādavedhaṃ ca tathā varjayec ca prayatnataḥ ॥
vṛṣṭivedhe netrarogo nṛpapīḍā ca jāyate ॥
prāsādavedhena tathā pūjāvaṃśaḥ purakṣamaḥ ॥
jagatyā koṇavedhena mahāduḥkhaṃ prajāyate ॥
śivasya tu viśeṣeṇa tasmād vedhaṃ parityajet ॥
tathā ॥
prāsādavedhe kāryā tu maṅgalā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
bhūmivedhe tathā kāryau gaṇeśatryambakau śubhau ॥
dvāravedhaṃ tathā kuryān maṇḍapaṃ bhittimandiram ॥
koṇavedhe tathā lakṣmī kuryād vā mātṛmaṇḍalam ॥
anyathā na śubhaṃ śakra yajamāne purasya ca ॥
tathā |
mātṛdakṣiṇā(ṇa)diksaṃsthabhāvāt paścimena vā ॥
bhedavarjaṃ dviśālaṃ vā śukaśālam athāpi vā ॥
saptabhaumaṃ vā naikabhaumaṃ maṭhaṃ bhavet ॥
āvāsadmaḥ samākhyāto vāsārthaṃ gṛhsandhinā ॥
[26]        maṭhaṃ viṣṇuśivabrahma kiṃcid deśagataṃ śubham ॥
nābhividdhaṃ na kurvīta marmaviddhaṃ na śāntidam ॥
nākāśagaṃ prakurvīta maṭhaṃ devāt kadācana ॥
mahābhayakaraṃ taddhīpararāṣṭrajanasya ca ॥
nottare kārayec chakra maṭhaṃ devaṃ śivāt kvacit ॥
śivasya mātradhā vā kāryā sarvasukhāvahā ॥
saptapañcatribhaumaṃ vā maṭhaṃ śastaṃ sadākṛtam ॥
ekabhūmā tu maṭhikā yatīnāṃ sā sukhāvahā ॥
śivā dakṣiṇagā viprāḥ śastā viṣṇos tathottare ॥
nṛpakopi tu śivavan madhye cānṛpadevayoḥ ॥
viprā bhītipradās tatra rājyāyuḥ sukhadāḥ sadā ॥
yāmyādipūrvam ācāryā saumyagā deśagāḥ śubhāḥ ॥
maṭhaṃ ca sārvabhaumasya padmaśastaṃ sadā bhavet ॥
svastikaṃ varddhamānaṃ ca caturvakraṃ tathā śubham ॥
nandyāvartaś ca sarvākhyaṃ haṃsādīnāṃ sukhāvaham ॥
guruśanikujabudhaśukrāhaṃsaśaśirāhubhadrāḥ ॥
māla janayanti svoccagṛhatrikoṇasaṃsthā yathākramaśaḥ ॥
yātiriktaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca diśaṃ kāraṃ surālayam ॥
[27]        siddhāntaṃ rāmabhadraṃ ca dhākāryaṃ maṭhaṃ śubham ॥
anyathā kalahodvegam uccaṭhaṃ merakaṃ tathā ॥
viparītā maṭhāśaka kuryur bhūpajanādiṣu ॥
maṭhānuvattau tathā –
ānandaṃ śobhanaṃ bhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ ca caturthakam ॥
pūrvādikramayogena viṣṇoḥ kāryā naro 'nyathā ॥
saṃkīrṇe tu bhaved yatra bhūmiḥ śakra na labhyate ॥
tatra jñātvā prakartavyam eṣāṃ madhye yathāśubham ॥
pūrvāmukhe tu prāsāde mandayāmye śubhapradam ॥
paścimāsye tu prāsāde pūrvasyāṃ paścime śubham ॥
gajaṃ tathottare kāryaṃ yadīcched dīrghajīvitam ॥
ekabhūmaṃ dvibhūmiṃ vā kartavyaṃ ca na doṣabhāk ॥
viṣṇudevīṣu kartavyaṃ naikabhūmimaṭhaṃ kvacit ॥
atha pratimādilakṣaṇam ॥
matsyapurāṇe [ 228, 1 taḥ]
ṛṣaya ucuḥ ॥
kriyāyoga kathaṃ sidhyed gṛhasthādiṣu sarvadā  ॥
jñānayogasahasrād dhi karmayogo viśiṣyate ॥
[28]                sūta uvāca ॥
kriyāyogaṃ pravakṣyāmi devatārcānukīrtanam ॥
bhuktimuktipradaṃ yasmān nānyal lokeṣu vidyate ॥
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ surāṇāṃ ca devatārcānukīrtanāt ॥
devayajñotsavaṃ cāpi 'sthāpanārcanam. ucyate ॥
viṣṇos tāvat pravakṣyāmi yādṛgrūpaṃ praśasyate ॥
śaṅkhacakrakaraṃ śārṅga padmahastaṃ gadādharam ॥
cakrākāraṃ śiras tasya kambugrīvaṃ śubhekṣaṇam ॥
tuṅganāsaṃ śuktikarṇaṃ praśāntorubhujakramam ॥
kvacid aṣṭabhujaṃ vidyācvcaturbhujamvathāpi vā ॥
dvibhujaś cāpi kartavyo bhavane sasurovayaḥ ॥
devasyāṣṭabhujasyāsya yathāsthānaṃ nibodhata  ॥
khaḍgagadācakrapadmaṃ deyaṃ dakṣiṇato hareḥ ॥
dhanuś ca kheṭakaṃ caiva śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ ca vāmataḥ ॥
caturbhujasya vakṣyāmi yathaivāyudhasaṃsthitim ॥
dakṣiṇena gadāṃ padmaṃ vāsudevasya kārayet ॥
[29]        vāmataḥ śaṅkhacakre ca kartavye bhūtim icchatā  ॥
kṛṣṇāvatāre tu gadā vāmahaste praśasyate ॥
yathecchayā śaṅkhacakre copariṣṭāt prakalpate ॥
adhastāt pṛthivīdevī kartavyā pādamadhyataḥ ॥
dakṣiṇe praṇataṃ tadvad garutmantaṃ niveśayet ॥
vāmatas tu bhavel lakṣmīḥ padmahastā suśobhanā ॥
garutmān agrato vāpi saṃsthāpyo bhūtim icchatā ॥
śrīś ca puṣṭiś ca kartavye pārśvayoḥ padmasaṃyute ॥
toraṇaṃ copariṣṭāt tu vidyādharasamanvitam ॥
devadundubhisaṃyuktaṃ gandharvamithunānvitam ॥
padmapatrasamopetaṃ siṃhavyālasamanvitam ॥
[ patravallīsamopetaṃ siṃhavyāghrasamanvitam  ॥ ]
tathā kalpalatopetaṃ stuvadbhir amareśvaraiḥ ॥
evaṃvidho bhaved viṣṇus tribhāgenāsya pīṭhikā ॥
navatālapramāṇāḥ syur devadānavakinnarāḥ ॥
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mānonmānaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
jālāntarapraviṣṭānāṃ bhānūnāṃ yad rajaḥ sphuṭam ॥
trasareṇuḥ sa vijñeyo vālānaṃ tair athāṣṭabhiḥ ॥
tadaṣṭakena likhyā tu kā likhyāṣṭakaṃ tathā ॥
[30]        yavo yūkāṣṭakaṃ tadvat tair aṣṭabhir athāṅgulam ॥
svakīyāṅgulamānena mukhaṃ syād vā daśāṅgulam ॥
mukhamānena kartavyaṃ sarvāvayavakalpanam ॥
sauvarṇī rājatī vāpi tāmrī ratnamayī tathā ॥
śailī dārumayī vāpi lohasīsamayī tathā ॥
rītikā dhātuyuktā vā tāmrakāṃsyamayī tathā ॥
śubhadārumayī vāpi devatārca praśasyate ॥
aṅguṣṭhaparvād ārabhya vitasti yāvad eva tu ॥
gṛheṣu pratimā kāryā nādhikā śasyate budhaiḥ ॥
ā ṣoḍaśāt tu prāsāde kartavyā nādhikā tataḥ ॥
madhyottamakaniṣṭhā tu kāryā vittānusārataḥ ॥
dvārocchrāyasya yan mānaṃ saptadhā tat tu kārayet ॥
bhāgam ekaṃ tatas tyaktvā pariśiṣṭaṃ tu yad bhavet ॥
bhāgadvayena pratimā tribhāgīkṛtya tat punaḥ ॥
piṇḍikā bhāgataḥ proktā nātinīcā na cochritā ॥
pratimāmukhamānena sarvabhāgān prakalpayet ॥
caturaṅgulā bhaved grīvā bhāgena hṛdayaṃ punaḥ ॥
nābhis tasmād adhaḥ kāryā bhāgenaikena śobhanā ॥
[31]        nimnatve vistaratve ca aṅgulaṃ parikīrtitam ॥
nābher adhastān meḍhraṃ ca bhāgenaikena kalpayet ॥
tribhāgam āyatāv ūrujānunī caturaṅgule ॥
jaṅghe tribhāgavikhyāte pādau ca caturaṅgulau ॥
caturdaśāṅgulas tadvan mauler asya prakīrtitaḥ ॥
urdhvaṃ mānam idaṃ proktaṃ pṛthutvaṃ ca nibodhata ॥
sarvāvayavabhāgeṣu vistaraṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
caturaṅgulaṃ lalāṭaṃ syād ūrdhvaṃ nāsā tathaiva ca ॥
dvyaṅgulaṃ tu hanur jñeyam oṣṭhaś cāṅgulasaṃmitaḥ ॥
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ lalāṭaṃ ca tāvanmātre bhruvau mate ॥
arddhāṅgulā bhruvor lekhā madhye dhanur ivānatā ॥
unnatāgrād bhavet sā ca lakṣṇā tīkṣṇā praśasyate ॥
akṣiṇī dvyaṅgulāyāme tadardhaṃ caiva vistare ॥
unnatodaramadhye tu raktānte śubhalakṣaṇe ॥
tārakā ca tribhāgena dṛṣṭiḥ syāt pañcabhāgikī ॥
dvyaṅgulaṃ tu bhruvor madhyaṃ nāsāmūlam athāṅgulam ॥
nāsāgravistaraṃ tadvat puṣṭam aṅgulamānataḥ ॥
nāsāpuṭabilaṃ tadvad ardhāṅgulam udāhṛtam ॥
[32]        kapole dvyaṅgule tadvat karṇamūlād vinirgate ॥
hanvagram aṅgulaṃ tadvad vistāro dvyaṅgulo bhavet ॥
ardhāṅgulā bhruvo rājī mṛṇālasadṛśī samā ॥
ardhāgulasamas tadvad uttaroṣṭhas tu vistare ॥
vistārasadṛśaṃ tadvan nāsāpuṭadalaṃ bhavet ॥
sṛkvaṇī 'īkṣṇatulye tu karṇamūlāt ṣaḍaṅgulam ॥
karṇau tu bhrūsamau jñeyau ūrdhvaṃ tu caturaṅgulam ॥
dvyaṅgulau karṇapārśvau tu mātrām ekāṃ tu vistṛtau ॥
karṇayor upariṣṭāt tu mastakaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam ॥
lalāṭaṃ pṛṣṭhato 'rdhena proktam aṣṭādaśāṅgulam ॥
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaś cāpi pariṇāhaḥ śiro gataḥ ॥
sakeśanicayasyāsya dvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ ॥
keśās tadvaj jñeyās tadvad aṅgulāni tu ṣoḍaśa ॥
grīvāmadhye parīṇāhaś caturviṃśatikāṅgulaḥ ॥
aṣṭāṅgulā bhabed grīvā pṛthutvena viśeṣate ॥
stanagrīvāntaraṃ proktam ekatālaṃ svayaṃbhuvā ॥
stanayor antaraṃ tadvad vā daśāṅgulam iṣyate ॥
stanayor maṇḍalaṃ tadvad dvyaṅgulaṃ parikīrtitam ॥
[33]        cūcukau maṇḍalasyāntar yavamātrāv ubhau smṛtau ॥
dvitālaś cāpi vistāro vakṣaḥsthalam udāhṛtam ॥
kakṣe ṣaḍaṅgule prokte bāhumūlastanāntare ॥
caturdaśāṅgulau pādau aṅguṣṭhas tu dviraṅgulaḥ ॥
pañcāṅgulaparīṇāham aṅguṣṭhāntam athottaram ॥
aṅguṣṭhasya samā tadvad āyāme syāt pradeśinī ॥
tasyāḥ ṣoḍaśabhāgena hīyate madhyamāṅgulī ॥
anāmikāṣṭabhāgena kaniṣṭhā cāpi hīyate ॥
parvatrayeṇa cāṅgulyau gulphau dvyaṅgulakau smṛtau ॥
pārṣṇir dvyaṅgulamātrāstu kalayoccaiḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
dviparvāṅguṣṭhakaḥ proktaḥ pariṇāhena suvratāḥ ॥
pradeśinī parīṇāhas tryaṅgulaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ॥
kanyasā cāṣṭabhāgena hīyate kramaśo dvijāḥ ॥
aṅgulenocchrayaḥ kāryo hy aṅguṣṭhasya viśeṣataḥ ॥
tadardhena tu śeṣāṇām aṅgulīnāṃ samucchrayaḥ ॥
jaṅghāgre pariṇāhas tu aṅgulāni caturdaśa ॥
kuṇṭhāmadhye parīṇāhas tathaivāṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ ॥
jānūcchrayo ‘ṅgulaḥ prokto maṇḍalaṃ tu triraṅgulam ॥
[34]        ūrumadhye parīṇāha aṣṭāviṃśatikāṅgulaḥ ॥
ekaviṃśopariṣṭāc ca vṛṣaṇau tu triraṅgulau ॥
dvyaṅgulaṃ ca tathā meḍhraṃ pariṇāhe ṣaḍaṅgulam  ॥ ॥
maṇibaddhodayo vidyāt kośarekhās tathaiva ca  ॥ ॥
maṇikasya pariṇāhaś caturaṅgula iṣyate ॥
vistāreṇa bhavet tadvat kaṭir aṣṭādaśāṅgulā ॥
dvātriṃśatis tathā strīṇāṃ stanau ca dvādaśāṅgulau ॥
nābhimadhye parīṇāho dvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ ॥
puruṣe pañcapañcāśat kaṭyāṃ caiva tu veṣṭanam ॥
kakṣayor upariṣṭāt tu skandhau proktau ṣaḍaṅgulau ॥
aṣṭāṅgulāt tu vistāro grīvāṃ caiva vinirdiśet ॥
pariṇāhe tathā grīvāṃ kalā dvādaśa nirdiśet ॥
āyāmo bhujayos tadvad dvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ ॥
kāryaṃ tu bāhuśikharaṃ pramāṇe ṣoḍaśāṅgulam ॥
ūrddhvaṃ yad bāhuparyantu vidyād aṣṭādaśāṅgulam ॥
tathaikāṅgulahīnaṃ tu dvitīyaṃ parva ucyate ॥
bāhumadhye parīṇāho bhaved aṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ ॥
ṣoḍaśoktaḥ prabāhus tu ṣaṭkalograkaropamaḥ ॥
[35]        saptāṅgulaṃ karatalaṃ pañca madhyāṅgulī matā ॥
anāmikā madhyamāyāṃ saptabhāgena hīyate ॥
tasyās tu pañcabhāgena kaniṣṭhā parihīyate ॥
madhyamāyās tu hīno vai pañcabhāgena tarjanī ॥
aṅguṣṭāt tarjanīmūlād adhaḥ proktas tu madhyamaḥ ॥
aṅguṣṭapariṇāhas tu vivijñeyaś caturaṅgulaḥ ॥
śeṣāṇām aṅgulīnāṃ tu bhāgo bhāgena hīyate ॥
madhyamā madhyaparvaṃ tu aṅguludvayam āyatam ॥
yavo yavena sarvāsāṃ tasyās tasyāḥ prahīyate ॥
aṅguṣṭaparvamadhyaṃ tu tarjanyāṃ sadṛśaṃ bhavet ॥
yavadvayādhikaṃ tadvad agraparva udāhṛtam ॥
pūrvārddhena (?) nakhān vidyād aṅgulīṣu samantataḥ ॥
snigdhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ prakurvīta īṣadvakraṃ tathāgrajam ॥
nimnapṛṣṭhaṃ bhaven madhyaṃ pārśvataḥ kalayochritam ॥
tathaiva keśavallaryaḥ skandhopari daśāṅgulāḥ ॥
striyaḥ kāryās tu vidvadbhiḥ stanorujaghanādhikāḥ ॥
caturdaśāṅgulāyāmam udaraṃ tāsu nirdiśet ॥
nānābharaṇasaṃpannāḥ kiñcicchlakṣṇabhujāḥ śubhāḥ ॥
kiñciddīrghaṃ bhaved vaktram alakāvalir uttamā ॥
nāsā grīvā lalāṭaṃ ca sārddhamātraṃ triraṅgulam ॥
adhyarddhāṅgulavistāraḥ śasyate 'dharapallavaḥ ॥
[36]        adhikaṃ netrayugmaṃ tu caturbhāgena nirdiśet ॥
grīvāvalīś ca kartavyā kiṃcidardhāṅgulochrayā ॥
evaṃ nārīṣu sarvāsu devānāṃ pratimāsu ca ॥
navatālam idaṃ proktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pāpanāśanam ॥
[ matsyapurāṇa – 259] |
[ sūta uvāca ]
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi devākārān viśeṣataḥ ॥
daśatālasmṛto rāmo balir vairocanis tathā ॥
vārāho nārasiṃhaś ca saptatālaś ca vāmanaḥ ॥
matsyakūrmau ca nirdiṣṭau yathāśobhaṃ svayaṃbhuvā ॥
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi rudrārcakāram uttamam ॥
sapīnorubhujaskandhas taptakāñcanasuprabhaḥ ॥
śuklānuraśmisaṅghātaṃś cadrāṅkitajaṭo vibhuḥ ॥
dviraṣṭavarṣākṛtibhṛj jaṭāmukuṭaśobhitaḥ ॥
bāhūvāraṇahastābhau vṛttajaṅghorumaṇḍalaḥ ॥
ūrddhvakeśas tu kartavyo dīrghāyatavilocanaḥ ॥
vyāghracarmaparīdhānaḥ kaṭisūtratrayānvitaḥ ॥
hārakeyūrasaṃpanno bhujaṃgābharaṇas tathā ॥
bāhavaś cāpi kartavyā nānābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ ॥
[37]        pīnorugaṇḍaphalakaḥ kuṇḍalābhyām alaṅkṛtaḥ ॥
ājānulambabāhuś ca saumyamūrtiḥ suśobhanaḥ ॥
kheṭakaṃ vāmahaste tu khaḍgaś caiva tu dakṣiṇe ॥
śaktiṃ daṇḍaṃ triśūlaṃ tu dakṣiṇeṣu niveśayet ॥
kapālaṃ vāmapārśve tu nāgaṃ khaṭvāṅgam eva ca ॥
ekaś ca varado hasto ghṛtākṣavalayo 'paraḥ ॥
vaiśākhaṃ sthānakaṃ kuryān nṛtyābhinayasaṃsthitau ॥
nṛtye daśabhujaḥ kāryo gajāsuravadhe tathā ॥
tathā tripuradāhe ca bāhavaḥ ṣoḍaśaiva tu ॥
śaṅkhacakragadāḥ śaṅku ghaṇṭā tatrādhikā bhavet ॥
tathā dhanuḥ pinākaṃ ca śaro viṣṇumayas tathā  ॥ ॥
caturbhujo ‘ṣṭabāhur vā jñānayogeśvaro mataḥ ॥
tīkṣṇanāsāgradaśanaḥ karālavadano mahān ॥
bhairavaḥ śasyate loke pratyāyatanasaṃsthitaḥ ॥
na mūlāyatane kāryo bhairavas tu bhayaṅkaraḥ ॥
narasiṃho varāho vā tathānye ‘pi bhayaṅkarāḥ ॥
nādhikāṅgī na hīnāṅgī kartavyā devatā kvacit ॥
svāminaṃ ghātayen nyūnā karālavadanā tathā ॥
adhikā śilpinaṃ hanyāt kṛśā caivārthanāśinī ॥
[38]        kṛśodarī tu durbhikṣaṃ nimāṃsā dhananāśinī ॥
duḥkhadā hīnavaktrā tu pāṇipādakṛśā tathā ॥
hīnāṅgī hīnajaṅghā ca bhramonmādakarī nṛṇām ॥
śuṣkavaktrā tu rājānaṃ kaṭihīnā ca yā bhavet ॥
pāṇipādavihīnāyāṃ jāyate narako mahān ॥
jaṅghāhīnā tu jānvor vā śatrukalyāṇakāriṇī ॥
putramitravināśāya hīnā vakṣaḥsthalena yā ॥
saṃpūrṇāvayavāṅgī tu āyurlakṣmīpradā sadā ॥
evaṃ lakṣaṇam āsādya kartavyaḥ parameśvaraḥ ॥
stūyamānaḥ suraiḥ sarvaiḥ samantāt kalpayed bhavam ॥
śakreṇa nandinā caiva mahākālena śaṅkaram ॥
praṇatā lokapālāś ca pārśve tu gaṇanāyakāḥ ॥
nandibhṛṃgiriṭiś caiva bhūtavetālasaṃvṛtaḥ ॥
sarve hṛṣṭās tu kartavyāḥ stuvantaḥ parameśvaram ॥
gandharvavidyādharakinnarāṇām
athāpsaro guhyakanāyakānām ॥
gaṇair anekaiḥ śataśo mahendra-
munipravīrair api nṛtyamānam ॥
dhṛtākṣasūtraiḥ śataśaḥ pravāla-
puṣpopahārapracayaṃ dadadbhiḥ ॥
saṃstūyamānaṃ bhagavantam ādyaṃ
netratrayeṇāmaravaryapūjyam ॥
[sūta uvāca] |
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi ardhanārīśvaraṃ haram ॥
[39]        arddhena devadevasya nārīrūpaṃ suśobhanam ॥
īśārddhe tu jaṭābhāro bālendukalayā yutaḥ ॥
umārddhe vāpi dātavyau sīmantatilakāv ubhau ॥
vāsukiṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe vāme kuṇḍalam ādiśet ॥
bālikāropariṣṭāt tu kapālaṃ dakṣiṇe kare ॥
triśūlaṃ vāpi kartavyaṃ devadevasya śūlinaḥ ॥
vāmato darpaṇaṃ kuryād utpalaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ ॥
vāmabāhuṃ ca kartavyaḥ keyūravalayānvitaḥ ॥
upavītaṃ ca kartavyaṃ maṇimuktāmayaṃ tathā ॥
stanabhāram athorddhave (?) tu vāme pītaṃ prakalpayet ॥
vāmārddham ujvalaṃ kuryāt śroṇyarddhaṃ ca tathaiva ca ॥
liṅgavan mūrddhakaṃ kuryāt vyāghrājinakṛtāmbaram ॥
vāme lambaparīdhānaṃ kaṭisūtratrayānvitam ॥
nānāratnasamopetaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ bhujagānvitam ॥
devasya dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ padmopari susaṃsthitam ॥
kiṃcidūrdhvaṃ tathā vāme bhūṣitaṃ nūpureṇa tu ॥
ratnair vibhūṣitān kuryād aṅgulīṣv aṅgulīyakam ॥
sālaktakaṃ tathā pādaṃ pārvatyā darśayet sadā ॥
arddhanārīśvarasyedaṃ rūpam asminn udāhṛtam ॥
umāmaheśvarasyāpi lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
[40]        saṃsthānaṃ tu tayor vakṣye līlālalitavibhramam ॥
caturbhujaṃ dvibāhuṃ vā jaṭābhārendubhūṣitam ॥
locanatrayasaṃyuktam anekaskandhapāṇikam ॥
dakṣiṇenotpalaṃ śūlaṃ vāme kuvalayaṃ kare ॥
dvīpicarmaparīdhānaṃ nānāratnopaśobhitam ॥
supratiṣṭhaṃ suveṣaṃ ca tathārddhenduhutāśanam ॥
vāme tu saṃsthitā devī tasyādho bāhugūhitā ॥
śirobhūṣaṇasaṃyuktair alakair lalitānanā ॥
śevālikā karṇavatī lalāṭatilakojvalā ॥
maṇikuṇḍalasaṃyuktā karṇikābharaṇānvitā ॥
hārakeyūrabahulā haravaktravilokinī ॥
vāmāṃsaṃ devadevasya spṛśantī līlayā tataḥ ॥
dakṣiṇaṃ tu bahiḥ kṛtvā bāhuṃ dakṣiṇatas tathā ॥
kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe kukṣau spṛśaty aṅgulijaiḥ kvacit ॥
vāme ca darpaṇaṃ dadyād utpalaṃ vā suśobhanam ॥
kaṭisūcatrayaṃ caiva nitambe syāt salaṃbakam ॥
jayāṃ ca vijayāṃ caiva kārtikeyavināyakau ॥
pārśvayor darśayet tatra toraṇe gaṇaguhyakān ॥
pretā vidyādharās tadvad vīṇā vānarāpsaro gaṇāḥ ॥
etadrūpam umeśasya kartavyaṃ bhūtim icchatā ॥
[41]        śivanārāyaṇaṃ vakṣye sarvapāpavināśanam ॥
vāmārddhe mādhavaṃ kuryād dakṣiṇe śūlapāṇinam ॥
bāhudvayaṃ ca kṛṣṇasya maṇikeyūrabhūṣitam ॥
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ śāntam āraktāṅgulivibhramam ॥
[cakrasthāne gadāṃ vāpi pāṇau dadyādcadhastale ॥
śaṅkhaṃ cottare dadyāt kaṭyardhaṃ bhūṣaṇojjvalam ॥
pītavastraparīdhānaṃ caraṇaṃ maṇibhūṣaṇam ॥]
dakṣiṇārddhe jaṭājūṭam arddhendukṛtalakṣaṇam ॥
bhujaṅgahāravalayaṃ varadaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ karam ॥
dvitīyaṃ cāpi kurvīta triśūlavaradhāriṇam ॥
vyālopavītasaṃyuktaṃ kaṭyarddhaṃ kṛttivāsasam ॥
maṇiratnaiś ca saṃyuktaṃ pādaṃ nāgavibhūṣitam ॥
śivanārāyaṇasyaivaṃ kalpayed rūpam uttamam ॥
mahāvarāhaṃ vakṣyāmi padmahastaṃ gadādharam ॥
tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrāgratorāsyaṃ medinī vāmakūrpare ॥
daṃṣṭrāgreṇoddhṛtāṃ dāntāṃ dharaṇīm utpalānvitām ॥
vismayotphullanayanāṃ tūpariṣṭāt prakalpayet ॥
[42]        dakṣiṇaṃ kaṭisaṃsthaṃ tu karas tasya prakalpayet ॥
kūrmopari tathā pādam ekaṃ nāgendramūrddhani ॥
saṃstūyamānaṃ lokeśaiḥ samantāt parikalpayet ॥
nārasiṃhaṃ prakartavyaṃ bhujāṣṭakasamanvitam ॥
raudraṃ siṃhāsanaṃ tadvad vidāritamukhekṣaṇam ॥
tam apīnasaṭākīrṇaṃ dārayantaṃ diteḥ sutam ॥
vinirgatāntrajālaṃ tu dānavaṃ parikalpayet ॥
vamantaṃ rudhiraṃ samyak kathayāmi yathā bhavet ॥
siṃhapadmāsanā saumyā jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitā ॥
śaṅkhābjaśūlavaradāḥ kamaṇḍalukarā śubhāḥ ॥
caturbhujā samākhyātā tathā cāṣṭabhujān śṛṇu ॥
varadābhayakhaḍgam akṣasūtraṃ ca dakṣiṇe ॥
dakṣiṇe śūlacāpaṃ ca caturthe kaṭakaṃ bhavet ॥
evaṃ cāṣṭabhujā devī maṅgalā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
kartavyā caiva prāsāde gṛhe vāpi viśeṣataḥ ॥
śakradevī prakartavyā na gṛhe sajaṭā śubhā ॥
ūrdhvasthitā gṛhe ghorā raudrī vāpi nareśvara ॥
nagare kamalādhyakṣa padmāsīnā śubhā matā ॥
hemarājatatāmrī vā mahāratnavibhūṣitā ॥
[43]        śailī vā kāñcanī vātha kāryā devī na cānyathā ॥
haimī tāmrī sīsakī vā rājyaṃ putrāṃś ca rājatī ॥
ārogyaṃ caiva saubhāgyaṃ dadyād devī tu tāmrajā ॥
prāsāde śailajā śreṣṭhā putrāyurdhanadā matā ॥
rājyadā sīsakenāpi sthāpitā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
śailajā na gṛhe pūjyā abhāvāt kāṣṭhajā śubhā ॥
śrīvṛkṣaś candanonimbor iṣṭaḥ puradā ....ca ॥
ti.... ko .... ko vipra śrīparṇī cāṣṭamī matā ॥
kiṃstu kāmyādhikāreṇa nimbāriṣṭamayī matā ॥
kanyasā madhyamā jyeṣṭhā pratimā śubhadā matā ॥
saptāṅgulāṃ samārabhya yāvac ca dvādaśāṅgulā ॥
gṛheṣv arcā samākhyātā prāsāde vādhikā śubhā ॥
matsyapurāṇe (260, 55 taḥ) –
kātyāyanyāḥ pravakṣyāmi rūpaṃ daśabhujaṃ tathā ॥
trayāṇām api devānām anukārānukāriṇīm ॥
jaṭājūṭasamāyuktām arddhendukṛtalakṣaṇām ॥
locanatrayasaṃyuktāṃ padmendusadṛśānanām ॥
atasīpuṣpavarṇābhāṃ supratiṣṭhāṃ sulocanām ॥
navayauvanasaṃpannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
sucārudarśanāṃ tadvat pīnonnatapayodharām ॥
tribhaṅgasthānasaṃsthānāṃ mahiṣāsuramardinīm ॥
[44]        triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇe dadyāt khaḍgaṃ cakraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
tīkṣṇabāṇaṃ tathā śaktiṃ vāmato 'pi nibodhata ॥
kheṭakaṃ pūrṇacāpaṃ ca pāśam aṅkuśam eva ca ॥
ghaṇṭāṃ vā paraśuṃ vāpi vāmataḥ saṃniveśayet ॥
adhastān mahiṣaṃ tadvad viśiraskaṃ pradarśayet ॥
śiraśchedobhavaṃ tadvad dānavaṃ khaḍgapāṇinam ॥
hṛdi śūlena nirbhinnaṃ niryadantravibhūṣitam ॥
raktāraktīkṛtāṅgaṃ ca raktasrāvāvilekṣaṇam ॥
veṣṭitaṃ nāgapāśena bhrukuṭībhīṣaṇānanam ॥
vamadrudhiravaktraṃ ca devyāḥ siṃhaṃ pradarśayet ॥
devyās tu dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ samaṃ siṃhopari sthitam ॥
kiṃcid ūrdhvaṃ tathā vāmam aṅguṣṭhaṃ mahiṣopari ॥
stūyamānaṃ ca tadrūpam amaraiḥ saṃniveśayet ॥
idānīṃ surarājasya rūpaṃ vakṣye viśeṣataḥ ॥
sahasranayanaṃ devaṃ mattavāraṇasaṃsthitam ॥
pṛthūruvakṣovadanaṃ siṃhaskandhaṃ mahābhujam ॥
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharaṃ pīnagātrabhujekṣaṇam ॥
vajrotpaladharaṃ tadvan nānābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
pūjitaṃ devarodgāraṃ bhṛkuṭīvadanekṣaṇam ॥
yudhyamānaṃ tu kurvīta kvacit karaṇabandhanaiḥ ॥
pariśrāntena daityena tarjamānaṃ muhur muhuḥ ॥
[45]        daityān pradarśayet tatra khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇaḥ ॥
stūyamānaṃ tadā viṣṇuṃ darśayed amarādhipaiḥ ॥
tathā trivikramaṃ vakṣye brahmāṇḍakramaṇolbaṇam ॥
pādapārśve tathā bāhum upariṣṭāt prakalpayet ॥
adhastād vāmanaṃ tadvat kalpayet sakamaṇḍalum ॥
dakṣiṇe daṇḍakaṃ dadyān mukhaṃ dīnaṃ prakalpayet ॥
bhṛṅgāradhāriṇaṃ tadvad baliṃ tasya ca pārśvataḥ ॥
bandhanaṃ cāpi kurvīta garuḍaṃ cāsya darśayet ॥
matsyarūpaṃ tathā matsyaṃ kūrmaṃ kūrmākṛtiṃ nyaset ॥
evaṃrūpas tu bhagavān kāryo nārāyaṇo hariḥ ॥
brahmā kamaṇḍaludharaḥ kartavyaḥ sa caturmukhaḥ ॥
haṃsārūḍhaḥ kvacit kāryaḥ kvacic ca kamalāsanaḥ ॥
varṇena padmagarbhābhaś caturbāhuḥ śubhekṣaṇaḥ ॥
kamaṇḍalur vāmakare sruvaṃ haste tu dakṣiṇe ॥
vāme daṇḍadharas tadvat sruvaṃ cāpi pradarśayet ॥
munibhir devagandharvaiḥ stūyamānaṃ samantataḥ ॥
kurvāṇam iva lokāns trīn śuklāmbaradharaṃ [?] vibhum ॥
mṛgacarmadharaṃ vāpi divyayajñopavītinam ॥
ājyasthālīṃ nyaset pārśve vedāṃś ca caturaḥ punaḥ ॥
vāmapārśve tu sāvitrī dakṣiṇe ca sarasvatī ॥
[46]        agre ca ṛṣayas tadvat kāryāḥ paitāmahe pade ॥
kārtikeyaṃ pravakṣyāmi taruṇādityavarcasam ॥
kamalodaravarṇābhaṃ kumāraṃ sukumārakam ॥
gaṇḍakaiś cīrakair yuktaṃ mayūravaravāhanam ॥
sthānīyake tu nagare bhujān dvādaśa kalpayet ॥
caturbhujaḥ ka[rvaṭe syāt] vane grāme dvibāhukaḥ ॥
śaktiḥ pāśas tathā khaḍgaḥ śaraḥ śūlaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
varadaś caikahastaḥ syād atha vābhayado bhavet ॥
ete dakṣiṇato jñeyāḥ keyūrakaṭakojvalāḥ ॥
dhanuḥ patākāmuṣṭiś ca tarjanī tu prasāritā ॥
kheṭakaṃ tāmracūḍaś ca vāmahaste tu śasyate ॥
dvibhujaś cet kare śaktir vāme syāt kukkuṭopari ॥
caturbhuje śaktipāśau vāmato dakṣiṇe tv asiḥ ॥
varado ‘bhayado vāpi dakṣiṇaḥ syād dvitīyakaḥ [?] ॥
vināyakaṃ ca vakṣyāmi gajavaktraṃ trilocanam ॥
lambodaraṃ caturbāhuṃ vyālayajñopavītinam ॥
śūrpakarṇaṃ bṛhattuṇḍam ekadaṃṣṭraṃ pṛthūdaram ॥
svadattadakṣiṇakare kamalaṃ cāpare tathā ॥
laḍḍukaṃ paraśuṃ caiva vāmataḥ parikalpayet ॥
[47]        bṛhantaṃ kṣipragamanaṃ pīnaskandhaṃ dvipāṇinam ॥
yuktaṃ buddhikubuddhibhyām adhastān mūṣakānvitam ॥
devīpurāṇe –
brahmovāca |
avatārāḥ samāsena kathitā sarvamaṅgalā  ॥
pratimālakṣaṇaṃ samyak kathayāmi yathā bhavet ॥
siṃhapadmāsanā saumyā jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitā  ॥
salakṣmasūtravaradā kamaṇḍaludharā śubhā ॥
caturbhujā samākhyātā tathā aṣṭabhujāṃ śṛṇu  ॥
varadam abhayaṃ khaḍgaṃ akṣasūtraṃ tu dakṣiṇe ॥
darpaṇaṃ śūlacāpaṃ ca caturthe kheṭakaṃ bhavet  ॥
evam aṣṭabhujā devī maṅgalā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
kartavyā caiva prāsāde gṛhe vāpi viśeṣataḥ  ॥
kiṃ tu te kathayiṣyāmi jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇīm ॥
prādeśamātra (?) kartavyā na gṛhe sajaṭā śubhā  ॥
.... na kartavyā kṛtā bhayakarī matā  ॥
tathā caturbhujā kāryā prāsāde vā gṛhe 'tha vā ॥
ūrdhvaṃ sthitā gṛhe ghorā raudrā vāpi nareśvara  ॥
nagare kamalādhyakṣā padmāsīnā śubhā matā ॥
[48]        hemarājata.... stavo mahāphaṇivibhūṣitā  ॥
śailī vā tālinī cātha kāryā devī na cānyathā ॥
hemotthā rājyadaghaṃ [?] sā rājyaputrāṃś ca rājatī ॥
ārogyaṃ caiva saubhāgyaṃ dadyād devī .... ॥
prāsāde śailajā śreṣṭhā putrāmūrdhalakāmadā  ॥ [ sic ]
rājyadā ca viśeṣeṇa sthāpitā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
śailajālagṛhe pūjyā abhāvāt kāṣṭhajā śubhā  ॥
śrīvṛkṣacandano nimbariṣṭaḥ puradāra ca ॥
bhitvā kāmadhuko vipra śrīparṇī cāṣṭamī matā  ॥
kiṃ tu kāmyādhikāreṇa nimbāriṣṭamayī tathā ॥
kaniṣṭhāmadhyamo jyeṣṭha pratimā śubhadā matā  ॥
maṇḍā .... lāsam ārabhya yāvac ca dvādaśāṅgulā |
gṛhesarcā samākhyātā grāmādevyādhikā śubhā  ॥
matsyapurāṇe –
kātyāyanyāḥ pravakṣyāmi rūpaṃ daśabhujaṃ tathā ॥
trayāṇām api devānāṃ .... rāmukāriṇīṃ ॥
atasīpuṣpavarṇābhāṃ supratiṣṭhāṃ sulocanām  ॥
[49]        navayauvanasampannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
sucārudaśanāṃ tadvat pīnonnatapayodharām  ॥
tribhaṅgasthānasaṃyuktāṃ mahiṣāsuramardinīm ॥
triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇe dadyāt śaṅkhacakraṃ tathaiva ca  ॥
tīrṇavānaṃ tathā śaktiṃ vāmato vinibodhata ॥
kheṭakaṃ pūrṇacāpaṃ ca pāśam aṅkuśam eva ca  ॥
ghaṇṭāṃ vā paraśuṃ cāpi vāmataḥ parikalpayet  ॥
adhastān mahiṣaṃ tadvat viśiraskaṃ pradarśayet ॥
śiracchedodbhavaṃ tadvat dānavaṃ khaḍgapāṇinam  ॥
hṛdi śūle vinirbhinnaṃ niryadantravibhūṣitam ॥
cakravartikṛtāṅgasya raktaviṭcaritekṣaṇam  ॥
veṣṭitannāgapāśena bhṛkuṭībhīṣaṇānanam ॥
vamadrudhiraraktaṃ ca devyāḥ siṃhaṃ pradarśayet  ॥
devyās tu dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ .... siṃhopari sthitam ॥
kiṃcit pūrṇas tathā vāmam aṅguṣṭhaṃ mahiṣopari  ॥
stūyamānaś ca tadrūpam amaraiḥ saṃniveśayet ॥
tadānīṃ surarājasya rūpalakṣe viśeṣataḥ  ॥
sahasranayanaṃ devaṃ mattavārasamanvitam ॥
pūjyoktarakṣovadanaṃ siṃhaskandhaṃ mahābhujam  ॥
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharaṃ pīvarorubhujekṣaṇam ॥
[50]        raktotpaladharaṃ tadvat nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
pūjitaṃ devagandharvair apsarogaṇasevitam  ॥
devīpurāṇe |
brahmovāca |
avatārāḥ samāsena kathitā sarvamaṅgalā ॥
pratimālavagandharvair apsarogaṇasevitam ॥
chatracāmaradhāriṇyaḥ striyaḥ pārśve tu kalpayet ॥
siṃhāsanagataṃ vāpi gandharvagaṇasevitam ॥
indrāṇī vāmataś cāsya kuryād utpaladhāriṇīm ॥
bhāskarasya pratimām idānīṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
rathasthaṃ kārayed devaṃ padmahastaṃ sulocanam ॥
saptāśvaṃ caikacakraṃ ca rathaṃ tasya prakalpayet ॥
mukuṭena vicitreṇa padmagarbhasamaprabham ॥
nānābharaṇabhūṣāḍhyaṃ bhujābhyāṃ dhṛtapuṣkaram ॥
skandhasthapuṣkaraṃ caiva līlayaiva dhṛtaṃ sadā ॥
[51]        colakacchannavapuṣaṃ kvacic citreṣu darśayet ॥
vastrayugmasaropetaṃ caraṇau tejasāvṛttau ॥
pratīhārau prakartavyau pārśvayor daṇḍapiṅgalau ॥
kartavyau khaḍgahastau ca pārśvayoḥ puruṣāv ubhau ॥
lekhanīkṛtahastaṃ ca pārśve dhātāram avyayam ॥
nānādevagaṇair yuktam evaṃ kuryād divākaram ॥
anūruṃ sārathiṃ cāsya padminīpatrasaṃnibham ॥
aśvau ca valitagrīvāvabandhau tasya pārśvayoḥ ॥
bhujaṅgarajjubhir baddhāḥ saptāśvā raśmisaṃyutāḥ ॥
padmasthaṃ vā hayasthaṃ vā padmahastaṃ prakalpayet ॥
padmapurāṇe |
ata eva raveḥ pādau na kaścit kārayet kvacit ॥
yaḥ karoti sa pāpiṣṭho gatiṃ gacchati ninditām ॥
kuṣṭharogam avāpnoti loke 'smin duḥkhasaṃjñakam ॥
punar matsyapurāṇe [ 261, 9 taḥ] |
vahnes tu lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye sarvakāmaphalapradam ॥
dīptaṃ suvarṇavapuṣam arddhacandrāsane sthitam ॥
bālārkasadṛśaṃ tasya vadanaṃ cāpi darśayet ॥
yajñopavītinaṃ devaṃ lambakūrcadharaṃ tathā ॥
[52]        kamaṇḍalur vāmakare dakṣiṇe varasūtrakam ॥
jvālāvitānasaṃyuktam ajavāhanam uttamam ॥
kuṇḍasthaṃ vāpi kurvīta mūrdhni saptaśikhānvitam ॥
tathā yamaṃ pravakṣyāmi daṇḍapāśadharaṃ vibhum ॥
mahāmahiṣam ārūḍhaṃ kṛṣṇāñjanacayopamam ॥
siṃhāsanagataṃ vāpi dīptāgnisamalocanam ॥
mahiṣaś citraguptaś ca karālāḥ kiṅkarās tathā ॥
samantād vaśagās tasya saumyāsaumyāḥ surāsurāḥ ॥
rākṣasendraṃ tathā vakṣye lokapālaṃ ca nairṛtam ॥
narārūḍhaṃ mahākāyaṃ rakṣobhir bahubhir vṛtam ॥
khaḍgahastaṃ mahānīlaṃ kajjalācalasaṃnibham ॥
narayuktavimānasthaṃ pītābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
varuṇaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi pāśahastaṃ mahābalam ॥
śaṅkhasphaṭikavarṇābhaṃ śatahārāmbarāvṛtam ॥
[vāyurūpaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhūmraṃ tu mṛgavāhanam  ॥
citrāmbaradharaṃ śāntaṃ yuvānaṃ kuñcitabhruvam ॥]
vṛṣāsanagataṃ śāntaṃ kirīṭavaradhāriṇam ॥
mṛgādirūḍhaṃ varadaṃ patākādhvajasaṃyutam ॥
kuberaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi kuṇḍalābhyām alaṅkṛtam ॥
[53]        hārakeyūraruciraṃ nīlāmbaradharaṃ śubham ॥
gadādharaṃ ca kurvīta varadaṃ mukuṭānvitam ॥
mahodaraṃ mahākāyaṃ nidhyaṣṭakasamanvitam ॥
guhyakair bahubhir yuktaṃ dhanavyagrakarais tathā ॥
tathaiveśaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhavalaṃ dhavalekṣaṇam ॥
triśūlapāṇinaṃ devaṃ tryakṣaṃ vṛṣagataṃ prabhum ॥
mātṝṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ ॥
brahmaṇī brahmasadṛśī caturvaktrā caturbhujā  ॥
haṃsādhirūḍhā kartavyā sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ ॥
maheśvarasya rūpeṇa tathā māheśvarī matā ॥
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃyuktā vṛṣasthā candraśekharā ॥
kapālaśūlakhaṭvāṅgavaradātha caturbhujā ॥
kumārarūpā kaumārī mayūravaravāhanā ॥
raktavastradharās tadvac chūlaśaktidharā matā ॥
hārakeyūrasaṃpannā kṛkavākudharā matā ॥
vaiṣṇavī viṣṇusadṛśī garutmatisamā sthitā ॥
caturbāhuś ca varadā śaṅkhacakragadādharā ॥
siṃhāsanagatā vāpi bālakena samanvitā ॥
vārāhīṃ ca pravakṣyāmi mahiṣopari saṃsthitām ॥
varāhasadṛśīṃ devīṃ ghaṇṭācāmaradhāriṇīm ॥
[54]        indrāṇīm indrasadṛśīṃ vajraśūlagadādharām ॥
gajādhirūḍhāṃ tāṃ devīṃ locanair bahubhir vṛtām ॥
taptakāñcanavarṇābhāṃ vastrābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
tīkṣṇakhaḍgadharāṃ tadvad vakṣye yogeśvarīm imām ॥
dīrghajihvām ūrdhvakeśām asthikhaṇḍaiś ca maṇḍitām ॥
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ kuryāc caiva kṛśodarīm ॥
kapālamālinīṃ devīṃ muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitām ॥
kapālaṃ vāmahaste tu māṃsaśoṇitapūritam ॥
sakeśaṃ ca śiro 'nyasmin śastrikāṃ dakṣiṇe kare ॥
gṛdhrasthā vāhanayutā nirmāṃsā vigatodarī ॥
karālavadanā tadvat karttavyā sā trilocanā ॥
cāmuṇḍā baddhaghaṇṭā ca dvīpicarmadharā śubhā ॥
digvāsāḥ kālikā tadvad rāsabhasthā kapālinī ॥
suraktapuṣpābharaṇā varddhanīdhvajasaṃyutā ॥
vināyakaṃ ca kurvīta mātṝṇāṃ saṃnidhau sadā ॥
vīreśvaraś ca bhagavān vṛṣārūḍho jaṭādharaḥ ॥
vīṇāhastas triśūlī ca mātṝṇām agrato bhavet ॥
śriyaṃ devīṃ pravakṣyāmi nave vayasi saṃsthitām ॥
suyauvanāṃ pīnagaṇḍāṃ raktauṣṭhīṃ kuñcitabhruvam ॥
pīnonnatastanataṭāṃ maṇikuṇḍaladhāriṇīm ॥
[55]        sumaṇḍalaṃ mukhaṃ tasyāḥ śiraḥ sīmantabhūṣitam ॥
padmasvastikaśaṅkhair vā bhūṣitāṃ kuṇḍalālakaiḥ ॥
kañcukābaddhagātrāṃ ca hārabhūṣau payodharau ॥
nāgahastopamau bāhū keyūrakaṭakojvalau ॥
padmaṃ haste pradātavyaṃ śrīphalaṃ dakṣiṇe kare ॥
mekhalābharaṇaṃ tadvat taptakāñcanasaprabhām ॥
nānābharaṇasaṃpannāṃ śobhanāmbaradhāriṇīm ॥
pārśve tasyāḥ striyaḥ kāryāś cāmaravyagrapāṇayaḥ ॥
padmāsanopaviṣṭā tu padmasiṃhāsanasthitā ॥
karibhyāṃ snāpyamānā sā bhṛṅgārābhyām anekaśaḥ ॥
pratipālayantau kariṇau bhṛṅgārābhyāṃ tathāparau ॥
stūyamānā ca lokeśais tathā gandharvakinnaraiḥ ॥
tathaiva yakṣiṇī kāryā siddhāsuraniṣevitā ॥
pārśvayoḥ kalaśas tasyās toraṇe devadānavāḥ ॥
nāgāś caiva tu kartavyāḥ khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇaḥ ॥
adhaḥ sarpākṛtis teṣāṃ nābher ūrdhvaṃ tu pauruṣī ॥
phaṇāś ca mūrdhni kartavyā dvijihvāḥ bahavo samāḥ ॥
piśācā rākṣasāś caiva bhūtavetālajātayaḥ ॥
nirmāṃsāś caiva rudrās te sarve vikṛtarūpiṇaḥ ॥
[56]        [kṣetrapālaś ca karttavyo jaṭilo vikṛtānanaḥ ॥
digvāsā jaṭilas tadvat śvagomāyuniṣevitaḥ ॥
kapālaṃ vāmahaste tu śiraḥ keśasamāvṛtam  ॥
dakṣiṇe śastrikaṃ dadyāt asurakṣayakāriṇīm ॥]
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dvibhujaṃ kusumāyudham ॥
pārśve cāsya mukhaṃ tasya makaradhvajasaṃyutam ॥
dakṣiṇe puṣpabāṇaṃ ca vāme puṣpamayaṃ dhanuḥ ॥
prītiḥ syād dakṣiṇe tasya bhojanopaskarānvitā ॥
ratiś ca vāmapārśve tu śayanopaskarānvitā ॥
paṭaś ca paṭahaś caiva kharaḥ kāmāturas tathā ॥
pārśvato jalavāpī ca vanaṃ nandanam eva ca ॥
suśobhanaś ca kartavyo bhagavān kusumāyudhaḥ ॥
saṃsthānam īṣadvaktraṃ syāt vismayasthitanetrakam ॥
etad uddeśataḥ proktaṃ pratimālakṣaṇaṃ mayā  ॥
vistareṇa na śaknoti bṛhaspatir api dvijāḥ ॥
[sūta uvāca] |
pīṭhikālakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ ॥
pīṭhocchrāyaṃ tathāvac ca bhāgān ṣoḍaśa kārayet ॥
bhūmāv ekaḥ pratiṣṭhaḥ syāc caturbhir jagatī matā ॥
vṛtto bhāgas tathaikaḥ syād vṛtapaṭṭas tribhāgataḥ ॥
bhāgais tribhis tathā kaṇṭhaḥ kaṇṭhapaṭṭas tribhāgataḥ ॥
[57]        bhāgābhyām ūrdhvakaṇṭhaś ca śeṣabhāgena paṭṭikā ॥
praviṣṭaṃ bhāgam ekaikaṃ jagatīṃ yāvad eva tu ॥
nirgamas tu punas tasya yāvad vai śeṣapaṭṭikā ॥
vārinirgamanārthas tu tatra kāryaḥ praṇālakaḥ ॥
piṇḍikānāṃ tu sarvāsām etat sāmānyalakṣaṇam ॥
viśeṣaṃ devatābhedāc chṛṇudhvaṃ dvijasattamāḥ ॥
sthaṇḍilā cātha vāpī ca yakṣī vedī ca maṇḍalā ॥
pūrṇacandrā ca vajrā ca padmā cārddhaśaris tathā ॥
trikoṇā daśamī tāsāṃ saṃsthānaṃ ca nibodhata ॥
sthaṇḍilā caturasrā tu varjitā mekhalādibhiḥ ॥
vāpī dvimekhalā jñeyā yakṣī caiva trimekhalā ॥
caturasrāyatā vedī na tāṃ liṅgeṣu yojayet ॥
maṇḍalā vartulā yā tu mekhalā triguṇā priyā ॥
riktā dvimekhalā madhye pūrṇacandrā prakīrtitā ॥
mekhalātrayasaṃyuktā ṣaḍasrā vajrikā bhavet ॥
ṣoḍaśāsrā bhavet padmā kiñcidbaddhā ca mūlataḥ ॥
tathaiva dhanuṣākārā sārddhacandrā prakīrtitā ॥
triśūlasadṛśī tadvat trikoṇā ūrdhvato matā  ॥
prāgudakapravaṇā tadvat praśastā lakṣaṇānvitā ॥
paribarhatribhāgena khyātaṃ caitat pramāṇataḥ ॥
[58]        vistārataḥ pramāṇaṃ ca mūlaṃ cārddhatadarddhataḥ ॥
jalamārgaś ca kartavyas tribhāgena suśobhanaḥ ॥
liṅgasyārddhatribhāgena sthaulyena samadhiṣṭhitā ॥
mekhalā tu tribhāgena khyātā caitat pramāṇataḥ ॥
atha vā pādahīnaṃ ca śobhanaṃ kārayet sadā ॥
uttarasthaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca pramāṇād adhikaṃ bhavet ॥
sthaṇḍilāyām athārogyaṃ dhanadhānyaṃ ca puṣkalam ॥
gopradā tu bhaved yakṣī vedī saṃpatpradā bhavet ॥
maṇḍalāyāṃ bhavet kīrtir varadā pūrṇacandrikā ॥
āyuṣpradā bhaved vajrā padmā saubhāgyadāyinī ॥
putrapradārddhacandrā syāt trikoṇā śatrunāśinī ॥
devasya yajanārthaṃ tu piṇḍikā daśa kīrtitāḥ ॥
śaile śailamayīṃ dadyāt pārthive pārthivīṃ tathā ॥
dāruje dārujāṃ kuryān miśre miśrāṃ tathaiva ca ॥
nānyayoniś ca kartavyā sadā śubhaphalepsubhiḥ ॥
arcāyām asamaṃ dairghyaṃ liṅgāyām asamaṃ tathā  ॥
yasya devasya yā patnī tāṃ pīṭhe parikalpayet ॥
dairghyaṃ yasya devasya yāvattāṃ parikalpayet ॥
evaṃ sarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ samāsāt pīṭhalakṣaṇam ॥
[59]                [sūta uvāca]
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi liṅgalakṣaṇam uttamam ॥
susnigdhaś ca suvarṇaś ca liṅgaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
prāsādasya pramāṇena liṅgamānaṃ vidhīyate ॥
liṅgamānena vā vidyāt prāsādaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam ॥
caturasre same gartte brahmasūtraṃ nipātayet ॥
vāmena brahmasūtrasya arcāyāṃ liṅgam eva tu ॥
prāguttareṇa nīlaṃ tu dakṣiṇāpadam āśritam ॥
purasyāparadigbhāge pūrvadvāre tu kārayet ॥
pūrveṇa cāparaṃ dvāraṃ māhendraṃ dakṣiṇottare ॥
dvāraṃ vibhajjya pūrvaṃ tu ekaviṃśatibhāgikam ॥
tato madhyagataṃ jñātvā brahmasūtraṃ prakalpayet ॥
tasyārddhaṃ tu tridhā kṛtvā bhāgaṃ cottaratas tyajet ॥
eka (ekaṃ?) dakṣiṇatas tyaktvā brahmasthānaṃ prakalpayet ॥
bhāgārdhena tu yaliṅgaṃ kāryaṃ tad iha śasyate ॥
pañcabhāge vibhakte tu tribhāge jyeṣṭham ucyate ॥
bhājite navadhā garbhe madhyamaḥ pañcabhāgikaḥ ॥
etasminn eva navadhā garbhe liṅgāni kārayet ॥
samasūtraṃ vibhajyātha navadhā garbhabhājitam ॥
jyeṣṭham ardhaṃ kanīyordhaṃ tathā madhyamamadhyamam ॥
[60]        evaṃ garbhaḥ samākhyātas tribhir bhāgair vibhājayet ॥
jyeṣṭhaṃ tu trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ madhyamaṃ trividhaṃ tathā ॥
kanīyas trividhaṃ tadval liṅgabhedā navaiva tu ॥
nābhyarddham aṣṭabhāgena vibhajyātha samaṃ budhāḥ ॥
bhāgatrayaṃ parityajya viṣkambhaṃ caturasrakam ॥
aṣṭāsraṃ madhyamaṃ jñeyaṃ bhāgaṃ liṅgasya vai dhruvam ॥
vikarṇe tu tato gṛhya koṇābhyāṃ lāñchayed budhaḥ ॥
aṣṭāsraṃ kārayet tadvad ūrdhvam apy evam eva tu ॥
ṣoḍaśāsraṃ kṛtaṃ paścād vartulaṃ kārayet tataḥ ॥
āyāmaṃ tasya devasya nābher vai kuṇḍalīkṛtam ॥
māheśvaraṃ tribhāgaṃ tu arddhaṃ vṛttaṃ tu yat sthitam ॥
adhastād brahmabhāge tu caturasro vidhīyate ॥
aṣṭāsro vaiṣṇavo bhāgo madhyasthaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ॥
evaṃ pramāṇayuktaṃ yal liṅgaṃ tadvad idaṃ bhavet ॥
tathānyad api vakṣyāmi garbhamānaṃ pramāṇataḥ ॥
garbhamānapramāṇena yal liṅgam ucitaṃ bhavet ॥
caturddhā tad vibhajyātha viṣkambhaṃ tu prakalpayet ॥
tad evam āyataṃ sūtraṃ bhāgatrayaṃ prakalpayet ॥
adhastāc caturasraṃ tu aṣṭāsraṃ madhyabhāgataḥ ॥
pūrvabhāgas tathārddhe tu nābhibhāgas tatocyate ॥
[61]        āyāme yad bhavet sūtraṃ vāhasya caturasrake ॥
caturasraṃ parityajya aṣṭāsrasya tu tad bhavet ॥
tasyāpy arddhaṃ parityajya tato vṛttaṃ tu kārayet ॥
śiraḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ tasya saṃkṣiptaṃ mūlato bhavet ॥
aṣṭapūtaṃ bhavel liṅgam adhastād vipulaṃ bhavet ॥
śirasaś ca yathā kṣipraṃ manojñaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam ॥
saumyaṃ tu dṛśyate yat tu liṅgaṃ tad buddhidaṃ bhavet ॥
atha mūle ca madhye ca pramāṇaṃ sarvataḥ samam ॥
evaṃvidhaṃ tu yal liṅgaṃ bhavet tat sarvakāmikam ॥
anyathā yad bhavel liṅgaṃ tad aśastaṃ pracakṣate ॥
evaṃ ratnamayaṃ kuryāt sphāṭikaṃ pārthivaṃ tathā ॥
śubhaṃ dārumayaṃ vāpi yad vā manasi rocate ॥
devīpurāṇe |
brahmovāca ॥
śailaṃ brahmā harir nityam indranīlamayaṃ yajet ॥
indro maṇimayaṃ cātha yakṣo haimaṃ samarcayet ॥
viśvedevās tathā raupyaṃ vāyuḥ pittalasambhavam |:
vasavaḥ kāṃsyajaṃ devam aśvinau mṛnmayaṃ punaḥ ॥
sphāṭikaṃ jaladevaś ca vahniratnamayaṃ yajet ॥
tāmraṃ sūryo 'rcayel liṅgaṃ somo mauktikasambhavam ॥
[62]        uragā vaidrumaṃ liṅgam āyasaṃ dānavottamāḥ ॥
piśācās trapujaṃ saisaṃ yoginyo bhūtanāyakāḥ ॥
guhyakās tāmrajaṃ liṅgaṃ vajralohaṃ tu mānavāḥ ॥
padmarāgaṃ samastās tu devadānavamānavāḥ ॥
pūjayanti śubhārthāya ubhayatra śubhāvaham ॥
vaiḍūryaṃ grahanakṣatrāḥ puṣparāgamahāmaṇim ॥
vārkṣaṃ vittapradaṃ liṅgaṃ sphāṭikaṃ sarvakāmadam ॥
narmadāgirijaṃ śailam anyad vāpi hi liṅgavat ॥
kṛtvā pūjaya viprendra lapsyase īpsitaṃ phalam ॥
suyonilakṣaṇopetaṃ kartavyaṃ siddhikāminā ॥
dvijāhṛtaṃ tu yat siddhaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ॥
śailamāṇikyasphaṭikahemarājatatāmrajam ॥
pīṭhāṅgaṃ savidhiḥ siddha yathā yasya tathā kuru ॥
sthitaṃ tu bhavane siddhau caturvṛttaṃ sukhāvaham ॥
atha pratiṣṭhākālaḥ ॥
[tatra] matsyapurāṇe [264, 3 taḥ] |
caitre vā phālgune vāpi jyeṣṭhe vā mādhave tathā  ॥ ॥
māghe vā sarvadevānāṃ pratiṣṭhā śubhadā bhavet ॥
[63]        prāpya pakṣaṃ śubhaṃ śuklam atīte dakṣiṇāyane ॥
pañcamī ca dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā saptamī tathā ॥
daśamī paurṇamāsī ca tathā śreṣṭhā trayodaśī ॥
tāsu pratiṣṭhā vidhivat kṛtā bahuphalā bhavet ॥
āṣāḍhe dve tathā mūlam uttarātrayam eva ca ॥
jyeṣṭhāśravaṇarohiṇyaḥ pūrvabhādrapadā tathā ॥
hastāśvinī revatī ca puṣyo mṛgaśiras tathā ॥
anurādhā tathā svātiḥ pratiṣṭhādiṣu śasyate ॥
budho bṛhaspatiḥ śukras traya ete śubhāvahāḥ ॥
ebhir nirīkṣitaṃ lagnaṃ nakṣatraṃ ca praśasyate ॥
grahatārābalaṃ labdhvā grahapūjāṃ vidhāya ca ॥
nimittaṃ śakunaṃ labdhvā varjayitvādbhutādikam ॥
śubhayoge śubhasthāne krūragrahavivarjite ॥
lagne ṛkṣe ca kurvīta pratiṣṭhādikam uttamam ॥
ayane viṣuve tadvat ṣaḍaśītimukhe tathā ॥
eteṣu sthāpanṃa kāryaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
prājāpatye tu śayanaṃ śvete tūtthāpanaṃ tathā ॥
[64]        muhūrte sthāpanaṃ kuryāt punar brāhme vicakṣaṇāḥ ॥
atha sūryapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ ॥
[tatra] bhaviṣyapurāṇe [134, 1-3] |
pratipac ca dvitīyā ca caturthī pañcamī tathā ॥
daśamī trayodaśī caiva paurṇamāsī tu kīrtitā ॥
somo bṛhaspatiś caiva śukraś caiva budhas tathā ॥
ete saumyagrahāḥ proktā pratiṣṭhāyajñakarmaṇi ॥
triṣūttarāsu revatyām aśvinyāṃ rohiṇīṣu ca ॥
haste punarvasau cāpi puṣyeṇa śravaṇena ca ॥
bharaṇyāṃ caiva nakṣatre bhānoḥ sthāpanam uttamam ॥
phālgune māsi caitre vā jyeṣṭhe vaiśākhayor api ॥
liṅgasthāpanamantrī tu kuryād dhomaṃ jitendriyaḥ ॥
harau supte na kartavyaṃ devatāsthāpanaṃ budhaiḥ ॥
kartavyaṃ tu vibuddhe tu haṃse cāpy uttaraṃ gate  ॥
sarvakāmasamṛddhyarthaṃ sthāpanam uttarāyaṇe ॥
varjayet sarvayatnena mantravid dakṣiṇāyane ॥
śuklapakṣe dvitīyāyāṃ sthāpitaṃ phaladaṃ bhavet ॥
[65]        abhicāraprasiddhyarthaṃ sthāpayed dakṣiṇāyane ॥
candrasūryoparāge ca liṅgaṃ na parikalpayet ॥
sagṛhe sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ bādhyante cāribhiḥ prajāḥ ॥
devīpurāṇe |
siṃhasaṃsthaṃ guruṃ śukraṃ sarvārambheṣu varjayet ॥
ārabdhaṃ na ca sidhyeta mahābhayakaraṃ bhavet ॥
putraṃ vātha kalatrāṇi hanyāc chīghraṃ na saṃśayaḥ ॥
kārako vrajate nāśaṃ santānaḥ kṣīyate 'cirāt ॥
devārāmataḍāgeṣu prapodyānagṛheṣu ca ॥
siṃhasthaṃ makarasthaṃ ca guruṃ yatnena varjayet ॥
tathā |
sarveṣāṃ caiva devānām atīte cottarāyaṇe ॥
sthāpanaṃ śubhadaṃ proktam anyeṣāṃ dakṣiṇāyane ॥
mātṛbhairavavārāhanarasiṃhatrivikramāḥ ॥
mahiṣāsurahantryāś ca pratiṣṭhā dakṣiṇāyane ॥
sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalyā yā devī kathitā tava ॥
caitre tasyāḥ pratiṣṭhā ca pūrvoktavidhinā śubhā ॥
āśvine māsi samprāpte arddharātre sitāṣṭamī ॥
[66]        tatkāle sthāpitā devī pūjitā ca mahābalā ॥
yasya devasya yaḥ kālaḥ pratiṣṭhādhvajarohaṇe ॥
garttāpūraśilānyāsaḥ śubhadas tasya bhāṣitaḥ ॥
tathā daśabhujā devī pratiṣṭhādividhā smṛtā ॥
gurau meṣagate śakra devyarcāṃ yaḥ pratiṣṭhayet ॥
ihaiva sa bhaved dhanyo mṛto gacchet paraṃ padam ॥
tasmān meṣasthite śakra uttama navamī matā ॥
mahānyāścimanā( ? )mānaṃ tat sarvaṃ sarvakāmadam ॥
devī tatra sadā śakra pāṃśubhiḥ sthāpitā api ॥
bhavet tu phaladā pusāṃ [puṃsāṃ?] karkasthe ca vṛṣadhvaje ॥
viśeṣaś cātra kathitaḥ sarvakāle 'pi maṅgalā ॥
yena kenāpi vittena sarvakāmaphalapradā ॥
atha sthāpakāḥ ॥ 7 ॥
[tatra] devīpurāṇe |
brahmovāca |
śivasya śivasiddhānte śaive kāryā niveśanā ॥
gṛhī vā brahmacārī vā muktihetoḥ pratiṣṭhakaḥ ॥
vāmadakṣiṇavettā yo mātṛvedārthapāragaḥ ॥
sa bhavej jāpakaḥ śreṣṭho devīnāṃ mātur eva ca ॥
pañcarātrārthakuśalo mātṛtattvaviśāradaḥ ॥
viṣṇor grahī mantravettā brahmacārī ca sāgnikaḥ ॥
[67]        śivaśāsanavettā yo gṛhamātṛgaṇārthavit ॥
saurārthavedakaḥ sūryasthāpakas tu śubhapradaḥ ॥
vedavedārthatattvajñā brāhmaṇā ṛtvijo dvijāḥ ॥
anyeṣu ca svaśāstrajñāḥ sthāpakāḥ pūjakāḥ śubhāḥ ॥
śaivāḥ sarveṣu kurvanti ye gṛhasthā dvijottamāḥ ॥
sarvasthāpanamantrārthavedakāś cādhikāriṇaḥ ॥
garuvetālatantre ca bhūtatantreṣu dakṣiṇe ॥
kālike vāmatantre ca vettā cāryaśubhāvahaḥ ॥
vityeśagaṇalokaiśabhūtatantrārthavic chive ॥
prādurbhāvakriyākārabhedasaṃskāra............ ॥
devīnāṃ sthāpanaṃ rūpaṃ buddhvā kāryaniveśanam ॥
viṣṇor dvādaśabhedāḥ syur māgādi vihitasya ca ॥
dvāḥsthaḥ kāryakriyāṃ buddhvā sthāpanā sā bhavec chubhā ॥
anyathā kalahodvegaṃ kuryān mṛtyuṃ mahābhayam ॥
yajamāne nṛpe rāṣṭre sthāpake vai nirāgame ॥
avicyādiṣu pacyante viparītā niveśakāḥ ॥
viṣṇoḥ śivasya ye brahman sthāpakā vidhivarjitāḥ ॥
pravarādiraver buddhvā kāyānun .... śubhāvahā ॥
bhayadā jāyate śakra sāmānyathā vāpi kalpanā ॥
prāyaśo matabhedena bhinnā pūjā niveśanā ॥
buddhvā kāryā suraśreṣṭha sarvakāmaphalapradā ॥
[68]
athādhikāriṇaḥ ॥ 8 ॥
[tatra] devīpurāṇe |
varṇāśramavibhedena devāḥ sthāpyā tu nānyathā ॥
brahmā ca brāhmaṇaiḥ sthāpyo gāyatrīsahitaḥ śubhaḥ ॥
caturvarṇaiḥ sthito viṣṇuḥ pratiṣṭhāpyaḥ sukhārthibhiḥ ॥
bhairavas tu caturvarṇair antyajānāṃ tathā mataḥ ॥
mātaraḥ sarvalokās tu sthāpayanti surottamāḥ ॥
liṅgaṃ gṛhī yatī vāpi saṃsthāpya pūjayet sadā ॥
āyūr rājyaṃ tathā putraṃ dadāti sthāpito haraḥ ॥
kiṃ tu yogārthibhiḥ sthāpyo yogeśo yogasaṃyutaḥ ॥
anyathā kalahodvegaḥ pure saṃjāyate bhṛśam ॥
atha sādhāraṇapratiṣṭhā' ॥ 9 ॥
[tatra] matsyapurāṇe |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi pratiṣṭhāvidhim uttamam ॥
kuṇḍamaṇḍapavedīnāṃ pramāṇaṃ ca yathākramam ॥
prāsādasyottare cāpi pūrve vā maṇḍapo bhavet ॥
hastān ṣoḍaśa kurvīta daśa dvādaśa vā punaḥ ॥
madhye vedikayā yuktaḥ parikṣiptaḥ samantataḥ ॥
parikṣiptaḥ paritaḥ ॥
pañcasaptacatur vāpi karān kurvīta vedikām ॥
caturbhis toraṇair yukto maṇḍapaḥ syāc caturmukhaḥ ॥
[69]        plakṣadvāraṃ bhavet pūrve yāmye caudumbaraṃ bhavet ॥
paścād aśvatthaghaṭitaṃ naiyagrodhaṃ tathottare ॥
bhūmau hastapraviṣṭāni caturhastāni cocchraye ॥
sūpaliptaṃ tathā lakṣṇaṃ bhūtalaṃ syāt suśobhanam ॥
vastrair nānāvidhais tadvat puṣpapallavaśobhitam ॥
kṛtvaivaṃ maṇḍapaṃ pūrvaṃ caturdvāreṣu vinyaset ॥
avraṇān kalaśān aṣṭau jvalatkāñcanagarbhitām ॥
cūtapallavasaṃyuktān sitavastrayugānvitān ॥
srakcandanaphalopetān candanodakapūjitān ॥
evaṃ niveśya tān garbhe gandhadhūpārcanādibhiḥ ॥
dhvajādhiropaṇaṃ kāryaṃ maṇḍapasya samantataḥ ॥
dhvajāś ca lokapālānāṃ sarvadikṣu niveśayet ॥
patākā jaladākārā madhye syān maṇḍapasya tu ॥
gandhadhūpādikaṃ kuryāt svaiḥ svair mantrair anukramāt ॥
baliṃ ca lokapālebhyaḥ svamatreṇa nivedayet ॥
ūrdhvaṃ tu brāhmaṇe deya adhastāc cheṣavāsukeḥ ॥
saṃhitāyāṃ tu ye mantras taddaivatyāḥ śrutau smṛtāḥ ॥
taiḥ pūjā lokapālānāṃ kartavyā tu samantataḥ ॥
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā pañcarātram athāpi vā ॥
atha vā saptarātraṃ tu kāryaṃ syād adhivāsanam ॥
[70]        evaṃ satoraṇaṃ kṛtvā adhivāsanamaṇḍapam ॥
tasyāpy uttarataḥ kuryāt snānamaṇḍapam uttamam ॥
tadarddhena tribhāgena caturbhāgena vā punaḥ ॥
ānīya liṅgam arcāṃ vā śilpinaḥ pūjayed budhaḥ ॥
vastrābharaṇaratnais tu ye ca tatparicārakāḥ ॥
kṣamadhvam iti tān brūyād yajamānas tataḥ param ॥
devaṃ prastaraṇe kṛtvā netrajyotiḥ prakalpayet ॥
lakṣaṇor uddharaṇaṃ vakṣye liṅgasyāpi samāsataḥ ॥
sarvatas tu baliṃ datvā siddhārthaghṛtapāyasaiḥ ॥
śuklapuṣpair alaṅkṛtya ghṛtagugguladhūpitam ॥
vipravācanakaṃ kuryād datvā śaktayā ca dakṣiṇām ॥
gām ekāṃ kanakaṃ tadvat sthāpakāya nivedayet ॥
lakṣaṇaṃ kārayed bhaktyā mantreṇānena vai dvijāḥ ॥
oṃ namo bhagavate tubhyaṃ, śivāya haraye namaḥ ॥
hiraṇyaretase viṣṇo viśvarūpāya te namaḥ ॥
mantro 'yaṃ sarvadevānāṃ netrajyotiṣv api smṛtaḥ ॥
evam āmantraya deveśaṃ kāñcanena vilekhayet ॥
maṅgalāni ca vādyāni brahmaghoṣaṃ ca gītakam ॥
[71]        vṛdhyarthaṃ kārayed vidvān maṅgalasya nibarhaṇam ॥
lakṣaṇoddharaṇaṃ kuryāl liṅgasya tu samāhitaḥ ॥
tridhā vibhajya pūjyāyāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ syād vibhāgikam ॥
rudrabhāgaḥ ॥
rekhātrayaṃ tu kartavyaṃ yavādyantarasaṃmitam ॥
na sthūlaṃ na kṛśaṃ tadvad avakraṃ chedavarjitam ॥
nimnaṃ yavaḥpramāṇena śreṣṭhaliṅgasya kārayet ॥
ślakṣṇāḥ tatas tu kartavyā saptamadhyamakaṃ nyaset ॥
aṣṭabhāgaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tyaktvā bhāgatrayaṃ budhaḥ ॥
lambayen maṇirekhās tu pārśvayor ubhayoḥ samāḥ ॥
tāvat pralambayed vidvān yāvad bhāgacatuṣṭayam ॥
[bhrāmyete pañcabhāgordhvaṃ kārayet saṅgamaṃ tataḥ ] ॥
rekhayoḥ saṅgame tadvad dvau bhāgau plaṣṭato bhavet ॥
evam etat samākhyātaṃ samāsāl lakṣaṇaṃ mayā ॥
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mūrtipānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam ॥
sthāpakasya samāsena lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ ॥
sarvāvayavasampūrṇo vedamantraviśāradaḥ ॥
[72]        purāṇavettā tattvajño dambhalobhavivarjitaḥ ॥
kṛṣṇasāraś caradeśe ya utpannaḥ śubhākṛtiḥ ॥
śaucācāraparo nityaṃ pākhaṇḍakulaniḥspṛhaḥ ॥
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca brahmopendraharapriyaḥ ॥
ūhāpohārthatattvajño vāstuśāstrasya pāragaḥ ॥
ācāryas tu bhaven nityaṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitaḥ ॥
mūrtipās tadvidyāś caiva kulīnā ṛjavas tathā ॥
dvātriṃśat ṣoḍaśāvāpi aṣṭau vā śrutipāragāḥ ॥
jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhās tu mūrtipāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ॥
tato liṅgam athārcāṃ vā nītvā snapanamaṇḍapam ॥
gītamaṅgalaśabdena snapanaṃ tatra kārayet ॥
pañcagavyakaṣāyena mṛdbhir bhasmodakena ca ॥
śaucaṃ tatra prakurvīta vedamantracatuṣṭayāt ॥
samudrajyeṣṭhamantraś ca āpodevīti cāparaḥ ॥
yāsāṃ rājetimantraś ca āpohiṣṭheti cāparaḥ ॥
[yāsāṃ rājeti yāsāṃ rājā varuṇo yāti madhya iti maṃtraḥ] ॥
evaṃ snāpya tato devaṃ pūjya gandhānulepanaiḥ ॥
pracchādya vastrayugmena abhivastrety udāharan ॥
utthāpayet tato devam uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate ॥
āmūrajeti ca tathā rathe tiṣṭheti cāparaḥ ॥
[73] abhivastrā suvasanānītyādi ॥ uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate devayanta ityanena ॥ tathā āmurajaḥ pratyāvarttayetyanena mantreṇa sthāpanaṃ kuryāt ॥ rathe tiṣṭhan nayati vājina iti ॥
rathe brahmarathe vāpi dhṛtaṃ śilpigaṇena ca ॥
āropya cānayed vidvān ākṛṣṇena praveśayet ॥
ākṛṣṇena rajasetyanena mantreṇa praveśanaṃ kuryāt ॥
tataḥ prastīrya śayyāyāṃ svāpayec chanakair bhuvi ॥
kuśān āstīrya puṣpāṇi svāpayet prāṅmukhaṃ tataḥ ॥
tatas tu nidrākalaśaṃ vastrakāñcanasaṃyutam ॥
śirobhāge tu devasya japann eva nidhāpayet ॥
āpo devīti mantreṇa āposmānmāntareti ca  ॥ ॥
tato dukūlapaṭṭaiś cācchādya netropadhānakam ॥
dadyāc chirasi devasya kauśeyaṃ vā vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
madhunā sarpiṣābhyajya tilasiddhārthakais tataḥ ॥
āpyāyasveti mantreṇa yā te rudra śiveti ca ॥
upaviśyārcayed devaṃ gandhapuṣpaiḥ samantataḥ ॥
sitaṃ pratisaraṃ dadyāt bārhaspatyeti mantrataḥ ॥
pratisaraṃ hastasūtram ॥
dukūlapaṭṭaiḥ kārpāsair nānācitrair athāpi vā ॥
ācchādya devaṃ sarvatra chatracāmaradarpaṇam ॥
pārśve tu sthāpayet tatra vimānaṃ puṣpasaṃyutam ॥
[74]        ratnāny oṣadhayas tadvad gṛhopakaraṇāni ca ॥
bhājanāni vicitrāṇi śayanāny āsanāni ca ॥
abhi tvā śūra mantreṇa yathā vibhavato nyaset ॥
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ tadvad bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca pāyasam ॥
ṣaḍvidhaiḥ svarasais tadvat samantāt paripūjayet ॥
baliṃ dadyāt prayatnena mantreṇānena bhūriśaḥ ॥
tryambakaṃ yajāmaheti sarvataḥ śanakair bhuvi ॥
mūrttipān sthāpayet paścāt sarvadikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
caturo dvārapālāṃś ca dvāreṣu viniveśayet ॥
śrīsūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ tu somasūktaṃ sumaṅgalam ॥
tathā ca śāntikādhyāyam indrasūktaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
rakṣoghnaṃ ca tathā sūktaṃ pūrvato bahuvṛcaḥ paṭhet ॥
'śrīsūktaṃ' hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīmiti ॥ 'pāvamānaṃ' svādiṣṭayā madiṣṭayetyādi  ॥
‘somasūktam' tvaṃ soma mahetyādi ॥ 'sumaṅgalaṃ' kanikrada[jja]nuṣam ityādi ॥ 'śāṃtikādhyāyaḥ’ śan na indrāgnī ityādi ॥ indrasūktaṃ yo jāta evetyādi ॥ 'rakṣoghnaṃ sūktaṃ' kṛṣṇuṣvapāja ityādi ॥
rudrān puruṣasūktaṃ ca ślokādhyāyaṃ ca śukriyam  ॥
tathaiva maṇḍalādhyāyam adhvaryur dakṣiṇe japet ॥
‘rudrān' namas te rudra manyava ityādimantrān ॥ 'puruṣasūktaṃ ' sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa’ ityādi ॥ ‘ślokādhyāyaḥ’ deva savitaḥ prasuva yajñam ityādi ॥ ‘śukriyaṃ’ ṛcaṃ vācaṃ prapadya ityādi ॥ 'maṇḍalādhyāyaḥ’ ādityo vā etan maṇḍalam ityādi ॥ ‘taittirīyakam’ ityādi ॥
[75]        vāmadevyaṃ bṛhatsāma jyeṣṭhasāma rathantaram ॥
tathā puruṣasūktaṃ tu rudrasūktaṃ saśāntidam ॥
bhāruṇḍāni tu sāmāni chandogaḥ paścime japet ॥
vāmadevyaṃ sāmakayāta ity asyāṃ ṛtviggītam ॥ ‘bṛhatsāma ' yaṃ tvām indra havāmaha ity asyām ṛci [tvig] gītam ॥ ‘jyeṣṭhasāma' mūrddhānaṃ diva ity asyāṃ gītam ॥ puruṣasūktaṃ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa ity etāsu gītam ॥ ‘rudrasūktaṃ ' rudrasaṃhitāyām 'āvo rājā tad vo varga ājya dohāni devavratānīty etāni ॥ ‘śāntikādhyāyaḥ ' sāmabhiḥ proktaḥ ॥ ‘bhāruṇḍāni sāmāni’ imaṃ stomam arhata ityādīni ॥
atharvāṅgirasaṃ tadvan nīlaṃ raudraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
tathāparājitā devī nīlasūktaṃ saraudrakam ॥
tathaiva śāntikādhyāyam atharvā cottare japet ॥
śiraḥsthāne tu devasya sthāpako homam ācaret ॥
śāntikaiḥ pauṣṭikais tadvan mantrair vyāhṛtipūrvakaiḥ ॥
‘ātharvaṇaṃ sūktaṃ’ doṣo gāyatra rudrāyetyādi | ‘āṅgirasaṃ’ aṅgiraso janmānītyādi | ‘nīlarudraṃ’ yām udbhava ityādi | ‘aparājitā devī’ parivartmānītyādi | 'madhusūktaṃ' madhuvātā ṛtāyata ityādi ॥ 'rudrasūktaṃ ' bhavāṃ śayau mṛḍataṃ mābhiyātam ityādi ṛk  ॥ 'śāntikādhyāyaḥ' śan na indrāgnītyādi ॥ 'śāntikaiḥ' śan na indrāgnī bhavatām avobhir ity etais tribhiḥ  ॥ 'pauṣṭikaiḥ' puṣṭiṃ nara evākṣitinaṣṭadhvī (puṣṭir naraṇvā kṣitir na pṛthvī) ॥ 'gayasphānomīvahā' ॥ 'tryambakaṃ yajāmaha' ity etais tribhiḥ ॥
[76]        palāśodumbarāśvatthā apāmārga: śamī tathā ॥
hutvā sahasram ekaikaṃ devaṃ pāde tu saṃspṛśet ॥
tato homasahasreṇa hutvā hutvā tatas tataḥ ॥
nābhimadhyaṃ tathā vakṣaḥ śiraś copalabhet punaḥ ॥
hastamātreṣu kuṇḍeṣu mūrttipāḥ sarvato diśam ॥
samekhaleṣu kartavyaṃ yonivaktreṣu cādarāt ॥
vitastimātrā yoniḥ syād gajoṣṭrasadṛśī tathā ॥
āyatā chidrasaṃyuktā pārśvataḥ kalayocchritā ॥
kuṇḍakaṇṭhānusāreṇa sarvataś caturaṅgulā ॥
vistāreṇocchritā tadvac caturasrā samā bhavet ॥
vedībhittiṃ parityajya trayodaśabhir aṅgulaiḥ  ॥ ॥
evaṃ navasu kuṇḍeṣu lakṣaṇaṃ ca tad iṣyate ॥
āgneyaśākrayāmyeṣu hotavyam udagānanaiḥ ॥
mūrtipair lokapālebhyo mūrtibhyaḥ kramaśas tathā ॥
tathā mūrtyadhidevānāṃ kramāt kuryuḥ samantataḥ ॥
vasudhā vasuretāś ca yajamāno divākaraḥ ॥
jalaṃ vāyus tathā soma ākāśaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ smṛtam ॥
devasya mūrtayaś cāṣṭāv etāḥ kuṇḍeṣu saṃsmaret ॥
etāsām adhipān vakṣye ye viprā mūrtipā matāḥ ॥
pṛthivīṃ pāti śarvas tu paśupaś cāgnim eva ca ॥
yajamānaṃ tathaivogro rudraś cādityam eva ca ॥
bhavo jalaṃ sadā pāti vāyum īśāna eva ca ॥
[77]        mahādevas tathā candro bhīmaś cākāśam eva tu ॥
sarvadevapratiṣṭhāyāṃ mūrttipā hy eta eva tu ॥
etebhyo vaidikair mantrair yathāsvaṃ homam ācaret ॥
tathā śāntighaṭaṃ kuryāt pratikuṇḍeṣu sarvataḥ ॥
śatānte vā sahasrānte supūrṇāhutir iṣyate ॥
samapādas tato ‘rddhas tu praśāntātmā vinikṣipet ॥
āhutīnāṃ tu saṃpātaṃ pūrṇakuṇḍeṣu vinyaset ॥
mūlamadhyottamāṅgeṣu devaṃ tenāvasecayet ॥
sthitaṃ ca snāpayet tena saṃpātāhutivāriṇā ॥
pratiyāmeṣu dhūpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ candanodakam ॥
punaḥ punaḥ prakurvīta homaḥ kāryaḥ punaḥ punaḥ ॥
punaḥ punaśca dātavyā yajamānena dakṣiṇā ॥
navavastrais tu te sarve pūjanīyā samantataḥ ॥
vicitrahemakaṭakair hemasūtrāṅgulīyakaiḥ ॥
vāsobhiḥ śayanīyaiś ca pratiyāme ca śaktitaḥ ॥
bhojanaṃ cāpi dātavyaṃ yāvat tasyādhivāsanam ॥
balis trisandhyaṃ deyas tu bhūtebhyaḥ sarvatodiśam ॥
brāhmaṇān pūjayet pūrvaṃ śeṣān varṇāṃs tu kāmataḥ ॥
rātrau mahotsavaḥ kāryo nṛtyagītakamaṅgalaiḥ ॥
sadā pūjyāḥ prayatnena caturthīkarmayāvatā ॥
[78]        trirātram ekarātraṃ vā pañcarātram athāpi vā ॥
saptarātram atho kuryāt kvacit sadyo'dhivāsanam ॥
sarvayajñaphalo yasmād adhivāsotsavas tathā  ॥
sūta uvāca [a. 266, 1 taḥ] |
kṛtvādhivāsaṃ devānāṃ śvabhraṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ ॥
prāsādasyānurūpeṇa mānaṃ liṅgasya vā punaḥ ॥
puṣpodakena prāsādaṃ prokṣya mantrayutena tu ॥
pātayet pakṣasūtraṃ tu dvārasūtraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
āśrayet kiñcid īśānīṃ madhyaṃ jñātvā diśaṃ budhaḥ ॥
īśānīm āśritaṃ devaṃ pūjayanti divaukasaḥ ॥
āyurārogyaphaladaṃ tathaivottaram āśritam ॥
śubhaṃ syād aśubhaṃ proktam anyathā sthāpanaṃ budhaiḥ ॥
adhaḥ kūrmaśilā proktā sadā brahmaśilādhikā ॥
uparyavasthitā tasmād brahmabhāgādhikā śilā ॥
tatas tu piṇḍikā kāryā pūrvoktair mānalakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
tataḥ prakṣālitaṃ kṛtvā pañcagavyena piṇḍikām ॥
kaṣāyatoyena punar mantrayuktena sarvataḥ ॥
devatāś cāśrayaṃ mantraṃ piṇḍikāsu niyojayet ॥
tata utthāya deveśam uttiṣṭha brahmaṇeti ca ॥
ānīya garbhabhavanaṃ pīṭhānte sthāpayed budhaḥ ॥
arghyapādyādikaṃ tatra madhuparkaṃ prayojayet ॥
tato muhūrtaṃ viśramya ratnanyāsaṃ samācaret ॥
[79]        vajramauktikavaidūryaśaṅkhasphāṭikam eva ca ॥
puṣparāgendranīlaś ca nīlaṃ pūrvāditaḥ kramāt ॥
tālakaṃ ca śilāvajram añjanaṃ śyāmam eva ca ॥
kāṅkṣīkāsīramākṣikaṃ gairikaṃ cāditaḥ kramāt ॥
'tālakaṃ' haritālam ॥ 'śilā' manaḥśilā ॥ ‘vajraṃ' candrakāntaḥ ॥ ' śyāmāñjanaṃ' sauvīrāñjanam ॥ 'kāṃkṣī’ tuvārakā ॥ 'mākṣikaṃ' atra suvarṇamākṣikaṃ ॥ ‘gairikaṃ' cāditaḥ ॥
godhūmaṃ ca yavaṃ tadvat tilaṃ mudgaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
nīvāram atha śyāmākaṃ sarṣapaṃ vrīhim eva ca ॥
nyaset krameṇa pūrvādi candanaṃ raktacandanam ॥
aguruṃ vījanaṃ caiva uśīraṃ ca tataḥ param ॥
vaiṣṇavīṃ sahadevīṃ ca lakṣmaṇāṃ ca tataḥ param ॥
svarlokapālanāmnā tu nyased oṃkārapūrvakam s
sarvabījāni dhātūṃś ca ratnāny oṣadhayas tathā ॥
kāñcanaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca pāradaṃ padmam eva ca ॥
kūrmaṃ dharāṃ vṛṣaṃ tatra nyaset pūrvāditaḥ kramāt ॥
brahmasthāne tu dātavyāḥ saṃhatāḥ syuḥ parasparam ॥
pulakaṃ vidrumaṃ tāmraṃ kāṃsyaṃ caivārakūṭajam ॥
rajataṃ vimalaṃ puṣpaṃ lohaṃ cānukrameṇa ca ॥
‘pulakaṃ ' romakam ayaskāntaviśeṣaḥ  ॥ vimari trividhaṃ ravimaṃḍalam  ॥ 'kāṃsyaṃ' vimalam iti prasiddham  ॥ 'puṣpam’ iti lohaviśeṣo yatra paṅkajākārā bindavo bhavantīti  ॥
[80]        kāñcanaṃ haritālaṃ ca sarvābhāve 'pi nikṣipet ॥
dadyād bījauṣadhisthāne sahadevīṃ yavān api ॥
nyāsamantraṃs tathā vakṣye lokapālātmakān iha ॥
indras tu sahasā dīptaḥ sarvadevādhipo mahān ॥
vajrahasto mahāsattvas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
āgneyaḥ puruṣo raktaḥ sarvadevamayo 'vyayaḥ ॥
dhūmaketur anādhṛṣyas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
yamaś cotpalavarṇābhaḥ kirīṭī daṇḍadharas tathā ॥
dharmasākṣī viśuddhātmā tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
nairṛtas tu pumān kṛṣṇaḥ sarvarakṣo ‘dhipo mahān ॥
khaḍgahasto mahāsattvas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
varuṇaḥ śābalo viṣṇuḥ paruṣo nimnagādhipaḥ ॥
pāśahasto mahābāhus tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
vāyuś ca sarvavarṇo 'yaṃ sarvagandhavahaḥ śubhaḥ ॥
puruṣo dhvajahastaś ca tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
gauro yas tu pumān saumyaḥ sarvauṣadhisamanvitaḥ ॥
nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ  ॥
īśānapuruṣaḥ śuklaḥ sarvavidyādhipo mahān ॥
śūlahasto virūpākṣas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
padmayoniś caturmūrtir vedavāsāḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
yajñādhyakṣaś caturvakras tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
yo 'sāv anantarūpeṇa brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaram ॥
puṣpavad dhārayen mūrdhni tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
[81]        oṃkārapūrvakā hy ete nyāse balinivedane ॥
mantrāḥ syuḥ sarvakāryāṇāṃ vṛddhiputraphalapradāḥ ॥
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu mantrāṇāṃ pāyasenānulepanam ॥
paṭenācchādayec chubhraṃ śuktenopari yatnataḥ ॥
tata utthāpya deveśam iṣṭāṃśe tu suśobhane ॥
dhruvā dyaur iti mantreṇa śvabhropari niveśayet ॥
tataḥ sthirīkṛtasyāsya hastaṃ datvā tu mastake ॥
dhyātvā paramasadbhāvād devadevaṃ tu niṣkalam ॥
devavrataṃ tathā somaṃ rudrasūktaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
ātmānam īśvaraṃ kṛtvā nānābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
yasya devasya yad rūpaṃ tad dhyāne saṃsmaret tadā ॥
atasīpuṣpasaṃkāśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam ॥
saṃsthāpayāmi deveśaṃ devo bhūtvā janārdanam ॥
tryambakaṃ daśabāhuṃ ca candrārddhakṛtaśekharam ॥
gaṇeśaṃ vṛṣasaṃsthaṃ tu sthāpayāmi trilocanam ॥
ṛṣibhiḥ saṃstutaṃ devaṃ caturvaktraṃ jaṭādharam ॥
pitāmahaṃ mahābāhuṃ sthāpayāmy ambujodbhavam ॥
sahasrakiraṇaṃ bhānum apsarogaṇasevitam ॥
padmahastaṃ mahābāhuṃ sthāpayāmi divākaram ॥
vedamantrāṃs tathā raudrān rudrasya sthāpane japet ॥
viṣṇos tu vaiṣṇavāṃs tadvad brāhmān vai brahmaṇo budhaḥ ॥
saurāḥ sūryasya japtavyās tathānyeṣu tadāśrayāḥ ॥
[82]        vedamantrapratiṣṭhā tu yasmād ānandadāyinī ॥
sthāpayed yaṃ tu deveśaṃ pradhānaṃ taṃ prakalpayet ॥
tasya pārśvasthitān anyān smaret tatparicāriṇaḥ ॥
gaṇaṃ nandimahākāyaṃ vṛṣaṃ bhṛṅgiriṭiṃ guham ॥
devīṃ vināyakaṃ caiva viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇam eva ca ॥
balāhakaṃ caiva viṣṇuṃ tathā brahmāṇam eva ca ॥
rudraṃ śakraṃ jayantaṃ ca lokapālān samantataḥ ॥
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā gandharvagaṇaguhyakān ॥
yo yatra sthāpito devas tasya tān paritaḥ smaret ॥
āvāhayet tathā rudraṃ mantreṇānena yatnataḥ ॥
yasya siṃhā rathe yuktā vyāghrabhūtās tathoragāḥ ॥
ṛṣayo lokapālāś ca devaḥ skandas tathā vṛṣaḥ ॥
priyo gaṇo mātaraś ca somo viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
nāgo yakṣaś ca gandharvo ye ca divyā nabhaścarāḥ ॥
tam ahaṃ tryakṣam īśānaṃ śivaṃ rudram umāpatim ॥
āvāhayāmi sagaṇaṃ sapatnīkaṃ vṛṣadhvajam ॥
oṃ āgaccha, bhagavann anugrahāya śivo bhava  ॥ śāśvato bhava pūjāṃ pratigṛhāṇa ॥ oṃ namaḥ svāgatam anusvāgataṃ bhavataḥ  ॥ namaḥ somāya sagaṇāya  ॥ paricāraṃ pratigṛhṇātu bhavan mantrapūrvam idam arghaṃ pādyam ācamanīyam āsanaṃ brahmaṇābhihitam  ॥ namo namaḥ svāhā ॥
[83]        tataḥ puṇyāhaghoṣeṇa brahmaghoṣaiḥ sapuṣkalaiḥ  ॥
snāpayīta tato devaṃ dadhikṣīraghṛtena ca ॥
madhuśarkarayā tadvat puṣpagandhodakena tu ॥
śivadhyānaikacittas tu mantrān etān udīrayet ॥
‘yaj jāgrato dūram udeti tato virāḍ ajāyata’ iti  ॥ ‘sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa’ iti ca ॥ 'abhitvā śūra nonuma' iti  ॥ 'puruṣa evedaṃ sarvam’ iti ॥ 'tripād ūrdhvam’ iti ॥ 'yenedaṃ bhūtam’ iti ॥ natvā vāṃ anya iti ॥
Sarvān etān pratiṣṭhāsu japtvā mantrān punaḥ punaḥ  ॥
catuṣ kṛtvā spṛśed devaṃ mūle madhye śirasy api ॥
sthāpite tu tato deve yajamānaḥ svamūrttipam ॥
ācāryaṃ pūjayed bhaktyā vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ ॥
dīnāndhakṛpaṇāṃs tadvad ye cānye samupasthitāḥ ॥
tatas tu madhunā devaṃ prathame 'hani lepayet ॥
haridrayātha siddhārthai [?] dvitīye 'hani tattvataḥ ॥
candanena yavais tadvat tṛtīye 'hani lepayet ॥
manaḥśilāpriyaṅgubhyāṃ caturthe 'hani lepayet ॥
saubhāgyaśubhadaṃ yasmāl lepanaṃ vyādhināśanam ॥
paraṃ prītikaraṃ nṝṇām etad vedavido viduḥ ॥
kṛṣṇāñjanaṃ tilaṃ tadvat pañcame 'hani lepayet ॥
ṣaṣṭhe tu saghṛtaṃ dadyāc candanaṃ padmakesaram ॥
rocanaṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca saptame 'hani dāpayet ॥
[84]        yatra sadyo 'dhivāsaḥ syāt tatra sarvaṃ nivedayet ॥
sthitaṃ na cālayed devam anyathā doṣabhāg bhavet ॥
pūrayet sikatābhis tu niśchidraṃ sarvato bhavet ॥
lokapālasya digbhāge yasya saṃcalate vibhuḥ ॥
tasya lokapateḥ śāntir deyāś cemāś ca dakṣiṇāḥ ॥
indrāya vāraṇaṃ dadyāt kāñcanaṃ cālpavittavān ॥
agneḥ suvarṇam eva syād yamasya mahiṣaṃ tathā ॥
ajaṃ ca kāñcanaṃ dadyād gurave rākṣasaṃ prati ॥
varuṇaṃ prati muktāni suślakṣṇāni pradāpayet ॥
rītikāṃ vāyave dadyād vastrayugmena saṃyutām ॥
somāya dhenur dātavyā rajataṃ savṛṣe śive ॥
yasya yasyāṃ saṃghalate śāntiḥ syāt tatra tatra tu ॥
anyathā tu bhaved dvāraṃ bhayaṃ kulavināśanam ॥
acalaṃ kārayet tasmāt sikatābhiḥ sureśvaram ॥
annaṃ vastraṃ ca dātavyaṃ puṇyāhajayamaṅgalam ॥
tripañcadaśasaptaiva dināni syān mahotsavaḥ ॥
caturthe 'hni mahāsnānaṃ caturthīkarma kārayet ॥
dakṣiṇā ca punas tadvad deyā tasyāpi yatnataḥ ॥
devapratiṣṭhāvidhir eṣa tubhyaṃ
niveditaḥ pāpavināśahetoḥ ॥
yasmād budhaiḥ pūrvam anantamuktam
anekavidyādharadevajuṣṭam ॥
[85]                sūta uvāca [ 267, 1 taḥ]
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devasnapanam uttamam ॥
ardhasyāpi samāsena śṛṇudhvaṃ vidhim uttamam ॥
dadhyakṣatakuśāgrāṇi kṣīraṃ dūrvās tathā madhu ॥
yavāḥ siddhārthakās tadvad aṣṭāṅgo ‘rghaḥ phalaiḥ saha ॥
gajāśvarathyāvalmīkavarāhotkhātamaṇḍalāt ॥
agnyāgārāt tathā tīrthād hradād [?] gomaṇḍalād api ॥
kumbhe tu mṛttikāṃ dadyād uddhṛtāsīti mantravit ॥
śanno devī hy apāṃ mantra āpo hiṣṭheti yā ṛcaḥ ॥
sāvitryādāya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam ॥
āpyāyasveti gokṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ॥
tejo 'sīti ghṛtaṃ tadvad devasya tveti codakam ॥
kuśamiśraṃ kṣiped vidvān pañcagavyaṃ samantataḥ ॥
snāpyātha pañcagavyena dadhnā śuddhena vai tataḥ ॥
dadhikrāvṇeti mantreṇa kartavyam abhimantraṇam ॥
āpyāyasveti payasā tejo 'sīti ghṛtena ca ॥
madhuvāteti madhunā tataḥ puṣpodakena ca ॥
sarasvatyai bhaiṣajyena kāryaṃ tasyābhimantraṇam ॥
hiraṇyākṣeti mantreṇa snāpayed ratnavāriṇā ॥
kuśāmbhasā tataḥ snānaṃ devasya tveti kārayet ॥
[ phalodakena ca snānam agna āyāhi kārayet ॥ ]
tataḥ sugandhatoyena sāvitryā cābhimantrayet ॥
tato ghaṭasahasreṇa sahasrārddhena vā punaḥ ॥
[86]        tasyāpy arddhena vā kuryād atha vāṣṭaśatena ca ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭyardhabhāgena tadarddhārddhana vā punaḥ ॥
caturbhir atha vā kuryād ghaṭānām alpavittavān ॥
sauvarṇai rājatair vāpi tāmrair vārītikodbhavaiḥ ॥
kāṃsyair vā pārthivair vāpi snāpanaṃ śaktito bhavet ॥
sahadevī vacā vyāghrī balā cātibalā tathā ॥
śaṅkhapuṣpī tathā siṃhī aṣṭamī tu suvarcalā ॥
mahauṣadhyaṣṭakaṃ hy etan mahāsnānaṃ suyojayet ॥
yavagodhūmanīvāratilaśyāmākaśālayaḥ ॥
priyaṅgavo vrīhayaś ca snāneṣu parikalpayet ॥
svastikaṃ padmakaṃ śaṅkham utpalaṃ kandakaṃ tathā ॥
śrīvatsaṃ darpaṇaṃ tadvan nandyāvarttam athāṣṭakam ॥
etāni gomayaiḥ kuryān mṛdā vā śubhayā tataḥ ॥
pañcavarṇaudanaṃ tadvat pañcavarṇaṃ rajas tathā ॥
dūrvā kṛṣṇabalis tadvan nīrājanavidhir mataḥ ॥
evaṃ nīrājanaṃ kṛtvā dadyād ācamanaṃ budhaḥ ॥
mandākinyās tu yad vāri sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham ॥
tato vastrayugaṃ dadyān mantreṇānena yatnataḥ ॥
vedasūtrasamāyukte yajñarudrasamanvite ॥
sarvavarṇaśubhe deva vāsasī tena nirmite ॥
tatas tu candanaṃ dadyāt samaṃ karpūrakuṅkumaiḥ ॥
[87]        imam uccārayen mantraṃ darbhapāṇiḥ prayatnataḥ ॥
śarīraṃ te na jānāmi ceṣṭāṃ naiva ca naiva ca ॥
mayā niveditaṃ gandhaṃ pratigṛhya vilepanam ॥
catvāriṃśat tato dīpān dadyāc caiva pradakṣiṇām ॥
tvaṃ sūryacandrajyotīṃṣi vidyud agnis tathaiva ca ॥
tvam eva sarvajyotīṃṣi dīpo 'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥
tatas tv anena mantreṇa dhūpaṃ dadyāt vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyo gandha uttamaḥ ॥
mayā nivedito bhatkyā dhūpo yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥
tatas tv ābharaṇaṃ dadyān mahābhūṣāya te namaḥ ॥
anena vidhinā kṛtvā saptarātraṃ mahotsavam ॥
devakumbhais tataḥ kuryād yajamāno 'bhiṣecanam ॥
caturbhir aṣṭabhir vāpi dvābhyām ekena vā punaḥ  ॥ ॥
sapañcaratnakalaśaiḥ sitavastrābhiveṣṭitaiḥ ॥
devasya tveti mantreṇa sāmnā cātharvaṇena ca ॥
abhiṣeke ca ye mantrā navagrahamakhe smṛtāḥ ॥
sitāmbaradharaḥ snātvā devān sampūjya yatnataḥ ॥
sthāpakaṃ pūjayed bhaktyā vastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ ॥
yajñabhāṇḍāṇi sarvāṇi maṇḍapopaskarādikam ॥
yac cāsya dayitaṃ gehe tad ācāryāya dāpayet ॥
suprasanne gurau yasmāt tṛpyante sarvadevatāḥ ॥
[88]        naitad viśīlena na dāmbhikena
na liṅginā sthāpanam atra kāryam ॥
