vipreṇa kāryaṃ śrutipārageṇa
gṛhasthadharmābhiratena nityam ॥
pāṣaṇḍinaṃ yas tu karoti budhyā
vihāya viprān śrutidharmayuktān ॥
guruṃ pratiṣṭhādiṣu tatra nūnaṃ
kulakṣayaḥ syād acirād apūjakaḥ ॥
sthānaṃ piśācaiḥ parigṛhyate vā
apūjyatāṃ yāty acireṇa loke ॥
vipraiḥ kṛtaṃ yac chubhadaṃ kule syāt
prapūjyatāṃ yāti ciraṃ ca kālam ॥
[89]
atha sūryapratiṣṭhā ॥ 10 ॥
[tatra] bhaviṣyapurāṇe [1.134] |
sādhayitvā tu vai bhūmiṃ tuṣakeśavivarjitām ॥
vālukāṅgārapāṣāṇā asthihīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
caturhastasamāyuktā vedī vistarato bhavet ॥
maṇḍapas tu pramāṇena daśahastaḥ samantataḥ ॥
maṇḍalaṃ candraśālābhiḥ kārayed vidhipūrvakam ॥
so ‘bdhijasya samā lokāl labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥
‘candraśālā’ śirogṛham ॥ ‘abdhijaś’ candraḥ ॥
nadīsaṅgamatīrotthāṃ vālukāṃ tatra kārayet ॥
upalipya tato bhūmiṃ kārayet kuṇḍam uttamam ॥
caturasaṃ śriyā yuktaṃ pūrvakuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet ॥
dakṣiṇe cārdhacandraṃ syād vāruṇyāṃ diśi vartulam ॥
padmākāraṃ tu vai kuryād uttareṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
toraṇāni tataḥ kuryāt pañcahastāni suvrataḥ ॥
nyagrodhodumbaraś caiva bilvaḥ pālāśa eva ca ॥
aśvatthaś ca śamī caiva candanaḥ plakṣa eva ca ॥
śuklavastropagūḍhāni daśacīrānvitāni ca ॥
[90]        sālānvitāni sarvāṇi toraṇāni vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
agnim īḷeti mantreṇa dadyād vai pūrvatoraṇam ॥
iṣe tvorjeti mantreṇa yajed dakṣiṇatoraṇam ॥
agna āyāhi mantreṇa paśvimaṃ tu prakalpayet ॥
śaṃ no devīti mantreṇa dadyād uttaratoraṇam ॥
kalaśāṃs tu samādāya hemagarbhasamanvitān ॥
śvetacandanapaṅkena kaṇṭhe svastikabhūṣitān ॥
yavaśāliśarāvais tu vastrālaṅkāravigrahān ॥
ājighreti ca mantreṇa kalaśāṃs tu niveśayet ॥
dukūlaiś citrapaṭṭaiś ca veṣṭayet stambhamālikām ॥
dhvajachatrapatākābhiś cāmarais tu vitānakaiḥ ॥
śaṅkhaghaṇṭāninādaiś ca geyaṃ maṅgalavācanaiḥ ॥
tūryabherīninādaiś ca vedadhvanisamanvitaiḥ ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca kārayet tan mahotsavam ॥
patākābhir vicitrābhir dhvajamālopaśobhitam ॥
vicitrasragvitānasthaṃ prakīrṇakusumotaram ॥
tanmadhye tu kuśāṃs tīrya devārcāṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ ॥
patākāṃ pītavarṇāṃ ca pūrveṇendrāya dāpayet ॥
āgneyyāṃ raktavarṇaṃ ca yāmyāṃ ca yamasaṃnibhām ॥
nīlāñjanasamaprakhyāṃ nairṛtyāṃ ca pradāpayet ॥
[91]        vāruṇyāṃ sitavastrābhāṃ kṛṣṇāṃ vāyavyagocare ॥
haritāṃ yakṣarājāya īśānyāṃ sarvavarṇikām ॥
śvetaraktena varṇena vedīm ālikhya yatnataḥ ॥
vaidyāvedīti mantreṇa vedyā ālabhanaṃ bhavet ॥
pūrvāgrānuttarāgrāṃś ca kuśān āstīrya yatnataḥ ॥
yogeyogeti mantreṇa kuśair āstaraṇaṃ bhavet ॥
śayyā tatraiva kartavyā divyāstaraṇasaṃyutā ॥
gaḍuke dge [?] vicitre tu tanmadhye sthāpayed budhaḥ ॥
dīpamālāṃ vicitrāṃ ca bhakṣyabhojyānnapānakān ॥
apūpāṃś ca vicitrāṃ vai modakāṃś ca pradāpayet ॥
pāyasaṃ kṛśaraṃ caiva dadhyodanasamanvitam ॥
aśvijāṃśusamaprakhyaṃ śubhaṃ chatraṃ ca vinyaset ॥
[nārada uvāca] |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi snānakarmavidhiṃ tava ॥
sthāpakas tu mahāprājño brāhmaṇo vedapāragaḥ ॥
abhijñaḥ sarvaśāstrāṇām āruṇeyaś ca suvrataḥ ॥
bhojako bhojakaiś cānyair brāhmaṇaiś ca yathāvidhi ॥
diśābhāge maṇṭapasya aiśāne vā yathākramam ॥
hastamātrapramāṇaṃ tu bhadrapīṭhaṃ tu vinyaset ॥
hastinā śakaṭenāpi bhaktyā brahmarathena vā ॥
maṅgalair brahmasūtraiś ca devaṃ prāsādam ānayet ॥
[92]        bhadrapīṭhaṃ samādāya bhadraṃ karṇeti mantritam ॥
sūtradhāras tataḥ prājñaḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ śuciḥ ॥
snāpayet kalaśaṃ gṛhya devadevaṃ vibhāvasum ॥
sāmudraṃ toyam āhṛtya jāhnvyaṃ yāmunaṃ tathā ॥
sārasvataṃ jalaṃ puṇyaṃ cāndrabhāgaṃ ca nārmadam ॥
puṣkarasya jalaṃ gṛhya giriprasavaṇodakam ॥
anyad vā śuci yat toyaṃ nadīnadataḍāgajam ॥
yathāśakti samāhṛtya kalaśaiḥ kāñcanādibhiḥ ॥
bhojakāṣṭāv aṣṭabhis tu kalaśaiḥ snāpayanti ca ॥
tatas tu maṇiratnāni sarvabījauṣadhāni ca ॥
sugandhāni ca mālyāni sthalajāny ambujāni ca ॥
candanāni ca mukhyāni gandhāś ca vividhās tathā ॥
brāhmī suvarcalā mustā viṣṇukrāntā śatāvarī ॥
dūrvā ca śaṅkhapuṣpī ca priyaṅgu rajanīvacā ॥
saṃhṛtyaitāṃś ca saṃbhārān snānakarmavidhānavit ॥
vaṭāśvatthaśirīṣāṇāṃ [?] pallavaiḥ kusumānvitaiḥ ॥
kalaśopari vinyastair deyaṃ snānodakaṃ raveḥ ॥
kāñcanair rājatais tāmrair mṛṇmayaiḥ kalaśais tathā ॥
sākṣataiḥ sahiraṇyaiś ca sarvauṣadhisamanvitaiḥ ॥
gāyatryā paripūtais taiḥ sodakaiḥ snāpayed ravim ॥
kuśottarāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vedīṃ pakveṣṭakāmayīm ॥
[93]        tasyāṃ vedyāṃ samāropya paridhāpya ca vāsasī ॥
pratimām abhiṣiñceta sopavāsaḥ prayatnataḥ ॥
mūrdhni sarvoṣadhiṃ datvā tathaivāmalakāni ca ॥
mantreṇa mṛttikāṃ cāpi mantrataś ca jalaṃ tathā ॥
tvaṃ devī vanditā devaiḥ sakalair daityadānavaiḥ ॥
tena tvaṃ sthāpitā mūrdhni mayā devasya śuddhaye ॥
ādis tvaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ devatānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ॥
rasānāṃ pataye tubhyam āhvānaṃ hi kṛtaṃ mayā ॥
itthaṃ paurāṇikair mantrair vaidikaiś ca viśeṣataḥ ॥
kāryaṃ hi vāruṇaṃ snānaṃ devasya yadunandana ॥
idam uccārayan vākyaṃ kuryāt snānaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
devas tvām abhiṣiñcantu brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ ॥
vyomagaṅgāmbupūrṇena kalaśena surottamam ॥
marutaś cābhiṣiñcantu bhuktimanto divaspatim ॥
meghatoyābhipūrṇena dvitīyakalaśena tu ॥
sārasvatena toyena pūrṇena surasattamāḥ ॥
vidyādharābhiṣiñcantu tṛtīyakalaśena tu ॥
[94]        śakrādyāś cābhiṣiñcantu ṛṣayaḥ saptakhecarāḥ ॥
vasavaś cābhiṣiñcantu kalaśenāṣṭamena ca ॥
aṣṭamaṅgalayuktena devadeva namo 'stu te ॥
tato vai kalaśair divyaiḥ snānakarma samārabhet ॥
samudraṃ gaccha yat proktaṃ mantram etam udīrayet ॥
samudrajyeṣṭheti mantreṇa kṣālayen mṛttikānvitam ॥
sinīvālīti mantreṇa dadyād valmīkamṛttikām ॥
śamyodumbaram aśvatthaṃ nyagrodhaṃ ca palāśakam ॥
yajñaṃ yajñe 'ti mantreṇa dadyāt pañcakaṣāyikam ॥
pañcagavyaṃ pavitraṃ tu āharet tāmrabhājane ॥
gāyatryā caiva gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam ॥
[95]        āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇe dadhi smṛtam [?] ॥
tejo 'si śukram ity uktaṃ devasyatvāt kuśodakam ॥
evamādi vidhiyuktaṃ pañcagavyaṃ prakīrtitam ॥
yā auṣadhīti mantreṇa snānam oṣadhbhiḥ smṛtam ॥
drupadādi punas tasya kuryāc codvartanaṃ budhaḥ ॥
śiraḥsnānaṃ tato dadyān mānastokābhimantritam ॥
[pañcagavyaṃ pavitraṃ tu āharet tatra yojayet ॥
gāyatryā caiva gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam ॥
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi ॥
tejo 'si śukram ity ājyaṃ devasyatvāt kuśodakam ॥ ]
viṣṇor arāṭa mantreṇa dadyād gandhodakaṃ śubham ॥
tato nadyudakenaivaṃ kṣālayec chuddhavāriṇā ॥
jātavedasam uccārya vastrapūtena vāriṇā ॥
tata āvāhayed devaṃ raktamālyāmbaraṃ śubham ॥
ehy ehi bhagavān bhāno lokānugrahakāraka ॥
yajñabhāgaṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ arkadeva namo 'stu te ॥
hiraṇmayena pātreṇa arghapātraṃ pradāpayet ॥
idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrame mantreṇārghaṃ pradāpayet ॥
pārthivaiḥ kalaśaiḥ pūrvaṃ snāpayed bhāskaraṃ budhaḥ ॥
tatas tv audumbarair vīra rājatais tadanantaram ॥
audumbaraiḥ tāmramayaiḥ ॥
[96]        tatas tu kāñcanair devaṃ snāpayed yadunandanam ॥
sarvatīrthajalair yuktaṃ sarvoṣadhisamanvitam ॥
śaṅkham ādāya devasya tato mūrddhani saṃkiret ॥
datvā puṣpāṇi devasya mūrdhni yatnād vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
dhūpam utkṣipya yatnena tataḥ snāpanam ācaret ॥
prathamaṃ snāpayed devaṃ vāriṇā yadunandanam ॥
tatas tu payasā rājan pāyasena tatas tu tam ॥
ghṛtena madhunā cāpi tataś cekṣurasena ca ॥
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya gosavasya ca suvrata ॥
jyotiṣṭomasya rājendra vājapeyasya vā vibho ॥
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ ghṛtādyair labhate phalam ॥
yas tu kārayate snānaṃ yas tu bhaktyā prapaśyati  ॥
snāne tu yatnataḥ kāryaṃ devadevasya suvrata ॥
yathā na laṅghayet kaccid devasya snapanaṃ vibho ॥
na prāśnanti yathā kākā śvāno vā lokagarhitāḥ ॥
snapayitvā krameṇetthaṃ snānakarmavidhānavit ॥
tato varddhanikāṃ gṛhya vāridhārāḥ samutsṛjet ॥
tisras tu purato 'rkasya ācamasveti ca bruvan ॥
vedo 'sīti ca mantreṇa upavītaṃ pradāpayet ॥
[97]        bṛhaspateti mantreṇa vastrayugmaṃ pradāpayet ॥
yaśaḥ śriyaṃ prakurvāṇaḥ puṣpamālāṃ pradāpayet ॥
dhūrasīti ca mantreṇa dhūpaṃ dadyāt saguggulam ॥
'        samiddhoñjana mantreṇa añjanaṃ ca pradāpayet ॥
yuñjāneti ca mantreṇa rocanāṃ tasya dāpayet ॥
ārārtikaṃ ca vai kuryād dīrghāyatvāya varcase ॥
snānakrama idaṃ proktaṃ bhāskarasya mahātmanaḥ ॥
bhojakā brāhmaṇāś caiva kriyāṃ kuryur mahātmanaḥ ॥
bahavṛco 'tharvaṇaś caiva chandogo ‘dhvaryur eva ca ॥
sthāpakasya ca cihnāni ye ca mūrtidharāḥ smṛtāḥ ॥
teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi bhedo (bhedān?) śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ svayam ॥
sampūrṇagātro matimān śāstrajñaḥ priyadarśanaḥ ॥
kulīnaḥ śraddadhānaś ca āryadeśasamudbhavaḥ ॥
na sthūlakṛśadīrghāṅgaḥ sauraśāstravidhānavit ॥
śuddhajanmābhiyuktaś ca gurubhakto jitendriyaḥ ॥
pañcaviṃśatitattvajñaḥ sthāpakaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ॥
varjanīyas tu vakṣyāmi yais tu karma na kārayet ॥
hīnāṅgaś cātiriktāṅgo vāmano vikaṭas tathā ॥
nātigauro nātikṛṣṇaḥ sthāpanāya praśasyate ॥
[98]        cārvāko yācakaś caiva nityaṃ gomukhadaṃśakaḥ ॥
aśucir vraṇasaṃyuktaḥ śyāmadanto 'tha matsarī ॥
rogayukto 'tha bālaś ca yuvā vā vṛddha eva ca ॥
śvitrī kuṣṭhī ca rogī ca kāṇo durmatir eva ca ॥
saṃkīrṇo jātihīnaś ca tathā ca vṛṣalīpatiḥ ॥
kubjāndhakaś ca khalvāṭo vyaṅgo vai vikalendriyaḥ ॥
avinīto durātmā ca vikalaḥ paṅgur eva ca ॥
tithinakṣatrayogānāṃ vārāṇāṃ ca tathā vibho ॥
sūcako jīvitārthaṃ hi yaś ca mūlyena pāṭhayet ॥
[bhaviṣyapurāṇe, 1.136] |
nārada uvāca |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi adhivāsanam uttamam ॥
sahasraśīrṣā puruṣo maṇḍapaṃ yatnato viśet ॥
tato 'nyatra śucau deśe susaṃmṛṣṭopalepane ॥
maṇḍapaṃ pañcavarṇais tu ālikhec cāturastrikam ॥
patākātoraṇacchatradhvajamālādyalaṅkṛtam ॥
vicitrasragvitānāḍhyaṃ prakīrṇakusumotkaram ॥
tasya madhye kuśāstīrṇā mūrtiḥ sthāpyā vivasvataḥ ॥
[99]        tato 'syāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā dadyād arghaṃ prayatnataḥ ॥
suvarṇamadhuparkādi sumanodhūpadīpakaiḥ ॥
devasya darśayed gāṃ ca savatsāṃ rohiṇīṃ śubhām ॥
‘rohiṇī; lohitavarṇā ॥
namo gopatayety uktvā sahasrāṃśo prasīda me ॥
evam argheṇa sampūjya paridhāya ca vāsasī ॥
yajñopavītam ābadhya badhvāvyaṅgaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
vatsare vatsare tasya navam avyaṅgam ādiśet ॥
śrāvaṇe māsi rājendra pavitraṃ tasya tadbudhaḥ ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu varṣe varṣe prayojayet ॥
avyaṅgaṃ yaduśārdūla śrāvaṇe māsi bhāskare ॥
'’avyaṅgaṃ’ vratakāṇḍasya kṛtye vyākhyātam ॥
sarvagandhaiḥ samālabhya candanāgurukuṅkumaiḥ ॥
alaṅkārair alaṅkṛtvā kusumaiś ca sugandhibhiḥ ॥
mālābhiś ca vicitrābhir ābaddhābhir anekaśaḥ ॥
tato dhūpaṃ nivedyāśu pratimāyai prayatnataḥ ॥
sahasraśīrṣā puruṣo maṇḍalaṃ taṃ praveśayet ॥
śaṃ bhavāyeti mantreṇa śayyāyāṃ viniveśayet ॥
viśvataścakṣur ity evaṃ kuryāt sakalaniṣkalam ॥
punar eva pravakṣyāmi śakalīkaraṇaṃ śubham ॥
[100]        sthāpakena sadā kāryaṃ svadehanyāsam uttamam ॥
pratimāyāṃ tathā kuryāt tathā cālambhanaṃ budhaḥ ॥
oṃ haṃ khaṃ kha kholkāya namaḥ mūlamantraṃ prakīrtayet ॥
ādityo 'yaṃ svayaṃ devas tryakṣareṇopabṛṃhitaḥ ॥
oṃkāraṃ vinyasen mūrdhni haṃkāraṃ nāsakopari ॥
vakāraṃ ca lalāṭe tu khakāraṃ vadane nyaset ॥
oṃkāraṃ caiva kaṇṭhe tu lakāraṃ hṛdaye nyaset ॥
yakāraṃ tu bhuje vāme nakāraṃ dakṣiṇe bhuje ॥
makāraṃ vāmakukṣau tu nakāraṃ dakṣiṇena tu ॥
nakāraṃ vāmapade tu makāraṃ dakṣiṇe nyaset ॥
oṃkāraṃ tu sadā dhyāyej jvālāmālāsamākulam ॥
hakāraṃ śuddhavarṇābhaṃ prasravantaṃ jalaṃ śubham ॥
khakāraṃ cintayet prājño bhinnāñjanasamaprabham ॥
taruṇāditya'varuṇābhaṃ khakāraṃ cintayed budhaḥ ॥
khokāraṃ tu mahābāho hemavarṇaṃ vicintayet ॥
śuklapadmanibhākāram akāraṃ cintayed budhaḥ ॥
jātīkusumasaṃkāśaṃ yakāraṃ cintayed budhaḥ ॥
nakāraṃ himakundābhaṃ makāram amṛtākṣaram ॥
[101]        hrīṃkāraṃ vidyutsaṃkāśaṃ hakāraṃ pītavarṇakam ॥
kṣīravarṇaṃ sakāraṃ tu cintayet satataṃ budhaḥ ॥
nakāraṃ taruṇābhaṃ tu makāraṃ kanakaprabham ॥
tato devaṃ mahātmānaṃ sahasrakiraṇaṃ ravim ॥
prasādābhimukhaṃ devaṃ śayanīye niveśayet ॥
agnikāryaṃ tataḥ kuryur agnikāṇḍeṣu vai dvijāḥ ॥
tato rathāt samutthāpya bhagaḥ saurādayo 'pi vā ॥
prajvālya ca vidhānena kuryur homaṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ ॥
bahvṛcaḥ pūrvakuṇḍe tu yāmye vājasaneyakaḥ ॥
paścime sāmavedas tu uttare 'tharvaṇo mataḥ ॥
madhye tu bhojakaḥ kuryād dhomaṃ prājño yadūttama ॥
pālāśodumbaraśamī apāmārgas tathaiva ca ॥
dvādaśaiva sahasrāṇi aṣṭau catvāri pañca vā ॥
dve trīṇi ca sahasrāṇi atha vāpy ekam eva hi ॥
agnir mūrddheti mantreṇa kuṇḍasthālambhanaṃ bhavet ॥
ullekhābhyukṣaṇe caiva agnidūtam iti smṛte ॥
saptame agna iti mantreṇa garbhādhānaṃ tu kārayet ॥
sīmanteti sahasraṃ tu mahāmantreṇa homayet ॥
[102]        jātakarma tathā proktaṃ prāṇāyeti vidur budhāḥ ॥
namaḥ svāheti mantreṇa nāmakarmaṇi homayet ॥
brahmayajñeti mantreṇa niḥkramaṃ homayet punaḥ ॥
annaprāśanamantreṇa annaprāśanam ādiśet ॥
‘annaprāśanamantreṇa’ annapatte 'nnasyetyanena ॥
jyeṣṭa yajñeti mantreṇa tathā cūlopakarmaṇi ॥
vratabandhasya mantreṇa vratabandhaṃ samācaret ॥
samāvartanam ity eva ākṛṣṇeti ca homayet ॥
patnī saṃyojane caiva tryambakaṃ ca prakalpayet ॥
agnihotrādikaṃ karma yajñakarmāṇi yāni ca ॥
mahāvyāhṛtimantreṇa hotavyāni samantataḥ ॥
mātṛṇāṃ yajñabhūtānāṃ balikarma pradāpayet ॥
sarvakāmasamṛddhyarthaṃ kārayed adhivāsanam ॥
trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā ahorātram athāpi vā ॥
tatas tv alaṅkṛtāṃ snātāṃ maṇiratnair vibhūṣitām ॥
kṛtarakṣāṃ tu yatnena pratimām adhivāsayet ॥
devāgārād atheśāne digbhāge divyasaṃjñite ॥
kuśair upari saṃstīrṇe varāstaraṇasaṃvṛte ॥
pūrvaśīrṣāṃ tathā śayyāṃ śuklāṃ śuklāmbarottarām ॥
śayyām āveśayet samyak mahāśvetām udāharan ॥
nikumbho dakṣiṇe pārśve vāme rājñi prakīrtitā ॥
[103]        daṇḍinau tu gaṇau cāsya sthitau pādapradeśataḥ ॥
tasyāṃ tu vai śitāyāṃ tu śarvaryāṃ pratimā raveḥ ॥
vasetāṃ rajanīṃ tatra stūyamānaś caturdiśam ॥
brāhmaṇair vahnibhiś cāpi gītajñaiś cāraṇais tathā ॥
kuryāj jāgaraṇaṃ tatra sūryabhaktisamanvitaḥ ॥
prabhātāyāṃ tu śarvaryyāṃ bodhayeta vidhānataḥ ॥
haviṣyaṃ bhojayitvā tu brāhmaṇān bhojakāṃs tathā ॥
dakṣiṇābhiś ca saṃpūjya taiḥ kṛte svastivācane ॥
tato garbhagṛhasyātha madhye kṛtvātha piṇḍikām ॥
avaṭaiś cāsya sauvarṇaṃ nyaset saptahayaṃ ratham ॥
tatra sarvauṣadhīś caiva tathā datvā vidhānavit ॥
datvārdhaṃ sthāpayet tatra yajamānaḥ sahāyavān ॥
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣair jaladhāraṃ sahākṣataiḥ  ॥ ॥
kṛtvā puṇyāhaśabdena ālayasya pradakṣiṇam ॥
sthāpanaṃ ca śubhe lagne dine ṛkṣe śubhe tithau ॥
muhūrte ca śubhe bhānoḥ pratimāṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ ॥
nādhomukhīṃ norddhvamukhīṃ na pārśvāvanatāṃ tathā ॥
samānābhimukhīṃ cemāṃ pratimāṃ ca niveśayet ॥
patnyau cāsya tathā samyak pārśvayoś ca niveśayet ॥
nakumbho dakṣiṇe pārśve rave rājñī tu cāgrataḥ ॥
[104]        tatas tairūparāhārthaṃ [?] sambhāraiḥ prāksamāhṛtaiḥ ॥
modakollāpikāpūpaśaṣkulībhūtaśīrṣakaiḥ ॥
kṛśarāpāyasonmiśraḥ sarvadikṣu kṣiped balim ॥
indrāya pūrvapataye baliṃ te vajrapāṇaye ॥
śatayajñādhipe tasmai pūrva-indrāya vai namaḥ ॥
trātāram indramantreṇa indrasyāvāhanaṃ bhavet ॥
agnaye raktanetrāya jvālāmālācitāya vai ॥
śaktihastāya tīvrāya namo 'stu kṛṣṇavartmane ॥
agnaye gāyatrāyeti vahner āvāhanaṃ smṛtam ॥
daṇḍahastāya kṛṣṇāya mahiṣadhvajavāhine ॥
sūryaputrāya devāya dharmarājāya vai namaḥ ॥
yamāya tvā makhāya tvā mantras tasmai prakīrtitaḥ ॥
nairṛtyāṃ khaḍgahastāya nīlalocanakāya ca ॥
sarvarakṣo’dhipataye virūpākṣāya te namaḥ ॥
āyaṅgaurīti mantreṇa nairṛtyāṃ tu prakalpayet ॥
vāruṇyāṃ pāśahastāya ūṣārūḍhasthitāya ca ॥
nimnagāpataye vīra varuṇāya ca vai namaḥ ॥
mantreṇāvāhanaṃ vidyāt pañcanadyaḥ sarasvatī ॥
prāṇātmakāya dhūmrāya sarvagāyānilāya ca ॥
dhvajahastāya bhīmāya namo gandhavahāya ca ॥
tasyāpy āvāhanaṃ vidyād yad devā devaheḍanam ॥
[105]        gadāhastāya somāya śuṣmiṇe rathagāya ca ॥
gadāpaṭṭiśahastāya somarājāya vai namaḥ ॥
āpyāyasva same tu te somamantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
gaṇādhipataye deva nīlakaṇṭhāya śūline ॥
virūpākṣāya rudrāya trailokyādhipataye namaḥ ॥
abhi tvā śūra nonumo īśānāya prakalpayet ॥
sarvanāgādhirājāya śvetavarṇāya bhogine ॥
sahasraśīrṣāya nityam anantāya namo namaḥ ॥
namo 'stu sarpebhya iti mantra eṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
caturmukhāya devāya padmāsanagatāya ca ॥
kṛṣṇājinaghṛtāṅgāya srucahastāya vai namaḥ ॥
brahmajñānam ity asya mantra eṣa udāhṛtaḥ ॥
namas te gajavaktrāya siddhibuddhipradāya ca ॥
namas te modakaruce namo lambodarāya ca ॥
gaṇānāṃ tvā gaṇapatim iti mantra udāhṛtaḥ ॥
pañcaratnādi vinyāsaṃ ratnanyāsaṃ prayojayet ॥
tarpayet kṣīramadhvājaiḥ stutistotraiś ca bhāskaram ॥
viprebhyo bhojakebhyaś ca tato deyā ca dakṣiṇā ॥
sūryakratuṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ naiva kuryāc ca dakṣiṇe ॥
saṃsthāpya tena vidhinā tadbhaktaiḥ pratimā tu yā ॥
sādhuvṛttikarī nityaṃ 'śāntidā ca sadā bhavet ॥
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ sthāpayed yas tu bhāskaram ॥
[106]        sa tīrṇaḥ sarvasaṃsārāt dhanamālitvam āpnuyāt ॥
paśyanti mānavā ye ‘pi ādityasyādhivāsanam ॥
saptajanmaśataṃ teṣāṃ na rogāḥ sambhavanti hi ॥
trirātraṃ ye 'py upāsante bhānor yatrādhivāsane ॥
gandhamālyopahāraiś ca te yānti bhavanaṃ raveḥ ॥
ātmīyaṃ parakīyaṃ vā pratimāsthāpanaṃ raveḥ ॥
yaḥ paśyati pumān bhaktyā svargalokam avāpnuyāt ॥
daśānām aśvamedhānāṃ vājapeyaśatasya ca ॥
phalaṃ prāpnoti puruṣaḥ pratiṣṭhāpya divākaram ॥
yāvat kīrtiḥ puṇyavato bhānoḥ sthānaniveśinaḥ ॥
tāvat sa tu yaduśreṣṭha sūryaloke mahīyate ॥
sthāpane cāsya vai mantraḥ samprokto lokapūjitaḥ ॥
dhruvā dyauś ca dhruvā pṛthvī dhruvaṃ viśvam idaṃ jagat ॥
śreyase yajamānasya tathā tvaṃ dhruvatāṃ vraja ॥
sthāpayitvā raviṃ bhaktyā vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
māsi māsi kratuphalaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥
ekāhenāpi yad bhānoḥ pūjayā prāpyate phalam ॥
na tat kratuśatair vīra prāpyate mānavo bhuvi ॥
[107]
atha śivapratiṣṭhā ॥ 11 ॥
[tatra] bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
[nārada uvāca] |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi adhivāsavidhikriyām ॥
tithinakṣatrayogāṃś ca sthāpakasya tu lakṣaṇam ॥
vyasanī vāmanaḥ kubjaḥ krodhanaḥ kunakhī śaṭhaḥ ॥
capalaś ca khalaś caiva hīnāṅgaḥ sūcakaḥ kṣayī ॥
vyādhitas tārkiko rājan mithyācāraḥ śaṭho ‘vratī (?) ॥
kākasvaro 'lpavidyaś ca matsarī samayacyutaḥ ॥
ajñaḥ sāhasiko lubdhaḥ śyāmadanto 'jitendriyaḥ ॥
gurudevadvijātīnāṃ nindako vṛṣalīpatiḥ ॥
piśuno dhānyavikretā kṛtaghnaḥ kāmatatparaḥ ॥
puṇyājīvī devalakaḥ kuṇḍajo nāstiko vṛṣaḥ ॥
klībaḥ krūraḥ kṛtaghnaś ca svadharmaparivarjitaḥ ॥
purodhāḥ sevakaś caiva paṇyastrī paradāragaḥ ॥
nodvigo na ca dīnātmā nāśucir nāpy asaṃskṛtaḥ ॥
putrako vāpi yedveṣṭā [?] sthāpakān parivarjayet ॥
prāsādasyāgrataḥ pūrvam anagho dhīmatāṃ varaḥ ॥
prāguttaraplavāṃ mantrī bhūmiṃ saṃśodhya yatnataḥ ॥
[108]        triśaddhastaṃ samaṃ tatra tathā viṃśatisaṃkhyayā ॥
daśaṣoḍaśahastaṃ vā maṇḍapaṃ kāmato 'pi vā ॥
vidiksthaiḥ stambhavinyāsaiḥ kārayet sumanoharam ॥
kaṭaiś chāditadigbhāgaṃ patākābhir dhvajair api ॥
caturhastā bhaved vedī caturbhis toraṇair yutā ॥
upalipyā kṛtais tatra śambhor bhavanam ālikhet ॥
caturasraṃ caturdvāraṃ samasūtram aninditam ॥
toyākṣatahiraṇyādyaiḥ kalaśair upaśobhitam ॥
pūrveṇendrāyudhanibhā āgneyām agnivarcasā ॥
kṛṣṇā dakṣiṇato rājan haritā caiva nairṛte ॥
vāruṇe śikhipicchābhā pītā cānilagocare ॥
uttare gopakanibhā patākā ceśvare hitā ॥
daṇḍakaiḥ pañcahastā tu daśahastān dhvajān api ॥
citravinyastadigbhāgān madhye vedīṃ prakalpayet ॥
pradīpadīpikābhiś ca ghaṇṭābhiś cāmarais tathā ॥
citraiḥ paṭavitānaiś ca ratnavic charitāmbaraiḥ ॥
vicitrair ratnavinyāsair ujvalair manasaḥ priyaiḥ ॥
ujvaladbhiś ca maṇibhiḥ stambhāsaktaiś ca darpaṇaiḥ ॥
hāraiḥ pralambamānaiś ca vidrumastabakais tathā ॥
phalastabakavinyāsaiḥ pallavaiś ca pṛthagvidhaiḥ ॥
srakdāmacāmaraiś chatraiḥ samantād bhavanaṃ nṛpaḥ ॥
[109]        bhūṣayitvā yathālābhaṃ tatreśānaṃ niveśayet ॥
nīrāṃ darbhāṃ samādāya gandhodakapariplutām ॥
udapā ....prā........ vedyām upari vinyaset ॥
karṇikāyā........ liṅgapracchādanapaṭānvitam ॥
sopadhānaṃ śivasyaivaṃ mantraṃ smṛtvā niyojayet ॥
pārśvataś cādmikuṇḍāni dikṣu kramavicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
prakalpayed yathoktāni catvāry ekam athāpi vā ॥
ācāryān mantratatvajñān aṣṭau mūrtidharān śubhān ॥
varayet putrakānyāpi tadabhāve dṛḍhavratān ॥
prakalpyaivam anudvignaḥ sthāpayec chivam āditaḥ ॥
tasya caivottare pārśve kartavyaṃ snānamaṇḍapam ॥
suśobhanaṃ tu kartavyaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ vālukāmayam ॥
tatas tasyāpare pārśve kṛtvā sthaṇḍilam āyatam ॥
akṣataiś caturasraṃ ca svastikāntikavigraham ॥
caturdvāraṃ (?) likhen mantrī caturbhis toraṇair yutam ॥
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ dārujaṃ kauśam eva ca ॥
tasya pīṭhaṃ nyasen madhye yathālābham aninditam ॥
dadhyakṣataśamīpatraphalāmbukusumādibhiḥ ॥
pātrāṇy aṣṭau suvarṇāni dikṣv aṣṭāsu niveśayet ॥
vinyased vaiṇave pātre raktavastrayugaṃ śubham ॥
[110]        āharet pūrṇapātreṇa rocanālaktakair yutam ॥
śarīrodvartanaṃ rājan yavagodhūmasaṃbhavam ॥
māṣapiṣṭamayaṃ vāpi gandhaṃ digdham upāharet ॥
jaṭāmāṃsy utpalopetaṃ devasyāmalakāny api ॥
vilepanaṃ ca vividham āhareta janādhipa ॥
kusumāni vicitrāṇi ratnāny ābharaṇāni ca ॥
vividhāni ca vastrāṇi pūjārtham upakalpayet ॥
striyaś cāvidhavās tatra catasraḥ samalaṅkṛtāḥ ॥
āhared brāhmaṇakulān maṅgalān īrayanti yāḥ ॥
anyāṃś ca vādakuśalān gāyanāṃś ca tathāvidhān ॥
ṛkpāṭhakāṃs tathā viprān āhareta narādhipa ॥
sarvopakaraṇāny evaṃ āhṛtya tadanantaram ॥
gomayenāparaṃ mantrī kṛtvā maṇḍalakaṃ punaḥ ॥
tatra liṅgaṃ samānīya sudhṛtaṃ suparīkṣitam ॥
valmīkamṛttikābhis tu gomayena sabhasmanā ॥
kṣālayec chilpisaṃsparśadoṣāṇām upaśāntaye ॥
tataḥ śubhreṇa vastreṇa tat saṃmṛjya sugandhinā ॥
paścimāyāṃ tu vinyasya garbhamānavidhānavit ॥
rocanāliptasūtreṇa garbham āsphālayen nṛpa ॥
tatas tadūrdhvabhāgaṃ tu tribhir bhāgair vibhājitam ॥
kṛtvā samālikhed vṛttam adhobhāge tu lakṣaṇam ॥
gokarṇasadṛśākāraṃ tīkṣṇāgraṃ vā samālikhet ॥
[111]        brahmasūtrāvatārārthaṃ maṇirekhāṃ samāṃ śubhām ॥
na karālāṃ na cottānāṃ na vakrāṃ sarvataḥ samām ॥
jyeṣṭasyāṣṭayavapratyāṃ maṇirekhāṃ prakalpayet ॥
śeṣāṇām api liṅgānāṃ viditvā yāmavistarau ॥
kārayet sa navaṃ mantrī lakṣaṇaṃ sūkṣmam icchatā ॥
mṛddāruśailaliṅgānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ samudāhṛtam ॥
lakṣaṇe tu kṛte liṅgaṃ tryahād ūrdhvaṃ na dhārayet ॥
tryahād ūrdhvaṃ niviṣṭe tu pūjanaṃ na bhavet sadā ॥
siddhibhaṅgaṃ karoty āśu sādhakānāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ॥
tasmāt tannipuṇo mantrī śobhane' hani yatnataḥ ॥
śuklavāsāḥ śuciḥ snāto maṇirekhāṃ prakalpayet ॥
kāṃsyadohāṃ raupyakhurāṃ hemaśṛṅgīṃ payasvinīm ॥
gāṃ dadyāt sthāpakaśreṣṭhau maṇirekhāṃ samālikhet ॥
na nakhena na kāṣṭhena nāṅgāreṇa na bhasmanā ॥
lakṣaṇaṃ kalpayec chambhor na kapālāśmaloṣṭakaiḥ ॥
suvarṇaṃ śucim ādāya prayatnena narādhipa ॥
sūtrakāryaṃ prakurvīta yatnena na yathecchayā ॥
[112]        oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya hiraṇyaretase parāya paramātmane ॥
viśvarūpāya umāpriyāya namaḥ ॥
alakṣaṇaṃ yato liṅgaṃ na bhavaty arthasiddhaye ॥
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena lakṣaṇaṃ tu prakalpayet ॥
nātisthūlaṃ kṛśaṃ rājan viṣamaṃ vikramaṃ tathā ॥
madhyamaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tatra pūrayen madhusarpiṣā ॥
nadīkūlāt samādāya valmīkāc ca mṛdaṃ śubhām ॥
tābhir gandhodakāsnābhiḥ kṣālayel liṅgam āditaḥ ॥
tanmaheśābhijaptena pañcagavyena buddhimān ॥
snāpayed divyagandhaiś ca kaṣāyaiḥ kṣīravṛkṣajaiḥ ॥
kuṅkumāktena toyena prakṣālya tadanantaram ॥
gandhamālyair alaṅkṛtya candanāgurudhūpitam ॥
vācayitvā dvijavarān nudrāyety [?] atra saṃsmaret ॥
śanakair liṅgam uddhṛtya pīṭhasyopari vinyaset ॥
tasya dakṣiṇataḥ sthitvā śivam ātmānam eva ca ॥
mānasīṃ vṛttim āsthāya kuryāt sakalaniṣkalam ॥
sakalīkṛtya deveśam evam arcādi pūrvavat ॥
raktavastrayugaṃ cāsya vāmena paridhāya ca ॥
tato rocanayā liṅgam abhyajya tu ghṛtāktayā ॥
alaktakaiś ca .... saṃspṛśet sugandhibhiḥ ॥
catasras tāḥ striyaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ kramād arghādipūrvakam ॥
savyāpasavyasūtreṇa saṃspṛśeyuḥ kuśair api ॥
[113]        aṣṭārghapātratoyena vastrālambena buddhimān ॥
spṛṣṭvāṣṭau mūrtayaḥ śambhos tābhir arghaṃ pradāpayet ॥
vastrālambaiś ca vidhivad dadhikṣīraghṛtādibhiḥ ॥
snāpayed īśvaraṃ mantrī yathālābham anukramāt ॥
udvarttayet tato liṅgam aṅgodvartanakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
tastathedaṃ yathā divyaiś cāmalakādibhiḥ  ॥ ॥
caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ pūrvam aṣṭabhis tadanantaram ॥
snāpayed devadeveśaṃ śūlinaṃ mantrasaṃstutaiḥ ॥
sitavastreṇa deveśaṃ parimṛjya sugandhinā ॥
candanenālabhet snānaṃ gandhavādyair alaṅkṛtaiḥ ॥
paridhāyya ca guhyena śuklavastraṃ sugandhibhiḥ ॥
pūjayet kusumaiś citrair bhūṣaṇaiś cābhimantritaiḥ ॥
tato mūrtidharān aṣṭāv aṅgulīyakakuṇḍalaiḥ ॥
āhūya vastrayugalair gandhamālyaiś ca pūjayet ॥
taiḥ śāyayītam īśānam uddhṛtya kalaśaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
pūrvasthitipadaṃ kṛtvā śayane saṃniyojayet ॥
śubhena sitavastreṇa goptā ity atra saṃsmaran ॥
chādayet pūjayitvā tu vividhaiḥ kusumair vibhum ॥
lokapālaiś ca saṃyuktaṃ maṅgalaiś ca samantataḥ ॥
tarpayed vividhair bhakṣyair adhivāsitam īśvaram ॥
devīṃ gaṇādhikā rājan piṇḍikārūpadhāriṇīm ॥
śivavad raktavasanāṃ svamantreṇādhivāsayet ॥
[114]        piṇḍikābhihitā devī liṅgaṃ devaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
tayor yaḥ kurute yogaṃ sa gacchet paramaṃ padam ॥
na hi devasya devyāś ca viyogo 'sti kadācana ॥
ato 'rtham anayor yogaḥ kriyate samakālikaḥ ॥
piṇḍikārahitaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ vidhināpi yat ॥
na kalpayati sāṃnidhyaṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇāti naiva ca ॥
śokam āvahate kartuḥ pūjakānām anirvṛtiḥ ॥
tasmān na sthāpayel liṅgaṃ piṇḍikārahitaṃ budhaḥ ॥
vināpi piṇḍikāṃ rūpaṃ lobhād ajñānato 'pi vā ॥
yaḥ sthāpayati so 'bhyeti narakaṃ pāpakarmakṛt ॥
vāhanaṃ ca maheśasya dharmaṃ vṛṣabhalakṣaṇam ॥
gandhapuṣpopahāraiś ca tam apy evādhivāsayet ॥
anenaiva vidhānena mantrī skandavināyakau ॥
sthāpayed devatāś cānyāḥ svamantraiḥ sapitāmahāḥ ॥
adhivāsya tato devaṃ devīṃ vṛṣabham eva ca ॥
gaṇānāṃ tvā vidhivad dadyād baliṃ dikṣu vidikṣu ca ॥
tatra devāḥ sagandharvāḥ sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ ॥
piśācā mātaraḥ sarvā gaṇā vidyādharās tathā ॥
bhūtāni pitaraś caiva ṛṣayo 'nyāś ca devatāḥ ॥
parivāryeha tiṣṭhanti deveśam adhivāsitam ॥
śaṅkaraḥ sarvadevānāṃ pūjyo vandyaḥ paraṃ guruḥ ॥
tasmād evaṃ vidhānajñaḥ pūjayed balibhiḥ purā ॥
sitoṣṇīṣadharas tasmāc chraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ ॥
[115]        maunī bhasmasu saṃsnātaḥ prasannavadanekṣaṇaḥ ॥
kuṇḍeṣv ananyadhīḥ samyag janayitvā hutāśanam ॥
agnikāryaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā (?) mantrī mūrtidharaiḥ saha ॥
śivāṅgabrahmavidyeśalokapālātra saṃyutaiḥ ॥
homaṃ prakalpayen mantrair yathāśāstram aninditaḥ ॥
pūrveṇa brahmabhir homam aṅgair dakṣiṇatas tathā ॥
vidyeśaiḥ paścime tatra lokapālais tathottare ॥
astrair vidikṣu gāyatryā taiś caiva sahito hunet ॥
śikhinaḥ sarvamantrāṇi mantrapūrṇāhutiṃ kṣipet ॥
ghṛtena payasā vāpi tilair vāpi ghṛtaplutaiḥ ॥
homaṃ kuryād yathālābhaṃ madhumegnau (madhunāgnau) vidhānavit ॥
pālāśībhiḥ samidbhir vā ghṛtāktābhir asambhave ॥
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavābhir vā lājair dūrvābhir eva vā ॥
akṣatais taṇḍulair vāpi phalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sapallavaiḥ ॥
tarpayed bhakṣyabhojyair vā ghṛtālābhe śivānale ॥
mantram ekaikam evaṃ tu hutvā viṃśatisaṅkhyayā ॥
triḥ kṛtvā saṃspṛśed devaṃ śirasy urasi pādayoḥ ॥
evam etena vidhinā yo 'dhivāsayatīśvaram ॥
tarpitaṃ cārcitaṃ tena jagad etac carācaram ॥
tarpayitvā maheśānam evam agniśarīriṇam ॥
saṃhṛtya hṛnmaye vyomni śivālayam ataḥ param ॥
kalaśān upasaṃhṛtya baliṃ naivedyam eva ca ॥
[116]        arcayet snāpayed rājan pūjayec ca yathāvidhi ॥
sadyena snāpanaṃ kuryād aghoreṇa vilepanam ॥
vāmena cāstravinyāsa īśānenārcayec chivam ॥
puṣpair nānāvidhair divyaiḥ padmair hṛdayahāribhiḥ ॥
mukuṭair maṇiratnādimauktikacchuritāntaraiḥ ॥
devam evam alaṅkṛtvā astreṇārghaṃ nivedya ca ॥
praṇipatya yathānyāyaṃ vācayitvā dvijottamān ॥
tato mūrtidharair īśaṃ svahṛdistham asya vā sthitaiḥ ॥
aṣṭābhir ālabhen mantrī yugapat svāsu mūrtiṣu ॥
svayaṃ mūrtidharaiś caiva caturbhiḥ samalaṅkṛtam ॥
pañcāṅgena samālabhya kalākalitavigraham ॥
puṇyāhaśabdanir ghoṣaiḥ gītavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
vedaśabdajayāśībhiḥ pavitrais tu nibandhanaiḥ ॥
sadyojātam anusmṛtvā tasmād uddhṛtya bhūtalāt ॥
sumantritaṃ nayen mantrī samama skhalitaṃ vibhum ॥
dhriyamāṇena devasya ātapatreṇa mūrdhani ॥
citraiḥ paṭṭavitānair vādhriyamāṇair manoramaiḥ ॥
cāmarair hemadaṇḍaiś ca vījyamānam itas tataḥ ॥
svastivāci caturdikṣu puraṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam ॥
[117]        anyathā ........na drutaṃ na vilambitam ॥
praveśayet prayatnena punar ānīya ceśvaram ॥
na kruddho na ca dīnātmā prasannamukhalocanaḥ ॥
samasūtraṃ tu niṣkampam acalāsanam īśvaram ॥
śanair brahmaśilāyāṃ tu guhyamantreṇa vinyaset ॥
piṇḍikām apy anenaiva mantreṇa vidhipūrvakam ॥
nyased upāri devasya yathā liṅgaṃ na pīḍyate ॥
piṇḍikāyāḥ prasaṅgena yadā sphuṭati mastake ॥
aśabdaṃ vā saśabdaṃ vā tadā rājā vinaśyati ॥
cakre tu sphuṭite liṅge yajamāno vinaśyati ॥
udare sphuṭite liṅge durbhikṣam upajāyate ॥
pṛṣṭhataḥ sphuṭite liṅge rājabhaṅgo bhaven nṛpa ॥
śakalaṃ muñcati yadā tadācāryo vinaśyati ॥
etannimittam anale śivenāṅgaiś ca mantravit ॥
ghṛtena sapta juhuyād āhutīs tatpraśāntaye ॥
tiryaggate ca calite skhalite namite ‘pi vā ॥
naṣṭasūtramakhedhvastā …. samāgate ॥
patrabhaṅge diśāṃ dāhe nirghātolkāṃ nipātane ॥
bhūbhṛtāṃ vadhāyāśu calane ketudarśane ॥
sphuṭe te vedikarmākhyāṃ liṅge vedicyute tathā ॥
pāṣāṇavṛṣṭipatane tathā sṛgvṛṣṭidarśane ॥
[118]        etāṃś cānyaṃś ca medhāvī vīkṣyotpātān samutthitān ॥
aghoreṇāhutiśataṃ juhuyāt tatpraśāntaye ॥
saṃsthāpya parameśānam evam utpātavarjitam ॥
pūjayed vidhivad bhūyaḥ sarvopakaraṇair vibhum ॥
sthāpayed vṛṣabhaṃ caiva śivasyābhimukhaṃ tataḥ ॥
pūjayed divyapuṣpaiś ca gandhaiś ca vidhipūrvakam ॥
sthāpayed dvārapālau ca vidhivat tadanantaram ॥
yajec candreśvaraṃ devaṃ yathāvac ca gajānanam ॥
umāṃ bhagavatīṃ durgāṃ tathā bhṛṅgiriṭiṃ nṛpaḥ ॥
ataḥ paraṃ caturthe ‘hni dvitīye vā samāhitaḥ ॥
tasmiṃś cāhany anudvignaś caturthīhomam ācaret ॥
lekhanābhyukṣaṇādyena karmaṇā vidhipūrvakam ॥
kṛtvā śivālayaṃ maunī paryukṣya juhuyāt tataḥ ॥
tribhir vyāhṛtibhiḥ pūrvaṃ hutvāgnaṃ manasā sakṛt ॥
huṃphaṭkārābhijaptena toyenābhyukṣya pāvakam ॥
āhutīḥ pañcagāyatryā juhuyāt pañca pañca ca ॥
ekaikāṃ pañcabhir dadyāt sakṛd vayāhṛtibhiḥ punaḥ ॥
pūrṇāhutiṃ śivenaikāṃ dattvopari yathāvidhi ॥
kṛtahomo 'gnim abhyarcya praṇipatya visarjayet ॥
kṛtakṛtyas tathā kartā prasannavadanekṣaṇaḥ ॥
pūrvaṃ mūrtidharān sarvān deśikāṃś ca samāgatān ॥
tataḥ pravrajitāṃś cānyān sagaṇā sarva .... kān ॥
[119]        vācakān gāyakāṃś caiva pūjayec chaktitaḥ kramāt ॥
iti mūrtidharaiḥ samyak deśikaiś ca samāgataiḥ ॥
sukhopaviṣṭam ācāryam arcayed vidhivan nṛpa ॥
suśuklair vastrayugalair maṇiratnādikuṇḍalaiḥ ॥
sauvarṇair upavītaiś ca kaṭakaiḥ kaṭisūtrakaiḥ ॥
hastyaśvarathavaryaiś ca gobhūmirajatādibhiḥ ॥
sahasradakṣiṇābhiś ca pūjayed vidhipūrvakam ॥
pūjite hi gurau rājan pratiṣṭhā saphalā bhavet ॥
saṃkṣepāt śṛṇu vipreśa pratiṣṭhām āditaḥ kramāt ॥
kṛtvā tu maṇḍapaṃ divyaṃ śāstroktaṃ supratiṣṭhitam ॥
dīpamālāparikṣiptaṃ dvāradāmopaśobhitam ॥
akālamūlān kalaśān dvāreṣu viniveśayet ॥
vastrapūtena toyena pūrayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
mantrapūtas tataḥ sarvān kurvīta sthāpakaḥ śuciḥ ॥
catuḥsamudraḥ mantreṇa jyeṣṭa ........ ti ca mantrayet ॥
caturbhiḥ sahire ……. kramād viprābhimantritaiḥ ॥
sarvāsu dikṣu ........ punar eva tu mantrayet ॥
kṛtalakṣma yadā liṅgam ānayed uttarāmukham ॥
bāhyamaṇḍapakair nyasya rakṣāṃ kurvīta yatnataḥ ॥
tato 'rcayet madhunā dadyād arghābhiṣecanam ॥
vāsāṃsi ca mahārhāṇi mantreṇa pratipādayet ॥
mantrī dīkṣā tapasostanūrasīti ॥
[120]        yātayāmāni vastrāṇi anyad eva ghṛtāni ca ॥
bhakṣitāny ākhubhir yāni na dadyāt tāni sambhave ॥
arghaṃ datvā ca namas tubhyaṃ samāhitaḥ ॥
tatas tv anyan mahad divyaṃ prāguktaṃ maṇḍapaṃ nayet ॥
śayyā tadvat prakartavyā śaileyeṣu viśeṣataḥ ॥
pūrvāgrām uttarāgrāṃ vā kuśaiḥ kṛtvā tu saṃstaram ॥
śobhanenaiva vastreṇa samantāt pariveṣṭayet ॥
prāsādābhimukhaṃ kṛtvā mantrapūrvam upānayet ॥
namaḥ svapadbhyo rudrebhyo jāgradbhayaś ca namo namaḥ ॥
audumbaraṃ tu kalaśaṃ pūrayitvā tu mantrataḥ ॥
śiraḥsthāne tu dātavyo devasya tveti suvrataḥ ॥
audumbaraṃ tāmramayam ॥
tāmrapātraṃ tato deyaṃ pādasthāne viśeṣataḥ ॥
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ datvā mantram etam udāharet ॥
'abhitvā śūra nonuma' iti ॥
vidvān pratisarāṃs tatra dadyāt siddhārthakān śubhān ॥
'pratisaro' mahatsūtraṃ, kaṅkaṇam iti prasiddham ॥
śvetāḥ sumanaso datvā dhūpaṃ dadyāc caturdiśam ॥
ātmano dakṣiṇe pārśve mantram etam udāharet ॥
'āpyāyasva' 'same tu te' 'viśvataḥ' 'somavṛtyayā ' |
tataḥ sarvaprayatnena śayyāṃ mantreṇa dāpayet ॥
[121]        chatraṃ patākayopetaṃ mantrapūrvaṃ tu dāpayet ॥
‘bṛhaspateś chandirasī 'ti  ॥
virūpaṃ janayitvā tu homaṃ śāntikam ācaret ॥
tapaś ca tejaś ca śraddhā ceti śamīpalāśanyagrodhāś ca  ॥
audumbarasahasrāṇi paśca hutvā ghṛtaplutāḥ ॥
mahāvyāhṛtihomena juhuyāt sthāpakaḥ śuciḥ ॥
samyak hutvā tu devasya mūrddhani saṃspṛśet tadā ॥
kuśodakaṃ gṛhītvā tu pratimantraṃ vidhānavit ॥
āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa tridhā hi prakṣipej jalam ॥
bhakṣyabhojyopahāraiś ca baliṃ kṛtvā samāhitaḥ ॥
prakīrṇa .... dātavyo devadevasya saṃnidhau ॥
'tryambakaṃ yajāmahe' iti prakīrṇā dātavyā bhūtebhyo mantrapūrvakam ॥
sarvatantrabaliṃ kṛtvā datvā tebhyaś ca dakṣiṇām ॥
'        nīlarudrāṃ baliṃ kṛtvā dikṣu sarvāsu nikṣipet ॥
vidiśāsv api sarvāsu brāhmaṇāṃś ca prapūjya tu ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu śaivān pāśupatāṃs tathā ॥
gṛhasthāṃś ca tataḥ śayyāṃ datvā tebhyaś ca dakṣiṇām ॥
nīlarudrāṃ japet tatra nīlasūktaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
atharvaśirasaṃ caiva sarvotpātapraśāntaye ॥
rudrāṃ puruṣasūktāṃśca ślokādhyāyaṃ saśukriyam ॥
[122]        brahmāṇaṃ sūryadevatyam adhvaryur dakṣiṇe japet ॥
devavrataṃ sagāyatraṃ jyeṣṭhasāmarathantaram ॥
vāmadevaṃ tathā sūktaṃ chandogo hy uttare japet ॥
sthāpakas tu tataḥ sādhyaṃ sāma .... vā ॥
japatas tasya viprasya na doṣaḥ sthāpane bhavet ॥
vāmadevaṃ tathā raudraṃ sā …... dhruvaṃ smṛtam ॥
gāyakair nartakaiś caiva rātrau kuryān mahotsavam ॥
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā devadevādhivāsane ॥
adhivāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā devaṃ veśma praveśayet ॥
pātayitvā tu sūtreṇa parimṛjya gṛhāṇi ca ॥
gṛhāṇi tu samāsādya sūtracchedaṃ tu lakṣayet ॥
homaśāntiḥ prakartavyāḥ sūtracchede diśaṃ prati ॥
dvihastaṃ ca trihastaṃ vā kṛtvā śvabhrasya vistaram ॥
jñātvā pramāṇaṃ liṅgasya tataḥ śvabhraṃ tu kārayet ॥
supakvābhir iṣṭakābhiḥ śvabhraṃ badhnīta vai śubham ॥
kuśodakena pūtena gomūtreṇa ca śodhayet ॥
śodhayitvā yathānyāyaṃ pāyasena tu lepayet ॥
aklinnena supakvena naivedyaṃ rudano [taṇḍulodbhavam] ॥
nikṣipet vārimantreṇa pāyasaṃ brahmavittamaḥ ॥
gauro yaḥ puruṣaḥ sarvoṣadhimayo mataḥ ॥
[123]        nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
nīlaṃ gauravatāṃ brahma nairṛtyāṃ vinikṣipet ॥
īśānaḥ puruṣaḥ śuklaḥ sarvavidyādhipo mahān ॥
gandharvasevitaś caiva tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
sarvabījāni gandhāś ca gairikaṃ kāñcanaṃ vṛṣam ॥
padmakena tu saṃyuktaṃ brahmasthāne niveśayet ॥
padmayoniś caturmūrtir vedavāsāḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
yajñaṃ rakṣati yo nityaṃ tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
mantraḥ ॥
yadi na syād yathoktaṃ tu maṇiratnaṃ yathā diśam ॥
suvarṇaṃ tatra dātavyaṃ viduṣā siddhim icchatā ॥
śabdebhyo namaḥ ॥ viśvedevebhyo namaḥ ॥
gandharvāya namaḥ ॥ prajāpataye namaḥ ॥
‘brahma jajñānaṃ prathamam’iti mantram ॥
paṭena mahatā caiva samantāt pariveṣṭayet ॥
surakṣitaṃ tu kartavyaṃ vighnādy atra bhavanti vai ॥
samāpte tu tathā home mantreṇotthāpayec chivam ॥
mantraḥ ॥ ‘uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate’ ॥
snapanaṃ ca prakurvīta vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
punar anyāni vāsāṃsi dadyāt prayatamānasaḥ ॥
liṅgaṃ sucitritaṃ kṛtvā rajjubhiś ca yathāvidhi ॥
śucibhiḥ brāhmaṇair vṛddhaiḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ ॥
[124]        pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā devadevaṃ praveśayet ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca brahmaghoṣaiś ca puṣkalaiḥ ॥
dvārasthāne tu devasya arghaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥
praveśayet tataḥ kṣipraṃ mantram enam udāharet ॥
‘viśvaprabhavasī’tīti ॥
rudrapīṭhe tato 'nyasya punaḥ sūtrāṇi lakṣayet ॥
āśritaṃ kiñcid īśānaṃ maṅgalārthaṃ bhavet punaḥ ॥
pāyasenātha madhunā mūlaṃ devasya lepayet ॥
‘madhuvātā' ity anena mantreṇa nimittam aśubhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kiñcitkālaṃ vidhārayet  ॥
āvāhayet tato devam arghaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥
yasya siṃhā rathe yuktā vyāghrabhūtagaṇās tathā ॥
ṛṣayo lokapālāś ca devau skandabṛhaspatī ॥
gaṇāś ca mātaraś caiva viṣṇuḥ somaḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
nāgā yakṣāś ca gandharvā ye tu devā nabhaścarāḥ ॥
āhvaye tryakṣam īśānaṃ śipiviṣṭaṃ satīpatim ॥
āgaccha bhagavan deva lokānugrahakāmyayā ॥
idam āsanam āsādya brahmaṇā nirmitaṃ tava ॥
‘śivo bhava viśvo bhava dhruvārthaṃ mayāsī’ti pratiṣṭhāpayet ॥
dhruvā dyaur dhruvā pṛthvī dhruvā ime parvatāḥ  ॥ ॥
[125]        dhruvo yajño yajamāno dhruvo rājā dhruvaṃ viśvam iti namo namaḥ ॥
sthāpayen niṣprakampaṃ ca niḥśabdaṃ niścalaṃ tathā ॥
sthāpyamānaṃ yadā liṅgaṃ kampate calate 'pi vā ॥
tadā śāntiḥ prakartavyā pauṣṭikī nābhicāriṇī ॥
diśāṃ tu devam uddiśya diśāsu calanena vā ॥
aindrī ca kārayec chāntiṃ dadyāc ca vṛṣabhaṃ śivam ॥
yāmyāṃ ca kārayec chāntiṃ dadyād aśvaṃ ca dakṣiṇām ॥
vāruṇīṃ kārayec chāntiṃ dadyād viprāya vāsasī ॥
saumyāṃ ca kārayec chāntiṃ dhenuṃ dadyād dvijātaye ॥
yasyāṃ yasyāṃ caled devas tasyāṃ śāntiṃ tu kārayet ॥
āvāhayet tato devam arghaṃ datvā yathāvidhi ॥
āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi akṣatādadhitaṇḍulāḥ ॥
tilāḥ siddhārthakāś caiva aṣṭāṅgo ‘rghaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
paścagavyaṃ tataś caiva viprasaṃsarṣapaṃ ……. ॥
raktodakaṃ gandhavāri kuśodakaphalodakam ॥
apāmārjanakaṃ vāpi kuryāt tatra vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
snāpayet saṃhitāmantraiḥ sarvair eva samāhitaḥ ॥
snānasyāvāhanamantraḥ ॥
yasya siṃhā rathe yuktā vyāghrā yasyānugāminaḥ ॥
tam ahaṃ tryakṣam īśanaṃ rudram āvāhayāmy aham ॥
[126]        āgacchāgaccha bhagavan sarvagaṇaparivārasapārṣadā ॥
svāgataṃ bhagavate mahādevāya rudrāya sapārṣadāya namo namaḥ ॥
imā āpaḥ śivāḥ śivatamāḥ śāntāḥ śāntatamā amṛtā amṛtatamāḥ ॥
anena mantreṇārghaṃ dadyāt  ॥
‘namas te 'stu mā mā hiṃsīḥ śivo bhava sumanā bhava' iti pañcagavyamantraḥ ॥
'pañcanadyaḥ sarasvatīty evamāditaḥ ॥ ghṛtamantraḥ 'tejo ‘si śukram asī'ty evamādiḥ ॥
'sāvitryā kṣīram’ abhimantrayet ॥
'pāvamāna: 'sodyaṃ' iti devasyodvartanaṃ smṛtam ॥
ratnodakamantraḥ 'hiraṇyagarbha' ityevamādiḥ ॥
upamānikamantraḥ 'śanno devīr abhiṣṭaye' ityevamādiḥ ॥
śāntyudakaṃ prakartavyaṃ hutāgnau vidhivad dvijaiḥ ॥
‘āśuḥ śiśāno vṛṣabha’ ityevamādiḥ ॥
taijasāny arghapātrāṇi mṛṇmayāni na dāpayet ॥
sauvarṇaṃ vā bhavet sarvaṃ raupyaṃ vā tāmram eva ca ॥
anyathā kriyamāṇe tu viparītaphalaṃ bhavet ॥
susnāteti dvijā brūyuḥ sarve ca vratinas tathā ॥
gandhapuṣpāṇi deyāni dhūpo deyas tathaiva ca ॥
dīpān prajvālya mantreṇa nāma kiñcid udāharet ॥
dravyahīnaṃ na kartavyaṃ mantrahīnaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
ity eṣā tu śubhā pūjā pūrvā surabhigandhinī ॥
[127]        pūjā brahmapavitreṇa pratigṛhṇīṣva śaṅkaraḥ ॥
vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyo mama suvrata ॥
āhāraḥ sarvadevānāṃ gugguluḥ pratigṛhyatām ॥
agnir jyotiś ca śukraś ca mohapūrvo vibhāvasuḥ ॥
prabhākaro mahātejā dīpo 'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥
tataś caturvidhair vastrair yugmair yugmaiś ca chādayet ॥
pūrveṇa paṭṭajaṃ dadyād romajaṃ dakṣiṇena tu ॥
paścimena tu kārpāsam uttareṇa tu valkajam ॥
'rociṣṇur asī'ty ādarśaṃ darśayet ॥
naivedyaṃ cāsya dātavyaṃ saghṛtaṃ pāyasaṃ śubham ॥
'devadevabodha' mantreṇa ॥
vividhaṃ ca baliṃ datvā tarpayed bhūtajaṃ gaṇam ॥
gandhamālyaiś ca mantraiś ca pūjayitvā dvijottamān ॥
śāntyudakaṃ ca gṛhṇīyād brāhmaṇebhyaś ca kāmataḥ ॥
caṇḍeśvarāya yajñas tu kartavyas tadanantaram ॥
śayanāsanavastraiś ca pūjayet sthāpakaṃ svayam ॥
pūjayālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā tato deyā ca dakṣiṇā ॥
ibhaṃ madagalaṃ dadyād aśvaṃ vā hemabhūṣitam ॥
grāmaṃ cāpi suniṣpannaṃ yena vāstuspater guruḥ ॥
anyathā kriyamāṇena puṇyaṃ sthāpakam āśrayet ॥
[128]        tava prasādād viprendra duṣprāpaṃ prāptavān aham ॥
praṇamya śirasā brūyāt prasīdeti punaḥ punaḥ ॥
adhivāsanavastrāṇi śayyā snānaṃ vitānakam ॥
kalaśāś cārghapātrāṇi sarvāṇi sthāpakasya tu ॥
pratiṣṭhāpanayed dattaṃ yāvad daśamikā bhavet ॥
sthāpakasyānumodena (?) nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ॥
‘oṃ namo maheśvarāya svāhā mahāpuruṣāya svāhā’ ityanena mantreṇa liṅgamantraṃ japet ॥
caturthikāṃ tataḥ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
puṣpais tu vividhākāraiḥ kārayet piṇḍikārhaṇam ॥
vividhaṃ caiva kartavyaṃ bhojanaṃ [ tat ] dinaṃ prati ॥
eṣa dharmavidhiḥ kāryaḥ prāpte tu daśame 'hani ॥
varddhamānaṃ prakurvīta māsaṣāṇmāsikaṃ tathā ॥
lepanair vividhākāraiḥ kuryād vai devapūjanam ॥
pratimāsv api vijñeya eṣa eva vidhiḥ sadā ॥
mahākālādisarvāsu ardhanārīśvarasya ca ॥
īśvara uvāca ॥
vidhānena yathoktena ye me liṅgaṃ praropayet ॥
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi rudraloke mahīyate ॥
śūraś ca bahubhṛtyasya brāhmaṇo matparāyaṇaḥ ॥
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ purāṇāyataneṣv api ॥
brahmovāca ॥
śaileyeṣu vidhiḥ prokto yathā bhagavatas tvayā ॥
[129]        sauvarṇarajatānāṃ ca vaktum arhasy aśeṣataḥ ॥
īśvara uvāca ॥
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ brahman tāmraṃ vaiḍūryam eva ca ॥
yathotpannāni sthānāni nātra kālo vidhīyate ॥
prakṣipet tāmraratnānāṃ bījāni ca viśeṣataḥ ॥
na hi ratneṣu ratnānāṃ prakṣepo dṛśyate kvacit  ॥ ॥
dhātunā yena yal liṅgaṃ kuryād bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ ॥
tasya tenaiva yojyā tu piṇḍikā śubhalakṣaṇā ॥
madhukaṃ kāñcanaṃ deyaṃ vipulaṃ phalam icchatā ॥
āyuṣkāmeṇa putrāṇāṃ kulasya ca vivṛddhaye ॥
raupye ca candanaṃ datvā duḥkhaṃ jayati sarvataḥ ॥
ratnāni dāpayec chaile dhīnabījaṃ [?] ca kṛtsnaśaḥ ॥
prayatnena prakurvīta śailaje tu viśeṣataḥ ॥
atha sarvasamaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ yadā madhyaṃ varapradam ॥
adhastād vipule putrā uddhṛte bahavaḥ striyaḥ  ॥ ॥
adho vakre tataḥ putraṃ madhyavakre tato dhanam ॥
carmapāṃśukapāleṣu [tuṣabhasmā]sthiromasu ॥
śayyāsu viparītāsu sthāpayec cābhikārikam ॥
kṛṣṇāya sārakūpaś ca padmalohamayāś ca ye ॥
khalvevācaṃ [?]viticicya sthāpayed vidhivad dvijaḥ ॥
pātakā sthāpayet kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśī ॥
[130]        kalaśāni ca śaktiś ca kartavyaṃ viprabhojanam ॥
sidhyanti sarvakarmāṇi viprā yais tu prapūjitāḥ ॥
samidbhī rudhirāktābhiḥ khādirībhir viśeṣataḥ ॥
evam etāni jānīyād ābhicārikapauṣṭikam ॥
puruṣāsthikapāleṣu sthāpayen na kadācana ॥
śatravas tena varddhante dvau māsau ca bhayaṃ bhavet ॥
pāśaṃ tu puṣṭikāmasya liṅgaṃ śāntikaraṃ bhavet ॥
sarvabījāni prakṣipya māsād ūrdhvāṃś ca dhārayet ॥
pramādāt tu bhavet trāsaḥ prayatnāc ca śivaṃ bhavet ॥
sukhāni trīṇi yūpasya caturthaṃ nopalabhyate ॥
avocanābhya ( ? ) karmāṇi anāmāni tu yāni vai ॥
adhastād upariṣṭāc ca madhye cāpi vinirdiśet ॥
sthāpako hatadoṣas tu sopadeśaḥ samāhitaḥ ॥
sasuvarṇena hastena ratnādīni vinikṣipet ॥
vajraṃ ca haritālaṃ ca vrīhīṃ vā māṣam eva ca ॥
tābhyāṃ vinikṣipet samyag evaṃ śāntiḥ kṛtā bhavet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
puruṣāṇāṃ varaḥ śrīmān sarvadevādhipo mahān ॥
vajrahasto mahāsattvas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
muktā manaḥśilāyuktaṃ grāmāraṇyakayācakān ॥
[131]        maṇinyā nikṣiped vidvān navātrā [?] toyaṃ dadhiḥ ॥
mantraḥ ॥
āgneyaḥ puruṣo raktaḥ sarvadevamayo 'vyayaḥ ॥
sadhūmrākṣas tv anādhṛṣyas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
mudgāgodhumakāś caiva .... gṛṃjavāṃjanaṃ ( ! ) tathā ॥
yāmyāyām apramatto 'tha nikṣiped īpsitārthadaḥ ॥
mantraḥ ॥
yāmyāṃ nīlotpalābho yaḥ puruṣo ghoradaṇḍabhṛt ॥
karmasākṣī viśuddhātmā tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
uttilo ‘yaṃ tilāś caiva mukhaṃ śyāmākam eva ca ॥
naiṛtyāṃ diśi dātavyaṃ sarvasiddhiprasādhakam ॥
mantraḥ ॥
varuṇaḥ śabalo vipraḥ puruṣo nimnagādhipaḥ ॥
pāśahasto vimītātmā tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
godhūmaḥ sarṣapaś caiva candrakāntamaṇis tathā ॥
[132]        saurāṣṭrakāyā tikṣiped vicakṣaṇaḥ (?) ॥
pramāṇayuktaṃ kurvīta tarpayed vidhivad dvijān ॥
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena pramāṇasvaṃ tu kārayet ॥
īdṛśe sthāpite yūpe rājā rāṣṭraṃ vivardhate ॥
eṣa evaṃvidho yūpo mayā proktas tavānagha ॥
eteṣām ekam āśritya yathākālaṃ yajasva mām ॥
atha snānavidhiṃ caturvidham anūpamam ॥
ādyaṃ pādyaṃ mārjanaṃ ca tathopamānikaṃ param ॥
vāsāṃsi kalaśā deyāḥ kaṣāyāmṛttikās tathā ॥
caturvidhāni caitāni sarvāṇy eva bhavanti hi ॥
etaiḥ snānavidhiḥ kārya īśvarasya mahātmanaḥ ॥
liṅgasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ brāhmaṇair vedapāragaiḥ ॥
caturvidhaṃ tu sampūrṇā cāturmāsika āgate ॥
ṣāṇmāsike tu kalpānte varddhamāne prajāyate ॥
ānupūrveṇa kartavyaṃ krameṇa vidhinā vibho ॥
caturvidhaṃ yad uktaṃ hi vādyaṃ tad idam ucyate ॥
ghanaṃ vitatamānārthaṃ caturthaṃ sukhinaṃ param ॥
caturvidhaṃ cārghapātram arghādyaṃ ca pradāpayet ॥
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ caturthaṃ kāṃsyam ucyate ॥
sauvarṇādīnām alābhe dhātukaṃ parikalpayet ॥
kṛtvā tu tatra bījāni arhhaṃ dadyāt prajāpate ॥
[133]        āpo dadhi payo darbhāḥ śuktāḥ siddhārthakā vibho ॥
parāḥ kṛṣṇaḥ tilā gandhās taṇḍulāś cākṣatā vibho ॥
puṣpāṇi ca sugandhīni phalāni vividhāni ca ॥
paridhūpya gṛhaṃ datvā āpohiṣṭhābhimantritam ॥
vāruṇena tu mantreṇa tat paścād abhimantrayet ॥
atha caturvarṇāṃ vedānām abhimantrayet  ॥ 'kayānaś citre'ti cābhimantrayet  ॥ oṃkārapūrvābhir mahāvyāhṛtibhir abhimantrya brahmaśilām upanayet  ॥ bhagavato maheśvarasya cārgham iti paścimaṃ pūrvottarāyāṃ diśi samupanayet  ॥ dakṣiṇato rudrādhipataye namaḥ  ॥ tam evārghaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya devasyopari dāpayet  ॥ arghais tu dattair bhavet  ॥ tathā caturvidhā upasānikā bhavanti ॥ tathā śuklā raktā pītā kṛṣṇā ceti  ॥ atha caturvidhā mṛttikopamānikāś ca bhavanti  ॥ tad yathā ॥ pārvatī nādeyī vālmīkā kuśastambamṛttikāś ceti  ॥ kuśodakena pradakṣiṇam uttare visarjayet ॥
atha caturvidhāḥ snehāḥ snapane bhavanti  ॥ pañcagavyaṃ prathamam  ॥ dvitīyaṃ kṣīram  ॥ tṛtīyaṃ dadhi  ॥ caturtha ghṛtam iti ॥ pañcamam akṣatodakam  ॥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ phalodakam  ॥ saptamaṃ ratnodakam  ॥ aṣṭamaṃ gandhodakam  ॥ tatra prathamaṃ pañcagavyena snapayet  ॥ paścād adbhiḥ snapayitvā ghṛtena snapayet  ॥ ghṛtena snapayitvodvarttayet tato daghnā snapayitvā svanāmnā snāpayet  ॥ kāṣāyair udvartayet ॥ caturvidhāḥ kāṣāyāḥ  ॥ plakṣanyagrodhamadhukaśirīṣādyair udvartyāmbhasā snāpayet  ॥ tatra āpaś caturvidhā bhavanti  ॥ tad yathā  ॥ nadyas taḍāgaḥ satsaraḥ palvalāś ceti  ॥ sarṣapakaṣāyacūrṇenodvartayet  ॥ sarvauṣadhiphalāni bhavanti  ॥ tatra caturvidhāḥ sauvarṇāś catvāro rājatāś catvāras tāmrajāś catvāraḥ kāṃsyamayāś catvāraḥ iti ॥ tatra prathame kalaśe pañcagavyaṃ kṣipet  ॥ [134] dvitīye kṣīram  ॥ tṛtīye dadhi  ॥ caturthe ghṛtam  ॥ pañcame 'kṣatodakam  ॥ ṣaṣṭhe phalodakam  ॥ saptame ratnodakam  ॥ aṣṭame gandhodakam  ॥ avaram ahataṃ vāso deyaṃ snāpayitvā gandhapuṣpaiś cābhyarcayet ॥ tatra gandhāḥ  ॥ tagaramustakahrīve verāguruturuṣkacandanaśarasamūhagandhāḥ  ॥ atha puṣpāṇi bhavanti  ॥
tad yathā ॥ tilakārka-kurabaka-dhattūra-karavīrotpala-campaka-punnāga-nāgakesarakundādīnāṃ śatasahasraṃ gandhapuṣpaiś cābhyarcya dhūpagrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā nivarttayet  ॥ tilataṇduladadhyakṣatair balim upaharet ॥ atha caturvidhāni vāsāṃsi bhavanti ॥ tad yathoṇḍajaṃ voṇajaṃ valkalajaṃ lomajam iti ॥ tatrāṇḍajaṃ kauśeyaṃ voṇajaṃ kārpāsikam valkalajaṃ paṭṭajaṃ lomajam āvikam iti ॥ tatra mūrtiṣu aṇḍajaṃ dadyāt ॥ dakṣiṇena tilalomajam ॥ paścime voṇajam ॥ devadeveśvarāya uttare valkalajaṃ dadyāt ॥ atha caturvidhāś caravo bhavanti ॥ tatra pūrvo mūrtyāṃ yāvakam  ॥ dakṣiṇamūrtyāṃ kṛśaram  ॥ paścimamūrtyāṃ pāyasam  ॥ uttaramūrtyāṃ dadhyodanāni ॥ evaṃ kṛte sadā sampūrṇaphalam āpnoti  ॥ yatra jāyate gaṇādhipatyam avāpnoti ॥ sarpisnānenāśvamedhaṃ, gomūtreṇa rājasūyaṃ, gomayena gomedhaṃ, dadhnā puṇḍarīkaṃ, kṣīreṇa sautrāmaṇiṃ, dadhyudakenātirātraṃ, vājapeyaphalaṃ ॥
tathā ॥
ambhasā divaṃ śreṣṭhānāṃ kratūnāṃ phalam āpnuyāt  ॥
vistareṇa mayā proktaṃ tava brahman samantataḥ ॥
liṅgapratiṣṭhāpanavidhau snapanasyottamo vidhiḥ ॥
tathā ॥
liṅgam iṣṭaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sarvayatnair vidhānataḥ ॥
yat puṇyaphalam āpnoti tad ekāgramanāḥ śraṇu ॥
sarvayajñatapodānatīrthavedeṣu yat phalam ॥
tat phalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ sthāpya liṅgaṃ samāpnuyāt ॥
liṅgaṃ yaḥ sthāpayed ekaṃ vidhipūrvaṃ sadakṣiṇam ॥
[135]        sarvam evoditaṃ puṇyaṃ koṭikoṭiguṇaṃ bhavet ॥
mātrikaṃ pitrikaṃ caiva yatra vovo.... kastriyaḥ ( ? ) ॥
kulaikaviṃśam uttārya rudraloke mahīyate ॥
bhuktvā tu vipulān bhogān pralaye samupasthite ॥
jñānayogaṃ samāsādya tatraiva ca vimucyate ॥
sarvatīrthamayaṃ liṅgaṃ śāṅkaraṃ tattvasāgaram ॥
darśanāt sparśanād eva kṛtsnam enaḥ kṣayaṃ nayet ॥
sthāpanād brahmahatyāś ca bhrūṇahatyāṃ tathaiva ca ॥
mahāpāpāni cānyāni kṣaṇoti śivatejasā ॥
[136]
atha brahmapratiṣṭhā ॥ 12 ॥
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe ॥
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi adhivāsanam uttamam ॥
yajño brahmamayo hy eṣa sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ ॥
prāsādasthāpanaṃ kuryāt sthaṇḍilaṃ nātidūrataḥ ॥
daśahastaparīṇāhaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ ॥
caturbhis toraṇair yuktaṃ parikṣiptaṃ kuśais tathā ॥
tatropari vitānāṃś ca diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ॥
brahmasthāne tu navamīṃ sadā kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
nadīsaṅgamatīrthāc ca sikatās tatra dāpayet ॥
alaṅkṛtaṃ gandhapuṣpair ucchritadhvajaśobhitam ॥
akṛṣṇamūlakān kalaśān saṅkhyayā śatasaṃmitān ॥
vastrapūtena toyena pūrayec ca vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
mantrapūtāṃs tataḥ sarvān kurvīta sthāpakaḥ śuciḥ ॥
mantraḥ ॥
catuḥsamudrajyeṣṭhayety anena mantreṇābhimantrayet ॥ tathā brahmadevatya ṛgbhiś ca tatsaṅkhyābhir abhimantrayet ॥
[137]        pūrvottaradiśābhāge tathānyaṃ maṇḍapaṃ vibho ॥
kuryāt kanīyasaṃ divyaṃ dhvajamālākulaṃ śubham ॥
tatrāpi maṇḍape kāryāḥ kalaśāṣṭau suśobhanāḥ ॥
evam aṣṭottaraśataṃ kalaśānāṃ suśobhanam ॥
dadhinānvitaṃ sarvaṃ ratnagarbhaṃ tathā vibho ॥
kṛtvādhivāsaṃ vidhivat tato devaṃ samānayet ॥
kṛtvā lakṣaṃ yadā devam ānayed uttarāmukham ॥
bāhyamaṇḍapake sthāpya rakṣāṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ ॥
ahaṃ parastād anena mantreṇa rakṣāṃ kurvīta ॥
śubhapīṭhagataṃ kṛtvā sthāpayet taṃ yathāvidhi ॥
caturvedavidaṃ vipraṃ sthāpakaṃ tatra nirdiśet ॥
dvitīyo bhojakas tadvat sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ ॥
bahvṛcādhvaryavo viprāś (?) chandogātharvaṇāś ca ye ॥
eṣāṃ mantreṇa saṃsthāpyo bhagavān padmasambhavaḥ ॥
āpohiṣṭheti vai mantrān japamānair narādhipa ॥
tūryaśaṅkhaninādaiś ca gaṇikānartakais tathā ॥
vedapuṇyāhaśabdaiś ca nānātīrthodakair nṛpa ॥
upahāraiś ca vividhair gandhamālyodakais tathā ॥
nadīsaṅgamadivyāṃś ca valmīkād āhṛtāṃs tathā ॥
dadyād devasya śirasi mṛttikāṃ pītavarṇinīm ॥
aśvakānteti vai dadyān mantrair mantravidāṃ varaḥ ॥
sarvoṣadhīnāmayuktāṃ sadūrvāśamipatrikām ॥
[138]        sthāpakā brāhmaṇāḥ sarve bhojakā viprakanyakāḥ ॥
snāpayanti tato devaṃ vidhivad bharatarṣabha ॥
vāruṇaṃ japato mantraṃ tīrthanāmāni vā vibho ॥
anena vidhinā snāpya gṛhītvā śaṅkham uttamam ॥
śanno devīti japan payasā pūrayed budhaḥ ॥
mantreṇālabhya tacchiras tato mūrdhni jalaṃ kṣipet ॥
pṛthivī te pātram anena labhya śiraḥ ॥
idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrameti kṣipej jalam ॥
śanno devīti mantreṇa punaḥ snānaṃ pravarttayet ॥
sauvarṇaṃ pātraṃ saṃskṛtaṃ ghṛtaṃ gavyaṃ ca dīyate ॥
yātayāmāni vastrāṇi anyad eva dhṛtāni ca ॥
tathā vā nīlaraktāni na dadyād āvikaṃ - le ॥
dadyān mālyāni deveśa devadevasya mūrddhani ॥
'śrīś ca te lakṣmīś ca te' mantreṇārghaṃ datvā namaskṛtya samāhitaḥ ॥
tatas tv anyan mahad divyaṃ prāguktaṃ maṇḍapaṃ nyaset ॥
dātavyā śobhanā śayyā śaileyeṣu viśeṣataḥ ॥
pūrvāgram uttarāgraṃ vā kuśair kṛtvā tu saṃstaram ॥
śobhaneneha vastreṇa samantāt pariveṣṭayet ॥
savastrakuśastāṃmbair (?) divyamālyaiḥ prayojayet ॥
prāsādābhimukho bhūtvā mantrapūrṇasvaro bhavet ॥
[139]        ‘namo svapadbhyo jāgradbhyaś ca vo’ mantreṇeti ॥
pūrayitvā tu kalaśam ekaṃ tāmraṃ susaṃskṛtam ॥
śiraḥsthāne tu dātavyaṃ 'devasya tveti' bhārata ॥
tāmrapātraṃ tato deyaṃ śiraḥsthāne viśeṣataḥ ॥
kṣīrakṣaudre tato datvā mantram etam udīrayet ॥
'abhi tvā śūra nonuma' iti  ॥
śvetāḥ sumanaso datvā dhūpaṃ datvā caturdiśam ॥
paṭena mahatā caiva samantāt pariveṣṭayet ॥
ātmano dakṣiṇe pārśve mantram etam udāharet ॥
‘āpyāyasva sametu te viśvataḥ vṛṣṇam’ iti ॥
tataḥ sarvaprayatnena śayyām atra praveśayet ॥
‘bṛhaspatecchadiras’ityanena mantreṇābhimantrayet  ॥ śayyām iti ॥
brahmasūktaṃ tato japtvā homaṃ śāntiṃ (?) ca kārayet ॥
‘brahmasūktaṃ’ ‘brahmajajñānam’ ityādi  ॥
śamīpalāśanyagrodha aśvatthodumbaras tathā ॥
sahasrāṇi tu vai trīṇi śāntiṃ kṛtvā ghaṭotkaṭam ॥
mahāvyāhṛtimantreṇa juhuyāt sthāpako dvijaḥ ॥
caturṣv api diśāsv evaṃ agnikuṇḍāni kārayet ॥
bahuvṛcādhvaryuchandogātharvaṇāś ca dvijottamāḥ ॥
homaṃ kurvanti rājendra prāsādasthā mahīpate ॥
evaṃ hutvā tu rājendra pāvakaṃ mūrdhni saṃspṛśet ॥
kuśodakaṃ gṛhītvā tu abhimantraya vidhānavit ॥
āpo hi ṣṭheti mantreṇa triḥ kṛtvā prakṣipej jalam ॥
[140]        bhakṣabhojyopahāraiś ca baliṃ dadyāt samāhitaḥ ॥
prakīrṇā aprakīrṇā ca dātavyā devasaṃnidhau ॥
sarvatas tu baliṃ datvā dikṣu caiva punaḥ kṣipet ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvāṣṭau haviṣyaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ ॥
tebhyo vilepanaṃ dadyāc candanena sugandhinā ॥
dakṣiṇā ca yathāśakti prīyatāṃ me pitāmahaḥ ॥
santu devāḥ sadā prītāḥ siddhāś caiva sacāraṇāḥ ॥
gandharvāpsarasaḥ sarve gaṇā vidyādharās tathā ॥
saptarṣayo vālakhilyā devā devarṣayas tathā ॥
nīlarudrān japet tatra brahmasūktaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
atharvaśirasaṃ caiva sarvotpātapraśāntaye ॥
ślokādhyāyaṃ tu rudriyaṃ brāhmaṇamaitreṇa maitraṃ cādhvaryur dakṣiṇe japet ॥
devavrataṃ pauruṣaṃ ca jyeṣṭhaṃ sāmarathantaram ॥
vāmadevyaṃ tathā sūktaṃ chandogaḥ paścime japet ॥
sthāpako 'pi tataḥ sādhyaṃ vikarṇaṃ duṣṭaśāntaye ॥
japatas tasya viprasya na doṣaḥ sthāpane bhavet ॥
gāyanair nartakaiś caiva tatra kuryān mahotsavam ॥
devasya purataḥ kāryaṃ puṇyaṃ pustakavācanam ॥
brahmaghoṣas tu kartavyo diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ॥
bahuvṛcādidvijaiḥ sarvarākṣasāsuraśāntaye ॥
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā evam evādhivāsayet ॥
adhivāsanaṃ tu vidhivat kṛtvā vai brahmaṇo nṛpa ॥
[141]        aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ॥
kṛtvādhivāsanaṃ deve evaṃ bhaktyā vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
kṛtvāgnikāryaṃ vidhivat tato brāhmaṇabhojanam ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu divyaṃ tauśāśca (?) śaktitaḥ ॥
devaṃ prati tato gacched brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhārata ॥
gṛhītvā devadeveśa tato devālayaṃ viśet ॥
śaṅkhapūrṇaninādaiś ca gītaśabdaiś ca puṣkalaiḥ ॥
puṇyāhaśabdaghoṣaiś ca maṅgalānāñ ca nisvanaiḥ ॥
jñātvā pramāṇaṃ devasya piṇḍikaśvabhram ācaret ॥
pakvābhir iṣṭikābhiś ca śvabhraṃ badhnīta ve śubham ॥
dvāravedhaṃ na kurvīta caturasraṃ samantataḥ ॥
kuśodakena pūtena gomūtreṇa ca śodhayet ॥
śodhayitvā yathānyāyaṃ gomayenopalepayet ॥
aklinnena supakvena navataṇḍulayena vai ॥
goro yaḥ puruṣaḥ saumyaḥ sarvauṣadhimayo 'mṛtaḥ ॥
nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
anena mantreṇa lepayed iti ॥
sarvabījāni gandhāṃś ca rocanaṃ kāñcanaṃ śubham ॥
padmarāgeṇa saṃyuktaṃ brahmasthāne niveśayet ॥
mantras cātra bhavati ॥
padmayoniś caturmūrtir vedavāsāḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
yajñarakṣaḥ ca [vai nityaṃ] tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
[142]        na bījagāḍhaṃ kurvīta maṅgalārthaṃ tu dāpayet ॥
aindrāgneyadiśor madhye kāñcanaṃ ca pradāpayet ॥
āgneyīyāmyayor madhye dadyād upalakaṃ maṇim ॥
madhyaṃ ca yāmyanairṛtyoḥ pravālaṃ tu pradāpayet ॥
madhye nairṛtavāruṇyor [?] [vīra] tāmraṃ vinirdiśet ॥
vāyuvāruṇayor madhye mauktikaṃ tu pradāpayet ॥
saumyavāruṇayor madhye maṇiś ca vimalaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
saumyeśānayor madhye dadyāc candanapūrvakam ॥
na saṃskāreṇa dātavyo mantras tatra na vidyate ॥
yadi na syād yathoktaṃ tu maṇiratnaṃ yathādiśam ॥
suvarṇaṃ tatra dātavyaṃ viduṣā siddhim icchatā  ॥ ॥
mantrāś cātra bhavanti  ॥
viśvedevebhyaḥ gandharvebhyo namaḥ ॥ prajāpataye namaḥ ॥
'brahmajajñānam’ iti śvabhram abhimantrayet  ॥ paṭena mahatā samantāt pariveṣṭayet ॥
surakṣitaṃ tu kartavyaṃ vighnāny atra bhavanti hi ॥
tatas tu sthāpayed devaṃ mantrato nalinodbhavam ॥
mantraḥ ॥
'uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate' ॥
snāpanaṃ tu prakurvīta vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
punar anyāni vāsāṃsi dadyāt prayatamānasaḥ ॥
devaṃ tu yantritaṃ kṛtvā rajjubhiś ca yathāvidhi ॥
brāhmaṇaiḥ śucibhir vṛddhaiḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ ॥
[143]        pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā devadevaṃ praveśayet ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca brahmaghoṣaiś ca puṣkalaiḥ ॥
dvārasthāne tu devasya arghaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥
praveśayet tataḥ kṣipraṃ mantraṃ caiman udāharet ॥
'viśvaṃ praviśatī'ti ॥
brahmapīṭhe tato nyasya punaḥ sūtrāṇi mokṣayet ॥
prāśayeta madhunā ca madhyaṃ devasya lepayet  ॥!
'madhuvātā' ityanena mantreṇa ॥
nimittam aśubhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kaṃcit kālaṃ vidhārayet ॥
āvāhayet tato devam arghaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥
āgaccha bhagavan brahman lokānugrahakāraṇāt ॥
idam āsanaṃ suraśreṣṭhanirmitaṃ viśvakarmaṇā ॥
sthiro bhava viśvā bhatra dhruvādyasamayam iti pratiṣṭhāpayet ॥
‘dhruvā dyaur dhruvā ime parvatā, dhruvo yajño, yajamāno dhruvo, rājā dhruvaṃ viśvam iti namo namaḥ ॥
iti pratiṣṭhāpanamantraḥ ॥
argheṇa pūjayed devaṃ arghaṃ śṛṇuta bhārata  ॥ ॥
āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi akṣatādadhitaṇḍulāḥ ॥
tilāḥ siddhārthakāś caiva aṣṭāṅgo ‘rghaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
pañcagavyaṃ dadhikṣīraṃ sarpiṣā saha bhārata ॥
gandhodakaṃ gandhavārikuśodakaphalodakam ॥
upahārāgranibhāś cātra pūrayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
saṃhitāḥ kārayen mantraiḥ sarvān eva samāhitaḥ ॥
[144] ‘āgacchāgaccha bhagavan suraśreṣṭha saveda, sapatnīka, pādyaṃ datvāgataṃ bhavate’ti ॥ pitāmahāya nalinayonaye namaḥ ॥ 'imā āpaḥ śivāḥ śivatamāḥ ॥ śāntāḥ śāntatamā’ ity anena mantreṇārghaṃ dadyāt ॥ ‘namaste tu mahāmāye'ti hiṃ sīḥ śivo namaḥ sumanā bhava’ iti pañcagavyamantraḥ ॥ ‘tejo ‘si śukramasī’ty ājyamantraḥ ॥ madhuvātā ityevamādi ॥ sāvitryā kṣīram abhimantrayet ॥
upamānikaiḥ kaṣāyaiḥ devasyodvarttanaṃ smṛtam ॥
‘hiraṇya’ ityevamādi upamānikāmantraḥ ॥
sthāpayen niṣprakampaṃ tu niḥśabdaṃ niścalaṃ tathā ॥
svasthāne devadeveśaṃ suraśreṣṭhaṃ caturmukham ॥
sthāpyamāno yadā devo namate kampate 'pi vā ॥
tadā śāntis tu kartavyā pauṣṭikī nābhicārikīm ॥
yadā tu calate deva aindrīṃ ca diśam āśritaḥ ॥
vāruṇīṃ kārayec chāntiṃ dadhād viprāya vāsasī ॥
yadā tu calate brahmā vāyavyāṃ diśam āśritaḥ ॥
vāyavyāṃ kārayec chāntiṃ dadyād dugdhaṃ dvijātaye ॥
yadā tu calate brahmā saumyāṃ ca diśam āśritaḥ ॥
[145]        saumyāṃ ca kārayec chāntiṃ dhenuṃ dadyād dvijātaye ॥
yadā tu calate deva aiśānīṃ diśam āśritaḥ ॥
tadā raudrī bhavec chāntīruktaṃ dadyād dvijātiṣu ॥
yasyāṃ yasyāṃ caled devas tasyāḥ śāntir (?) vidur budhāḥ ॥
nādhodṛṣṭiṃ nordhvadṛṣṭiṃ tiryagdṛṣṭiṃ ca kārayet ॥
pratiṣṭhāpya yathānyāyaṃ suraśreṣṭhaṃ mahīpate ॥
snāpanaṃ kārayed bhūmau vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
‘śan no devīr abhiṣṭaye’tyevamādi ॥
śāntyudakaṃ ca kartavyaṃ hutvāgnau vidhivad dvijaiḥ ॥
‘āśu[śuḥ] śiṣā[śā]no vṛṣabha’ ityevamādi ॥
taijasāny arghapātrāṇi mṛnmayaṃ tu na kārayet ॥
sauvarṇaṃ ca bhavet sarvaṃ raupyaṃ tāmram athāpi vā ॥
anyathā kriyamāṇe ca viparītaphalaṃ bhavet ॥
sunāteti dvijāḥ brūyuḥ sarve ca vratinas tataḥ ॥
gandhapuṣpāṇi devasya dhūpo deyas tathaiva ca ॥
dīpaṃ prajvālya mantreṇa nāma kiṃcid udāharet ॥
ime gandhāḥ śubhāḥ śītāḥ sūkṣmāḥ surabhigandhinaḥ ॥
pūtā brahmapavitreṇa pratigṛhāṇa yathāvidhi ॥
imāḥ sumanaso devyaḥ sarvagandhavibhūṣitāḥ ॥
pūtā brahmapavitreṇa pratigṛhāṇa pitāmaha ॥
ayaṃ vanaspatiraso divyagandhāḍhya uttamaḥ ॥
āhāraḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpo 'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥
[146]        agnir jyotiś ca śuklaś ca snehapūrvo vibhāvasuḥ ॥
prabhākaro mahātejā dīpo 'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥
tataś caturvidhair vastrair yugmabaddhaiḥ prapūjayet ॥
pūrveṇa paṭṭajaṃ dadyād romajaṃ dakṣiṇena tu ॥
paścimena tu kārpāsam uttareṇa tu valkajam ॥
'rociṣṇurasī'ty ādarśaṃ darśayati  ॥
naivedyaṃ tasya dātavyaṃ saghṛtaṃ pāyasaṃ śubham ॥
‘devasyatve’ti mantreṇa, atha vā ‘devatvāye’ti mantreṇeti ॥
vividhaṃ ca baliṃ kṛtvā tarpayed bhūtanāyakam ॥
gandhamālyaiś ca mantraiś ca pūjayitvā dvijottama ॥
śāntyudakaṃ gṛhītvā tu snapayet pūjayed grahān ॥
śayanāsanavastraiś ca pūjayet sthāpakaṃ tataḥ ॥
yuktaṃ ṣoḍaśabhir viprair bhojakena ca bhārata ॥
pūjayālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā tato deyā ca dakṣiṇā ॥
imaṃ madagalaṃ dadyāt prāsādaṃ vā subhūṣitam ॥
grāmaṃ cāpi niṣpannaṃ yena tuṣyati te guruḥ ॥
tava prasādād vipendra duṣprāpaṃ prāptavān aham ॥
praṇamya śirasā brūyāt prasīdeti punaḥ punaḥ ॥
adhivāsanavastrāṇi śayyā chatraṃ vitānakam ॥
kalaśāś cārghapātrāṇi sarvāṇi sthāpakasya hi ॥
pratiṣṭhāpayec ca sadvṛttaṃ na yāvad daśamikā bhavet ॥
sthāpakasyānumodena nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ॥
[147]        ya evaṃ vidhinā kuryāt pratiṣṭhāṃ brahmaṇo naraḥ ॥
sa sarvaguṇasampanno yāti brahmasado nṛpa ॥
‘oṃ namaḥ surāsura yathā svāhā pitāmahāya svāhā’ ityanena mantreṇa devam abhyañjayet ॥
caturthikāṃ tataḥ kuryād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
vividhaṃ ca yathā kuryyāt brāhmaṇānāṃ ca tarpaṇam ॥
eṣa eva vidhiḥ kāryaḥ prāpte ca daśame 'hani ॥
varddhamānāni kumbhāni māsaṣaṇmāsakāni ca ॥
idaṃ ca paramaṃ guhyaṃ brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śṛṇu ॥
vidhānena yad uktena yo me mūrtiṃ praropayet ॥
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi mama loke mahīyate ॥
tatsthāne punar āvṛttaḥ pārthivo jāyate mahān ॥
eṣa evaṃvidho kāryaḥ purāṇāyataneṣv api ॥
iti te kathito rājan pratiṣṭhādividhikramaḥ ॥
brahmaṇo 'yaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā mucyate brahmahatyayā ॥
[148]
atha viṣṇupratiṣṭhā ॥ 13 ॥
[tatra] bhaviṣyapurāṇe ॥
sumantur uvāca ॥
adhivāsanaṃ śṛṇu vibhor viṣṇor amitatejasaḥ ॥
prāsādān nātidūre tu kuryān maṇḍapam agrataḥ ॥
daśadvādaśahastaṃ vā parikṣiptaṃ samantataḥ  ॥ ॥
caturbhis toraṇair yuktaṃ puṇyadāmopaśobhitam ॥
plakṣodumbaram aśvatthavaṭaṃ pūrvāditaḥ kramāt ॥
hastaṃ prāveśayed bhūmau dṛśyaṃ hastacatuṣṭayam ॥
sūpalipte susaṃmṛṣṭe catustoraṇasaṃyute ॥
sthaṇḍilasyopari kṣiptaṃ samantād upaśobhitam ॥
nadītīramṛdāyukte vālukāyāṃ ca kārite ॥
akālān kalaśān aṣṭau teṣu dvāreṣu sthāpayet ॥
'akālān' akṛṣṇān ॥
āmrapallavasaṃchannān phalapuṣpair alaṅkṛtān ॥
ahatair yugalair vastrair grīvāyāṃ tu niveśitaiḥ ॥
candanodakatoyena pūrayitvā yathākramam ॥
dīptamālāparikṣiptapatākābhir alaṅkṛtam ॥
gāyanair nartakaiś caiva vāditrair vividhaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
patākāś caiva vakṣyāmi diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ॥
[149]        pūrveṇendrāyudhākārām anale lohitāṃ tathā ॥
kṛṣṇāṃ caiva tu yāmyāyāṃ nairṛtyāṃ ca tathaiva ca ॥
indragopanibhākārāṃ vāruṇyāṃ caiva dīpayet ॥
śirīṣapuṣpapratimāṃ vāyavyāṃ saṃniveśayet ॥
kauveryāṃ pītavarṇāṃ tu aiśānyāṃ pāṇḍurā bhavet ॥
madhye tu jaladākārāṃ patākām ucchrayet sadā ॥
etāḥ sarvāḥ samucchritya dhūpadīpādikaṃ kramāt ॥
svena svena tu mantreṇa diśāṃ devān kṣamāpayet ॥
ūrdhvas tu sarvadevāṃs tu brahmādyāṃs tu kṣamāpayet ॥
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā pañca ṣaḍ vādhivāsayet ॥
tato viṣṇuṃ samāsīnaṃ sudhautaṃ suparīkṣitam ॥
śilpinaḥ pūjayet paścād vastrālaṅkaraṇādibhiḥ ॥
anye ca pūjanīyāś ca ye cātra paricārakāḥ ॥
prastare tu tataḥ śayyāṃ divyāstaraṇasaṃyutām ॥
ācāryo mūrtipaiś caiva ṛtvigbhiḥ saha saṃyutaḥ ॥
viṣṇuvṛttadharaiḥ śāntair viṣṇuśāstrānurañjitaiḥ ॥
alolupair ahīnāṅgair āryadeśasamudbhavaiḥ ॥
[150]        viṣṇubhaktiparair dhīrair viprair bhāvayutaiḥ saha ॥
dvātriṃśadbhiḥ ṣoḍaśabhir aṣṭābhis tu krameṇa tu ॥
anāyitvā tu deveśaṃ snapayet snānamaṇḍale ॥
snapanaṃ tu tataḥ kāryaṃ divyamaṅgalavāditam ॥
yathānurūpair vividhair vāditraninadaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
kaṣāyaiḥ pañcagavyena tīrthavalmīkamṛjjalaiḥ ॥
samudrajyeṣṭhā salilasya madhyā
yā āpo divyā uta vā bhavanti ॥
yāsāṃ rājā varuṇo yāti madhye
yāsu rājā na varuṇo yāsu some ॥
ityetābhiḥ ṛgbhiḥ snapayet ॥
arghaṃ datvā namaskṛtya arcayitvā yathākramam ॥
‘yā divyā āpaḥ yaśasā’ ityanena mantreṇeti ॥
datvā tu guggulaṃ dhūpaṃ devadevasya dhīmataḥ ॥
pracchādya vastrayugmena ‘yuvaṃ vastrāṇī’tyanena mantreṇa,
tataḥ kāryṃ tu lakṣaṇam ॥
utthāpayet tato devaṃ mantreṇānena suvrataḥ ॥
‘utiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate rathe tiṣṭhannayati vājina' ityanena mantreṇa
ratham āropayet prabhum ॥
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣaiḥ patākābhir alaṅkṛtam ॥
āropya sadṛśe yāne ānayet tauraṇāntikam ॥
'udutyaṃ jātavedasam' ॥ 'ākṛṣṇena rajase'ti ca ॥
praveśayet tato devaṃ sthāpakaḥ prayataḥ śuciḥ ॥
tataś ca prastarec chayyāṃ sthāpakaḥ śaṃ niveśayet ॥
prāgagrair darbhavaṃśais tu rājan divyajalaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
[151]        upariṣṭāc ca śayyāyā deyaṃ prastaraṇaṃ śubham ॥
dukūlaṃ paṭṭasaṃcchatraṃ kāryā saṃśvātyasambhave (?) ॥
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sthāpakaḥ prayataḥ śuciḥ ॥
prāṅmukhaṃ tu tataḥ kṛtvā bhadrapīṭhe susaṃsthitam ॥
pūrayitvāmbhasā vipra kalaśaṃ cāpy alaṅkṛtam ॥
sapuṣpaṃ sākṣataṃ kṛtvā śirodeśe tu dāpayet ॥
āpo devīti mantreṇa mātaraḥ sudhayantu ca ॥
tataḥ śirotarobhūya upadhānasamanvite ॥
śayane kārayed devaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
madhunā sarpiṣābhyañjya dadyātsiddhārthakān śubhān ॥
sthitvaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve mantraṃ vai samudāharet ॥
āpyāyasveti mantreṇa yā te rudra śiveti ca ॥
sarvāṅgaṃ ca samālabhya dadyāt puṣpaṃ narādhipa ॥
śvetān pratiśarān dadyān mantreṇānena sādhipa ॥
bṛhaspataye vā māṃ mantreṇa pariveṣṭayet  ॥ ॥
dukūlapaṭṭakārpāsais tathā valkalaromajaiḥ ॥
citraṃ paṭṭavicitraiś ca chādayeta samantataḥ ॥
cāmaraṃ vyañjanaṃ chatraṃ vitānaṃ ca suśobhanam ॥
darpaṇaḥ pārśvataḥ sthāpyo ratnāny oṣadhayas tathā ॥
gṛhopakaraṇaiś cānyaiḥ samantāt parivārayet ॥
'abhi tvā śūra no numa' ityanenaiva kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ tathā  ॥ ॥
mantram etaṃ samuccārya pādasthāne niveśayet ॥
[152]        bhakṣyabhojyānnapānaiś ca ṣaḍvidhaiś ca viśeṣataḥ ॥
sarvatas tu baliṃ kṛtvā maṇḍapaṃ ca suśobhanam ॥
prakīrṇā aprakīrṇāś ca dātavyo devasaṃnidhau ॥
'prakīrṇa odanādi prakīrya yo balir dīyate sa 'prakīrṇaḥ'; evam aprakīrya dīyata ity aprakīrṇaḥ ॥ 'tryambakaṃ yajāmahe' ityanena mantreṇa sarvam etad dhi kārayet ॥
mūrtimān sthāpayet paścād diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ॥
śrīsūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca vāmyaṃ dāmyaṃ ca vājinaḥ ॥
vṛṣākapiṃ ca maitraṃ ca bahvṛcaḥ paścime japet ॥
devavrataṃ puruṣagatiṃ jyeṣṭhasāmarathantaram ॥
bhāruṇḍāni ca sāmāni vāmadevyaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
uttarāṃ diśam āsthāya chandogas tu japet tadā ॥
rudrāya puruṣasūktaṃ ca ślokādhyāyaṃ ca śukriyam ॥
brāhmaṇamantreṇa maitreya adhvaryuḥ pūrvato japet ॥
atharvāṅgirasaṃ caiva śivasūktaṃ tathaiva ca ॥
nīlarudraṃ caiva mantram atharvā dakṣiṇo japet ॥
viṣṇukalpaṃ pariṣadaṃ pañcarātrāṃśagāminam ॥
stotraṃ mantravidaś cātra paṭheyur dakṣiṇe sadā ॥
śiraḥsthāne tu kṛṣṇasya ācāryo homam ācaret ॥
śāntikaiḥ pauṣṭikaiś cāpi mantrair vyāhṛtibhis tathā ॥
palāśodumbarāśvattham apāmārgaśamīs tathā ॥
sahasram ekaṃ hutvā ca viṣṇupādau spṛśet tataḥ ॥
dvitīyaṃ tu tato hutvā samidbhis tābhir eva tu ॥
[153]        madhye tu saṃspṛśed devaṃ mūrdhni caiva krameṇa tu ॥
homāya prayatāḥ sarve mūrtimāṃś ca diśaḥśritāḥ  ॥ ॥
anantādiśivāntāś ca aṣṭau ye cakravartinaḥ ॥
teṣāṃ mantras tu hotavyāḥ juhuyāc ca yathākramam ॥
śāntitoyaghaṭaṃ gṛhya sampātāhutihutaṃ tadā ॥
tena pādau tu devasya śiraḥ prakṣālya yatnataḥ ॥
sthitaṃ ca snāpayet tena sampātāhutivāriṇā ॥
punaḥ pracchādayec cāpi dhūpaṃ dadyāt saguggulam ॥
nivedanaṃ haviṣyānnaṃ tathā modakasaṃyutam ॥
brāhmaṇān pūjayet pūrvaṃ śeṣān anyāṃs tu kāmataḥ ॥
rātrau mahotsavaḥ kāryaḥ śaṅkhabheryādiniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
gāyanair nartakaiś caiva brahmaghoṣais tathaiva ca ॥
purato vaiṣṇavān dharmāṃś (?) chrāvayed vācakas tathā ॥
trirātram ekarātraṃ vā kuryād devādhivāsanam ॥
iti te 'dhivāsanavidhiḥ kathitaḥ adhipeśvara ॥
adhivāsya tato viṣṇuṃ śubhraṃ kuryāt samantataḥ ॥
prāsādasyānurūpeṇa pratimāyāś ca bhārata ॥
prāsādaṃ prokṣya sarvatra mantrapūtena vāriṇā ॥
pātayet pakṣasūtrāṇi dvārasūtraṃ tathaiva ca ॥
tato madhyasamaṃ gṛhya kartavyaṃ sthiram ādarāt ॥
kuryāt sūtraṃ pramāṇena kṛṣṇasyādbhutakarmaṇaḥ ॥
[154]        śvabhrādyas tān nyaset pūrvaṃ śilāṃ brahmātmikāṃ śubhām ॥
tataś cotthāpayed devaṃ mantreṇānena kṣmādhipa ॥
'uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate' ityanena
puṇyāhabrahmaghoṣaiś ca śaṅkhabheryādiniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
rathaṃ cālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā tasmiṃś cāropayec chanaiḥ ॥
'rathe tiṣṭhan nayati vājina' ityanena mantreṇa
rathasthaṃ kārayed dharim ॥
grāmaṃ vā nagaraṃ vāpi śanaiḥ kuryāt pradakṣiṇām ॥
puṣpair gandhais tathānnādyaiḥ śakaṭaṃ pūrayec chubham ॥
śaṅkhabherīninādaiś ca patākābhir dhvajais tathā ॥
cāmaraiś ca vitānaiś ca kalaśaiḥ padmaśobhitam ॥
dīpadarpaṇasaṃyuktair dīpamaṅgalanāditaiḥ ॥
baliṃ prakṣipyamāṇas tu śanair gacchet pradakṣiṇam ॥
ānīya maṇḍapaṃ paścād arghapādyaṃ ca dāpayet ॥
tasmin muhūrtaṃ viśrāmya ratnanyāsaṃ tu kārayet ॥
sarvabījāni dhātūṃś (?) ca ratnāny oṣadhayas tathā ॥
kāñcanaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca pāradena samanvitam ॥
padmaṃ kūrmaś ca pṛthivī garuḍaś cānupūrvaśaḥ ॥
brahmasthāne tu dātavyā mantrapūtā yathāvidhi ॥
bījagāḍhaṃ prakurvīta samūlāṃs tān pradīpayet ॥
bījagāḍhe mahādoṣo vyādhidoṣabhayaṃ bhavet ॥
bījakānāṃ tv alābhe tu svarṇaṃ sarvatra vinyaset ॥
ataḥ paraṃ ratnaṃ nyāsasya mantrā bhavanti te ॥
oṃ indras tu mahasādīptaḥ sarvadevādhipo mahān ॥
dambholihastaḥ sattvāsvas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
[155]        āgneyaḥ puruṣo dīptaḥ sarvadevamayo 'vyayaḥ ॥
dhūmaketunarādhṛṣyaḥ tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
oṃ yamaś cotpalavarṇābhaḥ puruṣo daṇḍadhṛk mahān ॥
dharmasākṣī viśuddhātmā tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
nairṛtyāṃ puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sarvarakṣo 'dhipo mahān ॥
khaḍgahasto mahāsattvas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
oṃ varuṇaḥ pracalo viṣṇuḥ puruṣo nimnagādhipaḥ ॥
pāśahasto mahātmā ca tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
oṃ vāyur vai śyāmavarṇo 'yaṃ sarvagandhavahaḥ śuciḥ ॥
puruṣo dhvajahastas tu tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
oṃ ādyas tu puruṣaḥ saumya sarvauṣadhisamanvitaḥ ॥
nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
oṃ īśānaḥ puruṣaḥ śuklaḥ sarvavidyādhipas tathā ॥
śūlahasto virūpākṣas tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
padmayoniś caturmūrtir vedavyāsaḥ pitāmahaḥ ॥
yajñarakṣaś ca sat tat taṃ tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ ॥
ratnanyāse kṛte paścāt pāyasenopalepayet ॥
paṭṭena mahatā paścāc chuklena parichādayet ॥
vedī bhagavato meruḥ sauvarṇiḥ sā ca kīrtitā ॥
suvarṇapṛthivī caiva rājatau tatra kārayet ॥
aṣṭamaṅgalakaṃ nāma lohapaṅkavinirmitam ॥
padmaś caiva tu kūrmaś ca kāryau śuklamaya śubhau ॥
[156]        eteṣām evam ekaṃ tu madhyadravyam udāhṛtam ॥
nyāsaś caiva yathālābhaṃ hīnavittas tu mānavaḥ ॥
añjanaṃ madhukaṃ caiva akṣatā meruṇā saha ॥
brahmasthāne tu nyastabdhā ṣaḍ ete ca yathākramam ॥
kṣīravṛkṣatvacaṃ puṣpam aṣṭamaṃ guḍapāyasam ॥
śrīkāmas tu nyaset samyak deśavṛddhikaraṃ śubham ॥
kāñcanaṃ ghṛtagodhūmān dūrvā rocanayā saha ॥
gośṛṅgamṛttikāṃś caiva kūrmeṇa saha nikṣipet  ॥ ॥
sthitasya tasya devasya śriyā vṛddhiḥ sadā bhavet ॥
kṛśarāṃ kāñcanaṃ caiva padmena saha vinyaset ॥
sthitasya tasya devasya cāturvarṇyahitaṃ bhavet ॥
catuṣpādāś ca vardhante na ca sasyaṃ vinaśyati ॥
lājā madhu ghṛtaṃ caiva śamī cāśvattham eva ca ॥
vedyāṃ ca saha dātavyaṃ yajñotsavakaraṃ tathā ॥
mūrtikā parvatasyātha hṛdā dīpas tathaiva ca ॥
sadā krameṇa dātavyaṃ rāṣṭravṛddhikaraṃ nṛpa  ॥ ॥
kāñcanaṃ sarvabījāni medinyā saha vinyaset ॥
sthitasya tatra vai tasya prajā pramuditā bhavet ॥
candanaṃ sarvabījāni meruṇā saha vinyaset ॥
sthitasya tatra kṛṣṇasya prajā rājā ca varddhate ॥
[157]        brahmasthāne tu vinyasya sarvakāmārthadaṃ śubham ॥
na bhayaṃ naiva durbhikṣaṃ pañcadravye vyavasthitam ॥
śvabhrasya dakṣiṇe pārśve sarṣapān juhuyād budhaḥ ॥
agnaye brahmaṇe caiva śrīrudrāya ca viṣṇave ॥
skandāya gaṇarājāya gaṇamātṛbhya eva ca ॥
indrādyāṃl lokapālāṃś ca ādityāṃś ca vasūṃs tathā ॥
viśvedevās tathā sarvā svanāmnā copalakṣitān ॥
oṃkārādyān namontāṃś ca kṛtvotthāpya baliṃ haret ॥
utthitaṃ tatra deveśaṃ vālarajvā hi daṇḍavat ॥
tataḥ suyantritaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇān svastivācya ca ॥
jīvadānaṃ tato datvā devadevaṃ samutkṣipet ॥
sthāpakaḥ sthāpayed devaṃ mūrtipair balavattaraiḥ ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca brahmaghoṣaiś ca puṣkalaiḥ ॥
ācāryaś cāgrato gacched arghahastaḥ samāhitaḥ ॥
pṛṣṭhato yajamānas tu bhaktyā devaṃ tu saṃspṛśet ॥
putramātrakalatraiś ca bandhubhiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ ॥
sarve 'nyonyasya saṃlagnā dhyāyamānā janārdanam ॥
dvāradeśe samānīya arghaṃ tasmai nivedayet ॥
tataḥ praveśayed devaṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitam ॥
tiryag ūrdhvam adhaś caivam aspṛśaṃ taṃ praveśayet ॥
viṣṇuṃ pīṭhe nyaset paścād arghaṃ datvā samāhitaḥ ॥
[158]        kapilākṣīrasaṃsiddhaparamānnaṃ susaṃskṛtam ॥
kapilājyena saṃmiśraṃ tena śvabhraṃ tu lepayet ॥
‘sarasvatī pratidiśatu’ ityanena mantreṇa sarvāsu vinyaset ॥
gandhaṃ puṣpārghadhūpaiś ca pūjayet susamāhitaḥ ॥
paścād āvāhanaṃ kuryād devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ ॥
dhyātvā paramasadbhāvaṃ caturbāhuṃ caturbhujam ॥
tatra sandhyāya cittaṃ tu niścalaṃ kurunandana ॥
āvāhanaṃ tu kurvīta stutibhir mantrasaṃyutaḥ ॥
oṃ āgaccha bhagavann anugrahāya janārdana ॥
śāśvataṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇātu bhagavān mantrapūtam idam arghyaṃ pādyam ācamanīyaṃ caiva tadāsanaṃ brahmaṇā vihitaṃ svayam ॥ 'oṃ dhruvā dyau dhruvā pṛthivī dhruvaṃ viśvam idaṃ javād (?) iti  ॥ anena mantreṇa matimān ' āsanaṃ sthāpayet ॥ asaṃlagnam adbhutaṃ vilambitam ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca brahmaghoṣaiś ca puṣkalaiḥ ॥
śaṅkhabherininādaiś ca gītavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
sthitamātraṃ suvarṇena sthāpayed atha sarpiṣā ॥
tasyānte sthāpayet tena sampātāhuticāriṇā ॥
arghaṃ datvā namaskṛtya arcayitvā yathāvidhi ॥
saghṛtaṃ guggulaṃ dagdhvā niḥśabdaṃ kārayet tataḥ ॥
aiśāne tu diśābhāge sthitaḥ tūṣṇīṃ susaṃyutaḥ ॥
uccārayen mahāmantraṃ dvādaśākṣaram adbhutam ॥
[159]        tena vai jāyamānena vidhinā cchidratā bhavet ॥
mantrahīno vidhir yaś ca aṅgahīnaś ca yo bhavet ॥
hīno vai dakṣiṇābhis tu na praśastaḥ kadācana ॥
eṣa guhyo vidhiḥ prokto sarvathā vai mayā tava ॥
adhunā vaidikaṃ vakṣye vidhiṃ sāmānyaveditam ॥
‘yaj jāgrato dūram u daivam’ iti ॥ ‘yena karmāṇy apaso manīṣiṇo yajñe’ti ॥ ‘yat prajñānam uta ceto dhṛtiś ce’ti ॥ ‘yenedaṃ bhūtaṃ bhuvanaṃ bhaviṣyat’ ॥ ‘yasminn ṛcaḥ sāmayajuṃsi ॥ suṣārathiraśvān iva ॥
etābhir ṛgbhiḥ ṣaḍibhir (?) viṣṇuṃ sarvatra saṃspṛśet ॥
dakṣiṇā paścimā caiva uttarā pūrvam eva ca ॥
mūrdhni mūlena yatnena saṃspṛśet prayato budhaḥ ॥
madhuvātā ṛtetyanena mantreṇa abhyaṅgaṃ prathame 'hani ॥
pāṭanaṃ sarvarogāṇāṃ sarvaṃ nāśāya meṃ prabho ॥
haridrāsarṣapaiś caiva dvitīye 'hani lepayet ॥
pittarogabhayaṃ caiva na bhavet tu kadācana ॥
taṇḍulena yavaiś caiva tṛtīye 'hani lepayet ॥
śirorttim akṣirogaṃ ca karṇaśūlaṃ ca nāśayet ॥
manaśśilāṃ priyaṅguṃ ca caturthe 'hani lepayet ॥
saubhāgyaṃ yena satataṃ yajamānaś ca varddhate ॥
kṛṣṇājinaṃ tilāṃś caiva pañcame 'hani lepayet ॥
[160]        pitaras tasya tṛpyanti yāvajjīvaṃ sukhapradāḥ ॥
surabhicandanaṃ caiva saghṛtaṃ padmakesaram ॥
ṣaṣṭhe 'hani ca dātavyaṃ śatrudoṣavināśanam ॥
rocanāṃ nāgapuṣpaṃ ca saptame 'hani dāpayet ॥
'’kesaraṃ’ nāgakesaram ॥
kṣemaṃ gobahulaṃ cāpi subhikṣaṃ ca bhavet sadā  ॥ ॥
evaṃ saptāham abhyaṅgaṃ kāpilena ghṛtena tu ॥
dadyād vibhavataḥ śaktyā yadīcchec chriyam ātmanaḥ ॥
dīpā ghṛtena dātavyāś catvāraś ca pradakṣiṇam ॥
saptāhaṃ mantrayuktāś ca vibhavena tataḥ param ॥
sthitaṃ na cālayed devaṃ kadācid api paṇḍitaḥ ॥
hanyād vicālito jñānāt tasmāt taṃ na vicālayet ॥
yāṃ yāṃ diśaṃ called devas tāṃ tāṃ śāntiṃ tu kārayet ॥
anyathā hi bhaved ghoraṃ bhayaṃ rāṣṭravināśanam ॥
yathā na calate devas tathā badhnīta piṇḍikām ॥
śīghram eva tu kartavyaṃ mā vilambet kadācana ॥
pratimāṃ susthitāṃ kuryān nordhvāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ kadācana ॥
anyathā hi bhaved ghoraṃ bhayaṃ lokavināśanam ॥
nā'ghoṣṭiṃ prakurvīta tiryagdṛṣṭiṃ kadācana ॥
trirātraṃ saptarātraṃ vā daśarātram athāpi vā ॥
puṇyāhajayaśabdaiś ca kuryād devamahotsavam ॥
snapanaṃ satataṃ kuryāt tac ca saṃkṣepetaḥ śruṇu ॥
[161]        arghaṃ cāpi samāsena śṛṇuśvāvahito 'dhunā ॥
āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi dadhyakṣatatilās tathā  ॥ ॥
yavāḥ siddhārthakāś caiva argho 'ṣṭāṅgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
'akṣatāḥ' atra akhaṇḍataṇḍulāḥ ॥
pañcagavyaṃ dadhikṣīraṃ tathā madhu ghṛtaṃ nṛpa ॥
ratnavārikuśāmbhaś ca phalaṃ gandhodakaṃ tathā ॥
upasnānaiḥ kaṣāyaiś ca divyauṣadhibhir eva ca  ॥ ॥
valmīkāt parvatāgrāc ca sarastīrthād vrajād gajāt ॥
agnāgārāt tu gṛhṇīyād aṣṭamāt tu catuṣpathāt ॥
uddhṛtāsīti mantreṇa mṛdayā tu samālabhet ॥
upamānakais tu prathamaṃ mantreṇānena lepayet ॥
śanno devīr abhiṣṭayeti upamānakamantrāḥ ॥
snānamantrāṃs tathā cānyān pravakṣyāmi mahīpate ॥
gāyatryā caiva gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam ॥
āpyāyasve ti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikravṇeti ca dadhi ॥
tejo 'si śukram ity ājyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam ॥
ebhis tu pañcabhir mantraiḥ prathamaṃ snapayed dharim ॥
dvitīye ca dadhikrāvṇā dadhnā snānaṃ samācaret ॥
āpyāyasveti mantreṇa kṣīreṇa snāpayed budhaḥ ॥
tejo 'sīti tataḥ paścād ghṛtena snānam ācaret ॥
tadante madhunā snānaṃ madhunvātā ṛtāyate ॥
[162]        tato vāri tataḥ paścād dhiraṇyākhyeti mantrayet ॥
kuśāmbhasā tataḥ snānaṃ devasya tveti mantrayet ॥
phalodakais tataḥ snānaṃ agna āyāhi vītaye ॥
dadyāt tu gandhatoyāni sāvitrā tv abhimantrayet ॥
mahāsnānaṃ tataḥ kuryād dravyasnānād anantaram ॥
kalaśānāṃ sahasreṇa kuryāt pañcaśatena vā ॥
atha vādhyardham ardhena tathā cāṣṭaśatena vā ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭyā tadarddhena ṣoḍaśair aṣṭabhis tathā ॥
yathā lābhaṃ yathā kālaṃ yathā vibhavam ātmanaḥ ॥
akālamūlaiḥ kalaśaiḥ supakvaiḥ sudṛḍhair (?) navaiḥ ॥
rājatais tāmrasauvarṇaiḥ kāṃsyapaittalapārthivaiḥ ॥
balā cātibalā caiva śaṅkhapuṣpī suvarcalā ॥
sahadevī ca siṃhī ca vāyavyāghrī tathaiva ca ॥
oṣadhīnāṃ gaṇenaivaṃ snāpayed deva suvratam ॥
ity evaṃ snāpayitvā tu paścād arghaṃ pradāpayet ॥
āśuḥ śiśāneti ṛcā arghaṃ dadyāt prayatnataḥ ॥
tataḥ pūjāṃ prakurvīta saṃhitoktāṃ tu mantravit ॥
candanāgurukarpūrakuṅkumādyaiś ca lepayet ॥
nārāyaṇeti mantreṇa vastrair dhūpaiś ca yojayet ॥
evaṃ supūjitaṃ kṛtvā devadevaṃ janārdanam ॥
praṇamya daṇḍavad bhūmau śanair gacchet pradakṣiṇam ॥
punar āgatya purato vijñāpyena janārdanam ॥
[163]        sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vāpi jñānato 'jñānato 'pi vā ॥
tat sarvam ātmanā sārddhaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ niveditam ॥
tataḥ sampūjayed bhaktyā sthāpakaṃ vividhair nṛpa ॥
bhūmidānādibhir dānair godānaiś ca mahīpate ॥
yathā sutuṣyate rājan tathā kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ ॥
yathā śaktyānurūpeṇa pūjyaś cāpi dvijātayaḥ ॥
dīnāndhakṛpaṇānāṃ ca pūjā kāryā prayatnataḥ ॥
gāyanān nartakāṃś caiva yathāśakti prapūjayet ॥
rātrau mahotsavaṃ kuryāc chaṅkhabheryādiniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā saptarātram athāpi vā ॥
tasmād viṣṇo sado yāti ity eṣā phalasampadā ॥
nṛsiṃhapurāṇe |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca ॥
pratiṣṭhāvidhiṃ devasya viṣṇor amitatejasaḥ  ॥ ॥
pravakṣyāmi yathāśāstraṃ śṛṇu bhūpāla puṇyadam ॥
pratiṣṭhāṃ kartuṃ yo vātra viṣṇor icchati pārthiva ॥
sa pūrvasthiranakṣatre bhūmiśodhanam ācaret ॥
khātvā puruṣamātraṃ tu bāhumātraṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
pūrayec chuddhamṛdbhis tu jalāktaiḥ śarkarānvitaiḥ ॥
adhiṣṭhānaṃ tato baddhvā pāṣāṇeṣṭakamṛnmayam ॥
prāsādaṃ kārayet tatra vāstuvidyāvidāṃ nṛṇām ॥
[164]        caturasraṃ sūtramārge catuṣkoṇaṃ samaṃ śubham ॥
śilābhittikam utkṛṣṭaṃ tadalābheṣṭakāmayam ॥
tadabhāve tu mṛtkuḍyaṃ pūrvadvāraṃ suśobhanam ॥
jātikāṣṭhamayaiḥ stambhaiḥ tallagnaphalakānvitaiḥ ॥
uttare vanabhāge ca pātitaiś cātra śilpibhiḥ ॥
nicchidraiś ca tadā kāṣṭhaphalakaiś ca mahādṛḍhaiḥ ॥
ākrāntaṃ dāruvaṭakaiḥ sarvataḥ parilambakaiḥ ॥
krakacadāritadārūṇi saṃsthāpyopari kīlayet ॥
āyasair atitīkṣṇaiś ca kīlayitvā tu kīlakaiḥ ॥
mṛnmayair loṣṭaphalakaiḥ saktair upari sarvataḥ ॥
chādayed varṣapānīyaṃ bāhyaiḥ patati vai yathā ॥
itthaṃ tat kārayitvā tu harer veśma suśobhanam ॥
pūrvadvāraṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha sakapāṭaṃ sucitritam ॥
ativṛddhātibālais tu kārayen nākṛtiṃ hareḥ ॥
kuṣṭhādyupahatair vāpi anyair vā dīrgharomibhiḥ ॥
viśvakarmoktaśāstre tu nipuṇena ca śilpinā ॥
kārayet pratimāṃ divyāṃ puṣṭāṅgena tu dhīmatā  ॥ ॥
saumyānanāṃ suśravaṇāṃ sunāsāṃ ca sulocanām ॥
sukirīṭakamūrddhānāṃ paścime jaṭilāṃ śubhām ॥
nādhodṛṣṭiṃ nordhvadṛṣṭiṃ tiryagdṛṣṭiṃ na kārayet ॥
kārayet samadṛṣṭiṃ tu padmapatrāyatekṣaṇām ॥
[165]        subhruvaṃ sulalāṭāṃ ca sukapolāṃ samāṃ śubhām ॥
cārvoṣṭīṃ sucibukāṃ ca sugrīvāṃ kārayed budhaḥ ॥
upabāhukare devyaṃ dakṣiṇe cakram arkavat ॥
nātisaṃlagnadivyāraṃ parito nemisaṃyutam ॥
vāmapārśve hy upabhuje deyaṃ śaṅkhaṃ śaśiprabham ॥
pāñcajanyam iti khyātaṃ daityadarpaharasvanam ॥
hārācitodarāṃ divyāṃ kaṇṭhe trivalisaṃyutām ॥
sustanīṃ cāruhṛdayāṃ sujaṭharāṃ samākṛtim ॥
kaṭilagnavāmakarāṃ padmālaṅkṛtadakṣiṇām ॥
keyūrabāhukāṃ divyāṃ sunābhivalibhaṅgikām ॥
sukaṭiṃ sūkajaṅghāṃ ca vastramekhalasaṃyutām ॥
kaṭipārśve tu vistīrṇāṃ kḷptavastrāṃ suśobhanām ॥
sujānuṃ lakṣaṇopetāṃ pādayugmena susthitām ॥
padmopari suvinyastadivyapādāṃ sulakṣaṇām ॥
vāmapārśve gadopetāṃ sarvāvayavasaṃyutām ॥
suvarṇavastradānena tatkartṝn pūjyasattamaḥ ॥
pūrvapakṣe śubhe kāle sthire cordhvamukhe 'pi vā ॥
prāsādasyāgrataḥ kṛtvā yāgamaṇḍapam uttamam ॥
caturdvāraṃ caturdikṣu caturbhis toraṇair yutam ॥
[166]        saptadhānyāṅkurair yuktaṃ śaṅkhabherīnināditam ॥
pratimāṃ kṣālayed bāhye viṃśatyā tāṃ ghaṭodakaiḥ ॥
praveśya maṇḍape tasmin brāhmaṇair vedapāragaiḥ ॥
tatrasthāṃ snāpayet paścāt pañcagavyaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak ॥
tatoṣṇavāriṇā snāpya punaḥ śītodakena ca ॥
harikuṅkumābhyāṃ tu candanaiś copalepayet ॥
puṣpamālyair alaṅkṛtya vastrair ācchādya tāṃ punaḥ ॥
puṇyāhaṃ tatra kṛtvā tu śvabhis tāṃ prokṣya vāribhiḥ ॥
nītvā tāṃ brāhmaṇair yuktaiḥ śaṅkhabherīsvanair yutām ॥
vāsayet saptarātraṃ tu trirātraṃ vā nadījale ॥
hṛde tu vimale śuddhe taḍāge vā surakṣite ॥
adhivāsya jale devam evaṃ pārthivapuṅgava ॥
tata utthāpya viprais tu snāpyālaṅkṛtya pūrvavat ॥
tato bherīninādaiś ca vedaghoṣais tu keśavam ॥
ānīya maṇḍape śuddhe padmākāravilekhite ॥
kṛtvā tatra punaḥ snāpya viṣṇusūktair alaṅkriyāt ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu vidhivat ṣoḍaśartvijān ॥
caturbhir adhyayanaṃ kuryāc caturbhiḥ pāraṇaṃ tathā  ॥ ॥
caturbhis tu caturdikṣu homaḥ kāryo vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
puṣpākṣatānnamiśreṇa daśadikṣu baliṃ nṛpa ॥
ekena dāpayet teṣām indrādiḥ prīyatām iti  ॥ ॥
pratyekaṃ sāyaṃ saṃdhyāyāṃ madhyarātre tathoṣasi ॥
[167]        udite ca tato dadyān mātṛvighnagaṇāya ca ॥
japet puruṣasūktaṃ tu ekas tatra punaḥ punaḥ ॥
ekāgramanasā rājan viṣṇor mandiramadhyagaḥ ॥
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā yajamāno dvijaiḥ saha ॥
praveśya pratimāṃ dvāre śubhe lagne vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
pāṇipaṭṭe śubhe rājaṃs tasmiṃs tu sthāpya keśavam ॥
dhruvaṃ sūktaṃ tathoccārya ācāryaḥ sthāpayed dṛḍham ॥
saṃsthāpya viṣṇusūktena pāvamānyena vā punaḥ ॥
prokṣayed devadeveśam ācāryaḥ kuśavāriṇā ॥
tadagre cāgnim ādhāya samparistīrya yatnataḥ ॥
juhuyāj jātakarmādi gāyatryā vaiṣṇavena ca ॥
caturbhir ājyāhutibhir ekām ekāṃ kriyāṃ prati ॥
ācāryas tu svayaṃ kuryād daṣṭabandhaṃ ca kārayet ॥
trātāram indram aindrayāṃ tu kuryād vedhāḥ puro'nugaḥ ॥
pareyivāṃsaṃ yāmyāṃ tu vāruṇyām imaṃ meti ca ॥
yat te someti saumyāṃ tu hūyād ājyaṃ caruṃ nṛpa ॥
śanno mātreti sūktābhyāṃ sarvavyāhṛtibhir nṛpa ॥
hutvā japec ca vidhivad yad asyeti ca sviṣṭakṛt ॥
tataś ca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād ṛtvigbhiś ca yathārhataḥ ॥
vastre dve kuṇḍale śaiva (caiva?) gurave cāṅgulīyakam ॥
yajamānasthito dadyād vibhave gāṃ ca kāñcanam ॥
[168]        kalaśāṣṭasahasreṇa kalaśāṣṭaśatena ca ॥
ekaviṃśatinā vāpi snapanaṃ kārayed budhaḥ  ॥ ॥
śaṅkhadundubhinirghoṣair gītavāditramaṅgalaiḥ ॥
yavavīhibhṛtaiḥ pātrair uddhṛtair utthitāṅkuraiḥ ॥
dīpayaṣṭipatākābhiś chātracāmaratoraṇaiḥ ॥
snapanaṃ kārayitvā tu yathā vibhavavistaram ॥
tatrāpi dadyād viprebhyo yathāśaktayā tu dakṣiṇām ॥
evaṃ yaḥ kurute rājan pratiṣṭhāṃ deva cakriṇaḥ ॥
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ ॥
vimānena vicitreṇa trisaptakulajair yutaḥ ॥
pūjāṃ saṃprāpya mahatīm indralokādiṣu kramāt ॥
bāndhavāṃs teṣu saṃsthāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate ॥
tatraiva jñānam āsādya vaiṣṇavaṃ padam āpnuyāt ॥
pratiṣṭhāvidhir ayaṃ viṣṇor mayaivaṃ te prakīrtitaḥ ॥
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ ॥
[169]
atha viṣṇur eva pratimābhedena pratiṣṭhā ॥
[tatra] vārāhapurāṇe |
śṛṇu bhūmi mahābhāge mama karma vyavasthite ॥
kathayiṣyāmi te hīnaṃ karma saṃsthāpanaṃ vibho ॥
muktvā sarvāṇi kāṣṭhāni yāni kāni ca sundari ॥
tiṣṭhāmi madhukāṣṭheṣu guhyam etat paraṃ mama ॥
tasya karma pravakṣyāmi ekāgrā śṛṇu sundari ॥
madhulājāṃs tato dadyāt tathā dadhyakṣatāni ca ॥
dadyāc chvetāni mālyāni gandhāni ca tathaiva ca ॥
śvetāṃś ca pāyasaṃ dadyād arcanaṃ tu samācaret ॥
evaṃ tatra vidhiṃ kṛtvā mama karmavyavasthitaḥ ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udīrayet ॥
mantraḥ ॥ oṃ yo 'sau bhavān sarvajanārtināśano nārāyaṇaḥ sarvajagatpradhānaḥ ॥
etena mantreṇa praviśyam etat
madhūkavṛkṣeṣu ca lokanātha ॥
etena mantreṇa bhava tvaṃ sannihitaḥ
madhūkakāṣṭhena vidhiṃ karomi ॥
