vidyāsusthito namo vāsudeva śāntir bhavatu namo nārāyaṇāya ॥ iti ca svāhā ॥
[170] anena mantreṇānīya, vidhāya, kāṣṭhapratimānāṃ, saṃskāro vidhīyate ॥
evaṃ vidhiḥ kṛtvā sannihito namāmi ॥
evaṃ chitvā tato kāṣṭhaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
pūjayed upacāraiś ca yathā tuṣyati yena vā ॥
evam arcāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā surūpāṃ suvratāṃ śubhām ॥
tataḥ karmāṇi kurvīta yena tiṣṭhāmi śāśvatam ॥
śuklapakṣe tu dvādaśyāṃ nakṣatre revatau tathā ॥
tatra me sthāpanaṃ kuryād bhūmi siddhārthasādhanam ॥
yas tu bhāgavataḥ śuddho mama karmavyavasthitaḥ ॥
sa tu kurvīta karmāṇi bhūmi yaśca mama priyaḥ ॥
yāvakena dinaṃ caikam ekaṃ cakreṇa tiṣṭhati ॥
ekaṃ dinaṃ vāyubhakṣaś caturthe sthāpanaṃ kuru ॥
anyaṃ ca te pravakṣyāmi tac chṛṇuśva vasundhare ॥
adhivāsavidhiṃ caiva karmaṇā yena tatkriyā ॥
ghṛtena prathamaṃ mrakṣed dadhnā tu tadanantaram ॥
kalaśān gṛhya caturo vāripūrṇān athāvani [?] ॥
tataḥ pūrvābhimukho bhūtvā śuklavastreṇa bhūṣitaḥ ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udīrayet ॥
mantraḥ ॥ oṃ yo 'sau bhagavān sarvajagatpradīpo yasya rakṣaiḥ viśanti lokāḥ ॥
anena mantreṇa bhavasva vīra saṃsthāpitā tiṣṭhasva padmanābha ॥
atha tatra caturthe ‘hni kalyam utthāya mādhavi ॥
kuryād abhyañjanaṃ caiva tilatailena sarpiṣā ॥
[171]        tataś codvarttanaṃ kuryāt svakāyair gandhasaṃyutaiḥ ॥
plākṣaṃ vāṭaṃ cārjunaṃ ca laudhaṃ valkalam eva ca ॥
pālāśena tu saṃmiśraṃ kārayet tu vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
tato gandhāni sarvāṇi sarvabījāni mādhavi ॥
pūrvoktena vidhānena vāriṇā saha yojayet ॥
vastre caiva tato dadyād yathā sa gurus tuṣyati ॥
tataḥ pūrvāmukho bhūtvā sarvaṃ karmapathāsthitaḥ ॥
mama saṃsthāpanārthāya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥ oṃ yo 'sau bhūmiparo mahān maṅgalena pumān parataḥ sarvaratnapūrṇaḥ ॥
anena mantreṇa bhavastra vīra saṃsthāpitas tiṣṭhasva vāsudeva ॥
tato vāditraśabdena sūtabhir māgadhais tathā ॥
nartakair gītakaiś caiva bhūmiveśma praveśayet ॥
saṃsthāpya dvāramūle śubhakarmasuniṣṭhitaḥ ॥
paṭhanti sāmāni sūktāny anuvākaś ca brāhmaṇā mama pāṭhakāḥ ॥
teṣāṃ śāntyudakaṃ dadyur brāhmaṇāḥ sāmapāṭhakāḥ ॥
tac ca śāntyudakaṃ gṛhya saratnakanakojvalam ॥
snāpayed dvāramūle ca imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥ yo 'sau bhava .... bhūtapūrvābhimukhas tiṣṭhati viśvarūpa ॥
etena mantreṇa praviśya veśma saṃsthāpitas tiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
[172] etena bhūmiveśmapraveśe sthāpayitvā veśmamadhye imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ bhagavate tasmai jagatpratiṣṭhā praviśya kāṣṭhe ‘pi lokanātha ॥
ādhārabhūta subhavasva sthāne saṃpūjitas tiṣṭhasva vāsudeva ॥
tatas tu sthāpanaṃ kṛtvā snānaṃ tatra ca kārayet ॥
vividhair gandhalājaiś ca sarvabījasamanvitaiḥ ॥
tatra saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
vastraṃ dadyāt tato bhūmipītakāni na saṃśayaḥ ॥
yajñopavītakaṃ cāsya jānubhyāṃ bhūmisaṃsthitaḥ ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ nārāyaṇaṃ cādim anantarūpaṃ saṃsthāpito 'sau sarvato bhūmimantraḥ ॥
ityanena mantreṇa imaṃ vastraṃ gṛhṇīṣva lokanātha ॥
evaṃ vastraṃ tato dadyād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
tato dadyāc ca tān gandhān ye mayā samudāhṛtāḥ ॥
karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ caiva tvacaṃ cāgurum eva ca ॥
rasaṃ ca candanaṃ caiva silhakośīrakaṃ tathā ॥
etair vilepanaṃ dadyāt surabhis tu vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥
svastikaṃ varddhamānaṃ ca nandyāvarttāni mādhavi ॥
evaṃ sarvaṃ tato dadyād yan mayā parikīrtitam ॥
[173]        karmaṇā vidhidṛṣṭena śuddho bhāgavataḥ śuciḥ ॥
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavāṃs tiṣṭhate sarvajagatpradhānaḥ ॥
sarvāṇi lokāni ca yasya.... dehe ॥
sa deva devo bhuvanasya goptā
kāṣṭhe tu sāṃnidhyaṃ sadā karotu ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā kāṣṭhasya pratimāṃ tathā ॥
punaḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā śuddhair bhāgavataiḥ saha ॥
prajvālya dīpakāny aṣṭau kṛtvā cārghān susaṃsthitān ॥
tatas taiḥ karmabhiḥ sarvaṃ dambhapaiśunyavarjitaḥ ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktatvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
yo 'sau bhagavān sarvajagatprabhus tvaṃ
vasanti lokās tava deva dehe ॥
anena mantreṇa ca lokanātha
saṃsthāpitas tiṣṭhatu vāsudeva ॥
sarvam evaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā mama saṃsthāpanakriyām ॥
pūjyā bhāgavatāḥ sarve ye tatra samupāgatāḥ ॥
gandhamālyaiś ca dhūpaiś ca arcayitvā ca bhojanaiḥ ॥
kuryāt satkaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
etat karmavidhānaṃ ca madhukāṣṭhasya sundari ॥
dharmasaṃsthāpanārthāya etat te kathitaṃ mayā  ॥ ॥
[174]        ya etena vidhānena arcāṃ kāṣṭheṣu sthāpayet ॥
na ca gacchati saṃsāraṃ mama lokaṃ sa gacchati ॥
iti kāṣṭhārcāsthāpanam ॥
varāhapurāṇe [182, 1] ॥
varāha uvāca ॥
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi tac chṛṇuṣva vasundhare ॥
yathā tiṣṭhāmi śaileṣu pratimāyām itas tataḥ ॥
surūpāṃ ca śilāṃ dṛṣṭvā niḥśalyāṃ suparīkṣitām ॥
tatra dakṣaṃ rūpakāraṃ śīghram eva niyojayet ॥
puṣpe caiva ca nakṣatre pūrvāhṇe ca divākare ॥
śilām ālikhya varṇena śvetavarṇikayā tathā ॥
ālekhyaṃ lekhayitvā tu mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
arghaṃ sadakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā madhulājāsamanvitam ॥
dīpakaṃ ca baliṃ datvā dadhnā caivodakena ca ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhagavān sarvajagatpravīraḥ ॥
sadṛśam agnir dahanaḥ pradhānam ॥
etena mantreṇa tu vāsudeva surārividhvaṃsaka vāsudeva ॥
varāha jaya jayasva supratiṣṭhā supratiṣṭhitakīrtiḥ pravarddhatāṃ ca ॥
etena mantreṇa rūpaṃ kartavyaṃ yasya yādṛśaṃ rocate ॥
[175]        evaṃ rūpaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ramyaṃ ramyaśiloccaye [ ca ] ॥
tato vai snapanaṃ kuryāt sarvagandhasugandhibhiḥ ॥
saṃsthāpayet tatra bhūmeḥ pūrvābhimukham eva hi ॥
ahorātroṣitaś caiva śuklavastreṇa veṣṭitaḥ ॥
śuklayajñopavītī ca kṛtvā vai dantadhāvanam ॥
sarvagandhodakaṃ gṛhya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavāṃs tiṣṭhati sarvarūpa
māyāmayaṃ sarvajagatsvarūpam ॥
etena mantreṇa sarvasvarūpe
sampūjitas tiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
karaṇakāraṇa santaraṇa anagha anāde  ॥ ajarāmara sampūjya svāgatam ātmānam  ॥ anena
mantreṇa oṃ namo vāsudevāya ॥
evaṃ tu snapanaṃ kṛtvā śailārcā mama sundari ॥
tato 'dhivāsaḥ kartavyaḥ pūrvaproṣṭhapadāsu ca ॥
yo me saṃsthāpayed bhūme mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
‘[sa yāti vaiṣṇavaṃ lokaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ॥ ]
yāvakaṃ pāyasaṃ bhuktvā ahorātrāṇi kārayet ॥
tataḥ paścimasaṃdhyāyāṃ datvā vā sthānadīpakān ॥
pañcagavyaṃ ca gandhāṃś ca vāriṇā saha miśrayet ॥
[176]        caturaḥ kalaśāṃś caiva pādamūleṣu sthāpayet ॥
gītavāditraghoṣeṇa nādyaṃ tu saha kārayet ॥
[brāhmaṇaiḥ sāmagais tatra vedaghoṣan tu kārayet ॥ ]
paṭhanti brāhmaṇā mahyaṃ sāmavedaṃ guṇānvitāḥ ॥
brahmākṣarasahasrāṇi sandhāya tatra vācayet ॥
yeṣāṃ paṭhitaśabdena sarvagītasvanena ca ॥
āgamiṣyāmi kalyāṇi ātmamantraṃ paṭhet sthitaḥ ॥
evaṃ tu paṭhatāṃ teṣāṃ brāhmaṇā sāmagāyakāḥ ॥
samasaṃsthāpanārthāya paṭhanti mama karmakām ॥
tena vāditraghoṣeṇa sāmagā vājasaneyakāḥ ॥
tāny eva madhuparkeṇa gītenānyo mano........॥
niḥśabdaṃ tu tataḥ kṛtvā sthāpyo bhāgavataiḥ saha ॥
punaḥ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
āgaccha devātra svamantrayukta
pañcendriyaḥ ṣaṣṭamanaḥ svarūpa ॥
pañceṣu bhūteṣu parāvarajña
āvāsitas tiṣṭhasi lokanātha ॥
janadhāraṇakāraṇa anādimadhya
anantarūpa sampūjyase devavarapriya ॥
bhaktyā mayā bhāvita bhaktibhāva
etena mantreṇa vidhipūrvakam ॥
sarvagandhaiś ca lājaiś ca pañcagavyaiḥ punas tathā ॥
[177]        tato me sthāpane kuryād dvāri saṃtiṣṭhate mama ॥
vāditrasya ca śabdena brāhmaṇānāṃ svanena ca ॥
sarvagandhaṃ tato gṛhya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavān lakṣaṇalakṣitaś ca
lakṣmyā ca yuktaḥ satataṃ purāṇaḥ ॥
etena mantreṇa praviśya veśma
saṃtiṣṭhase śāśvata lokanātha ॥
tata etena mantreṇa prāsādaṃ ca praveśayet ॥
saṃsthāpitasya me dhātri tataḥ sthānam upārśvataḥ ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā kuryād udvartanaṃ vibho ॥
candanaṃ kuṅkumaṃ caiva miśrakālāguruṃ tathā ॥
imaṃ codvartanaṃ kṛtvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhagavān sarvajagatpradhānaḥ
sampūjito brahmabṛhaspatibhyām ॥
pravandinaḥ kāraṇamantrayuktāḥ
susvāgataṃ tiṣṭha sulokanātha ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā gandhamālyaiś ca pūjayet ॥
śuklavastrāṇi me dadyād imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
[178]        oṃ devadevasvarūpa ramyeṣu vastreṣv api yasya cittam ॥
etena mantreṇa grahāṇa vastraṃ
carasva mantreṇa purā kṛtena ॥
evaṃ vastraṃ tu me dadyād vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
dhūpaṃ ca me tato dadyāt kuṅkumāgurumiśritam ॥
evaṃ dhūpavaraṃ datvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyo varaṇa uttamaḥ ॥
bahudravyasamāyukto dhūpo 'yaṃ pratigṛyatām ॥ | svāhā ॥
oṃ asāv anādiḥ puruṣaḥ purāṇo
nārāyaṇaḥ sarvajagatpradhānaḥ ॥
manaḥ prasādaṃ sumana.... pratigṛhya dhūpam ॥
evaṃ pūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā kuryān me prāṇa.... ॥
pūrvoktena vidhānena yan mayā bhāṣitaṃ purā ॥
evaṃ me prāpaṇaṃ kṛtvā jalenācamanaṃ kuru ॥
'prāpaṇaṃ ' naivedyam ॥
śāntir bhavatu bhadraṃ te sarvasiddhārthasādhakam ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ śāntir bhavān kurvatu lokanātha
[179]                rājñaḥ sarāṣṭrasya ca brāhmaṇānām ॥
sabālavṛddheṣu gavāṃ gaṇeṣu
śāntiṃ ca kanyāsu pativratāsu ॥
naśyantu rogās tv atha ītayaś ca
bhavantu sarve muditāś ca bhūtāḥ ॥
śākeṣu vrīhiṣv atha karṣakāṇāṃ  ॥
subhikṣakālaḥ…..... nātha ॥
evaṃ tu satataṃ kṛtvā vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
pūjyabhāgavatān bhūmiṃ yathānyāyena karmaṇā ॥
bhojayed brāhmaṇāṃs tatra yathotpannaiś ca bhojanaiḥ ॥
ya etena vidhānena kuryān me sthāpanaṃ śubham ॥
tatrāhaṃ bhūmi tiṣṭhāmi etat te kāraṇaṃ mama ॥
yāvanto mama gātreṣu jalabindūn niyojayet ॥
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi mama loke sa tiṣṭhati ॥
yo māṃ tu sthāpayed bhūmi sarvāhaṅkāravarjitaḥ ॥
santāritāni kulāni tena sapta ca saptatiḥ ॥
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre śailārcāsthāpanaṃ mama ॥
dharmasandhāraṇārthāya mama bhaktasukhāvaham ॥
iti śailārcāsthāpanam ॥
[180]
atha mṛṇmayārcāsthāpanam ॥
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi tac chṛṇuṣva vasundhare ॥
pratiṣṭhāṃ mṛnmayārcāsu yathāvac ca bravīmy aham ॥
kṛtvā tu mṛnmayaṃ rūpam asphuṭaṃ cāpy akhaṇḍitam ॥
na caiva vāmanaṃ kuryān na vakraṃ nādhikaṃ tathā ॥
īdṛśīṃ pratimāṃ kṛtvā mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
sarvamūrtiṣu caivātra yathā vai rocate tathā ॥
bhūmi kāṣṭhasyābhāvena mṛnmayīṃ tatra kārayet ॥
śailajāṃ vā tato bhūmiṃ mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
tāmrakāṃsyena raupyeṇa sauvarṇatrapurītikāḥ ॥
kurvanti śubhakarmāṇaḥ kecid darbheṇa vā punaḥ ॥
arcanaṃ cāpare vedyāṃ mama karmaparigrahāt ॥
kecil lokāpavādena khyātyai kurvanti kecana ॥
kuḍye lekhye ca me kaścit paṭe kaścic ca mānavaḥ ॥
pūjayed yadi vā cakre mama tejo'ṃśasambhavam ॥
padme me pūjanaṃ śastaṃ śastaṃ caivātha sthaṇḍile ॥
bhūmi tatra hi jānīhi sthāpito 'haṃ na saṃśayaḥ ॥
sarvatra saṃpratiṣṭhāmi pūjyamānas tv ahaṃ tv ataḥ ॥
mantravad vidhipūrvāṇi yo me karmāṇi kārayet ॥
[181]        evam etan mahābhāge mayā samparikīrtitam ॥
mantreṇa vidhipūrveṇa yo ‘rcayen mama sundari ॥
yaś ca bhaktiprayatnena karmaṇaḥ parikīrtitam ॥
mama caiva prasādena sa sarvagata eva ca ॥
satyākāśe 'pi tiṣṭhet tu kṛtvā bhaktiṃ mamāntike ॥
dadyāj jalāñjaliṃ mahyaṃ yadīcched ātmanaḥ kriyām ॥
kiṃ te puṣpeṇa japyena sahasreṇāyutena ca ॥
yasya praśastaṃ cittaṃ ca mānasaṃ na ca lālasam ॥
evam etan mahābhāge mahāguhyaṃ prakīrtitam ॥
arcāṃ tu mṛnmayīṃ kṛtvā siddhāṃ supariniṣṭhitām ॥
śravaṇe caiva nakṣatre kuryāt tasyādhivāsanam ॥
apramattair gṛhītavyā yathā bhūmiṃ na gacchati ॥
snāpayet tu yathānyāyaṃ sampūjya vidhipūrvakam ॥
pañcagavyaṃ ca gandhāṃś ca vāriṇā saha miśrayet ॥
tato me snapanaṃ kṛtvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
yo 'sau bhagavān sarvajagatpradhāno
yasya prasādena dhavanti lokāḥ ॥
āyātu me śīghraṃ kuru prasādaṃ
[182]                tiṣṭhasva arcāsu ca mṛnmayīṣu ॥
kāraṇakāraṇaguhyamūrtte
bhajasvemāṃ pratimāṃ 'mamejyāṃ  ॥
namo namas te sahasramūrte tejasvinimāṃ
pratimāṃ mahāpuruṣāya namo namaḥ ॥
tato 'py etena mantreṇa veśma praveśma saṃsthāpayitvā ॥
evaṃ praveśanaṃ kṛtvā vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpayet tatra dadyād upari nirmitam ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpayitvā ca pūrvabhāgena niścalaḥ ॥
caturaḥ kalaśān gṛhya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ varuṇa sumudra lokasampūjito hy ātmagatiprasannaḥ ॥
etena mantreṇa mamābhiṣekaṃ prāpnuhi variṣṭha vāsudeva ॥
ūrdhvabāhur agnir agnir bhūmir āpo
bhavanti yasmāt tasmai namo namaḥ ॥
puruṣottamāyeti ॥
etenābhiṣecanaṃ kṛtvā mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
pūrvanyāyena he bhūmi gandhamālyaiḥ samarcayet ॥
aguruṃ kuṅkumaṃ caiva candanaṃ surasaṃ tathā ॥
namo nārāyaṇāyeti dhūpaṃ tatra pradāpayet ॥
dhūpaṃ datvā yathānyāyaṃ pītaṃ vastraṃ pradāpayet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ dhūpeṣu dīpeṣu sadā prasanno
yena prasannena jagatprasannam ॥
gṛhṇīṣva vastraṃ sukhasuprasannam
idaṃ ca tiṣṭhati sadā prayatnaḥ ॥
[183]        tata etena mantreṇa vastraṃ dadyād yathāvidhi ॥
pūrvanyāyena mantreṇa imāni karmāṇi kārayet ॥
kṛtvā tu vipulaṃ karma yan mayā parikīrtitam ॥
tato me snapanaṃ dadyāt pūrvanyāyena saṃskṛtam ॥
evaṃ tatra vidhiṃ kṛtvā dāpayet tatra prāpaṇam ॥
paścād ācamanaṃ dadyāt tatra mantram udāharet ॥
oṃ śāntir devānāṃ śāntir brāhmaṇānāṃ
śāntir astu me rāṣṭrakasya ॥
me śāntir vardhatāṃ śāntir gavāṃ śāntiś ca garbhiṇīnām ॥
śāntiś catuṣpade śāntir dhānyarāśe vrīhau śāntiḥ ॥
tato ‘py etena mantreṇa śāntiṃ kṛtvā mahaujasīm ॥
paścād bhāgavatān pūjayed yathānyāyaṃ svaśaktitaḥ ॥
evaṃ bhāgavatān pūjya brāhmaṇāṃś cātha pūjayet ॥
tato 'bhivādanaṃ kuryād upapannena bhojanam ॥
brāhmaṇāṃs tu tathā pūjya gṛhe śāntyudakaṃ tathā ॥
dadyāc chirasi me bhūmi yadīcchet siddhim uttamām ॥
evaṃ visarjanaṃ kṛtvā etāṃ bhūmim upāgatāḥ ॥
pūjayec ca guruṃ tatra yadīcchet karma śāśvatam ॥
yo guruṃ pūjayed bhaktyā vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ॥
tenāhaṃ pūjito nityaṃ bhūmi satyaṃ bravīmi te ॥
nedaṃ mamaiva śāstreṣu yad idaṃ vacanaṃ śubhe ॥
sarvaśāstreṣu kalyāṇi guruḥ pūjyo vyavasthitaḥ ॥
[184]        ya etena vidhānena kuryāt saṃsthāpanaṃ mama ॥
tāritāś ca kulās tena trīṇi triṃśac ca saptatiḥ ॥
yāvato jalabindūṃś ca mama gātreṣu yojayet ॥
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi mama loke sa tiṣṭhati ॥
ya etena vidhānena kuryād vai mṛnmayasya ca ॥
kathayiṣyāmi te hy anyat sarvaṃ bhagavataḥ priyam ॥
iti mṛnmayārcāsthāpanam ॥
atha tāmrārcāsthāpanam ॥
tāmrī tu pratimāṃ kṛtvā surūpāṃ ca subhāsvarām ॥
ucitenopacāreṇa tato veśma upānayet ॥
tato veśmany upāgamya sthāpayitvā udaṅmukham ॥
tataś citrāsunakṣatre kuryāt tatrādhivāsanam ॥
lājābhiḥ sarvagandhaiś ca pañcagavyena miśritaiḥ ॥
snāpayec ca tato māṃ vai imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavāṃs tiṣṭhati sākṣibhūta
āgaccha mūrtau saha paṃcabhūtaiḥ ॥
guhyapramāṇaṃ bhagavān puruṣottama śāśvata ॥
[185]        anenaiva tu mantreṇa sthāpayitvā yaśasvini ॥
pūrvanyāyena me tatra bhūmikarmāṇi kārayet ॥
vyatītāyāṃ tu śarvaśram udite tu divākare ॥
diśāsu ca prasannāsu snapanaṃ tatra kārayet ॥
brāhmaṇaiḥ paṭhyamāneṣu gītavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ ॥
maṅgalyāni ca vastūni dvāramūleṣu sthāpayet ॥
dvāramūlam upāgamya sarvagandhasugandhibhiḥ ॥
tato me snapanaṃ kuryād imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhagavān sarvavaraḥ prabhuś ca
māyābalo yogabalapradhānaḥ ॥
āyāhi āyāhi imāṃ ca mūrti
santiṣṭha tāmreṣv api lokanātha ॥
jvalana jvālana tapana tāpana svayaṃ tiṣṭha ॥
oṃ namo bhagavate puruṣottamāyeti 'sūktam ॥
tato dvāram upāgamya śīghraṃ veśma praveśayet ॥
vedyāṃ praveśanaṃ kṛtvā yuktyā dhyāyeta saṃskṛtam ॥
svapanaṃ ca tato dadyāt sarvaratnodakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
sarvagandhavimiśraiś ca manojñasukhaśītalaiḥ ॥
evaṃ māṃ snāpayitvā tu mama śāstraviśāradaḥ ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
ākāśaprakāśa, jagatprakāśa, āśāsu sarvāsu
[186]        etena mantreṇa kṛtādhivāsaḥ
sampūjitas tiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
apadapadamahāpada uttiṣṭha
bhagavan namaḥ puruṣottamāyeti sūtram ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā mama śāstrānusārataḥ ॥
pūrvanyāyena māṃ tatra gandhamālyaiḥ samarcayet ॥
mamārcanavidhiṃ kṛtvā sugandhaiḥ puṣpadāmabhiḥ ॥
śuklavastraṃ samādāya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ ekabhūta .... gṛhīṣva vastrāṇi me
mantram upāgatas tiṣṭhasva bhūtanātha ॥
pūtaḥ pavitraḥ sa tu sarva siddhaḥ ॥
parāparaś cāprameyāya namaḥ puruṣottamāyeti sūktam ॥
vastrair vibhūṣitaṃ kṛtvā mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
yathānyāyena me śīghram arcanaṃ tatra kārayet ॥
arcitālaṅkṛte kṛtvā sugandhaṃ dhūpamiśritam ॥
pūrvaṃ nyāyena me dadyāt prāpaṇaṃ lokabhāvanam ॥
datvā tu prāpaṇaṃ hy evaṃ dadyād ācamanaṃ vibhoḥ ॥
gobrāhmaṇaśivārthāya śāntimantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ śāntir devānāṃ śāntir viprāṇāṃ
śāntī rājñaḥ sarāṣṭrakasya ॥
[187]        śāntir bālakānāṃ vrīhipaṇyānāṃ śāntiḥ ॥
śāntir atha garbhiṇīnāṃ ca [dehinām] ॥
evaṃ śāntim upāgamya yaṣṭurasya yaśodhane ॥
guruṃ bhāgavataṃ caivam arcayec ca yathoditam ॥
evaṃ sampūjitān kṛtvā mama śāstrānusāriṇaḥ ॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayet tatra yathotpannena mādhavi ॥
tataḥ śāntyudakaṃ kāryaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo vasundhare ॥
mūrdhni śāntyudakaṃ dadyād yadīcchet siddhim uttamam ॥
sarvān pūjya visarjitvā kuryāt pādābhivādanam ॥
guruṃ sampūjayet paścād dānaiḥ saṃskaraṇena ca ॥
tenāhaṃ pūjito bhūmi satyaṃ satyaṃ bravīmi te ॥
gurur yasya na tuṣṭo vai mama satyakṛtena vā ॥
nāhaṃ tatrāvatiṣṭhāmi punaḥ satyaṃ bravīmi te ॥
ya etena vidhānena kuryāt saṃsthāpanaṃ mama ॥
tāritāni kulāni tena navatiḥ saptaviṃśatiḥ ॥
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre tāmrārcāsthāpanaṃ mama ॥
kathayiṣyāmi te hīdaṃ kāṃsyī yatra pravartate ॥
yāvanti jalabindūni mama snāneṣu sundari ॥
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi mama loke mahīyate ॥
iti tāmrapratimāsthāpanam ॥
[188]
atha kāṃsyapratimāsthāpanam ॥
kāṃsyīṃ tu pratimāṃ kṛtvā surūpāṃ sunirūpitām ॥
sarvāṅgāvayavair yuktāṃ vimalāṃ karminirmitām ॥
tāṃ tu jyeṣṭhāsu nakṣatre mama veśmany upānayet ॥
gītavāditraśabdena śaṅkhatūryaravais tathā ॥
arghaṃ gṛhya yathānyāyam imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
yo 'sau bhavān sarvayajñeṣu pūjyo
dhyeyo goptā viśvakāyo mahātmā ॥
prasannātmā bhagavān me prasannaḥ
sampūjitas tiṣṭha hi lokanātha ॥
arghaṃ datvā yathānyāyaṃ sthāpayed uttarāmukham ॥
yathānyāyena sthāpayitvārcayitvā tataḥ punaḥ ॥
kṛtvādhivāsanaṃ pūrvaṃ mahāsannidhikārakam ॥
catvāraḥ kalaśāś caiva pañcagavyasamāyutāḥ ॥
sarvagandhāṃś ca lājāṃś ca priyaṅgukesarāṇi ca ॥
tataś cāstaṃgate sūrye śuddhair bhāgavataiḥ saha ॥
snapanaṃ kurute bhūmi mama sthāpanajāṃ kriyām ॥
gṛhītvā kalaśāṃs tatra śuddho bhāgavatas tathā ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
[189] mantraḥ ॥
oṃ ādir bhavān brahmayugāntarūpaḥ
sarveṣu kāleṣv api kalpabhūtaḥ ॥
eko bhavan nāsti kaścid dvitīya
upāgatas tiṣṭha hi lokanātha ॥
akāra makāra chandorūpa kṣaramakṣara dhṛtirūpam iti namaḥ puruṣottamāyeti sūktam ॥
vyatītāyāṃ tu śarvaryām udite tu divākare ॥
śvetena tu muhūrtena prāpte kāle śubhottare ॥
pūrvoktena vidhānena mama śāstrānudarśanaḥ ॥
sthāpayed dvāramūleṣu veśmano mama sundari ॥
sa candanodakaṃ gṛhya sarvagandhaphalāni ca ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ indro bhavāmṃs tvaṃ ca yamaḥ kubero
jaleśvaraḥ somabṛhaspatīś ca ॥
śukraḥ śanaiśvaraḥ rāhur budhaś ca
āditya agnis tvaṃ śītaraśmis tathaiva ॥
tathaiva sarvauṣayo jalāni
mārutaḥ sarvaśarīraḥ śāstā ॥
dehe vasanto namaḥ puruṣottamāyeti sūktam ॥
[190]        sarvadevanamaskāraṃ mantreṇānena kārayet ॥
tata etena mantreṇa kṛtvā karma suduṣkaram ॥
apramādena ca tathā mama veśma praveśayet ॥
praveśayitvā tadveśma ekānte mama karmakṛt ॥
svapanaṃ ca yathānyāyaṃ yathā doṣo na vidyate ॥
nirdoṣaṃ tu tato jñātvā savyaṃ pārśvaṃ tato nayet ॥
sarvaratnasamāyuktaṃ sarvagandhodakena ca ॥
caturaḥ kalaśāṃs tatra mama śāstrānusāriṇaḥ ॥
pūrvanyāyena māṃ tatra arcayitvā yathoditam ॥
arcitālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā mālyagandhādilepanaiḥ ॥
paścād vastrāṇi me dadyān mama gātravibhūṣaṇam ॥
ānayitvā tatas tatra sthāpayitvā mamāgrataḥ ॥
ubhau tu caraṇau natvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ vastrāṇi vastrabhūtasarveṣu bhūteṣu ca labdha vastrāṇi gṛhṇīṣva ca lokanātha ॥
vedopaveda-ṛgveda-yajurveda-sāmaveda-atharvaveda saṃstute namaḥ
paramparāyeti sūktam ॥
arcitālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā sarvabhāvena sundari ॥
paścān me prāpaṇaṃ dadyān mantravad vidhipūrvakam ॥
prāpaṇe tu kṛte tatra pūrvamantrasamanvite ॥
paścād ācamanaṃ dadyāt śāntiṃ cottarāṃ tathā ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
[191] mantraḥ ॥
oṃ kurvato brāhmaṇāś ca varṇāś catvāro ye mahāś cāṣṭa eva sarve tatra saritaḥ sāgarāś ca kurvato ye mayā pūrvam uktāḥ ॥ āryāsādhya kāmadadhāmadaṃ oṃ namaḥ puruṣottamayeti sūktam ॥
vṛtteṣv evopacāreṣu mama kuryāt pradakṣiṇam ॥
abhivādanaṃ ca me kuryāt kuryāt sarvābhivādanam ॥
tat tu kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ pūjyo bhāgavataḥ śuciḥ ॥
teṣāṃ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā pūjanīyā dvijātayaḥ ॥
teṣāṃ śāntyudakaṃ gṛhya dvijānāṃ kamalekṣaṇe ॥
dadyād abhyukṣaṇaṃ mahyaṃ tenāhaṃ pūjito 'bhavam ॥
sarvān visarjayitvā tu yathā te samupāgatāḥ ॥
guruṃ sampūjayet tatra dānasaṃmānanais tathā ॥
yo guruṃ pūjayed bhaktyā bhūmidānena sundari ॥
tenāhaṃ pūjito bhūmi evam etan na saṃśayaḥ ॥
brāhmaṇān mama bhaktāṃś ca gurūn (?) naivābhinandati ॥
nāśayāmi na sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ bravīmi te ॥
yāvanti jalabindūni mama gātreṣu tiṣṭhati ॥
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi sthāpako mayi saṃsthitaḥ ॥
ya etena vidhānena bhūmi saṃsthāpayen mama ॥
tāritaṃ svakulaṃ tena evam etan na saṃśayaḥ ॥
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre kāṃsyārcāsthāpanaṃ mayā ॥
kathayiṣyāmi te hy evaṃ rūpaṃ yat tat pravartate ॥
iti kāṃsyārcāsthāpanam ॥
[192]
atha raupyasauvarṇapratimāsthāpanam ॥
rājatīṃ pratimāṃ kṛtvā surūpāṃ nirmalāṃ śuciḥ ॥
aśliṣṭāṃ caiva nirdoṣāṃ sarvataḥ pariveṣṭitām ॥
candrapāṇḍurasaṅkāśāṃ suślakṣṇāṃ nirbraṇāṃ śubhām ॥
śriyā yuktāṃ manojñāṃ ca dīpyamānāṃ diśo daśa ॥
īdṛśīṃ pratimāṃ kṛtvā mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ॥
arghaṃ gṛhya ca sampūjya imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yaḥ sarvaloke pratilabdham arghyaḥ
pūjyaś ca mānyaś ca divaukasām api ॥
upāgato gṛhṇa idaṃ mamārghyaṃ
prasīda saṃtiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
yo rājate dharmapatiś ca yajñe
sūryo yamaḥ karmāgnihotram ॥
mantras ceti ādimadhyasvarūpāyeti sūktam ॥
tata etena mantreṇa ardha datvā yathāvidhi ॥
vinato bahutoyaiś ca sthāpayed uttarāmukham ॥
āśleṣāsu ca nakṣatre rākṣau karkaṭake sthite ॥
astaṃgate dinakare japati svajane tadā ॥
tatrādhivāsanaṃ kuryād vidhivan mantraniścitam ॥
catvāraḥ kalaśās tatra candanodakapūritāḥ ॥
sarvagandhaiś ca ratnaiś ca sahakāravibhūṣitāḥ ॥
tatas te karmiṇaḥ sarve mama śāstrānusāriṇaḥ ॥
[193]        gurus tu candanaṃ devi manojñaṃ sukhaśītalam ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavān sarvalokaikakarttā āyātu lokanātha ॥
merudhruvatārakā akṣararūpa ॥ kṣamādamaḥ kālakalāmuhūrtā ūrdhvānyadho mokṣa me ॥
vyatītāyāṃ tu śarvaryām udite sūryamaṇḍale ॥
diśāsu ca prasannāsu dvāramūleṣu sthāpayet ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā mama śāstrānusāriṇaḥ ॥
ghaṭaiḥ pūrṇair yathānyāyaṃ kuryus tatrābhiṣecanam ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
oṃ yo 'sau gaganāya surabhilokaḥ
saṃdṛśyate naiva praṇaśyate yaḥ ॥
adṛṣṭanaṣṭaś ca gṛhāgameti
sānugrahas tiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
paraḥ parāṇāṃ vā parāyaṇaṃ hantā harttā parivartyamānaś ceti ॥
namaḥ anantāyeti sūktam ॥
tato 'bhiṣecanaṃ kṛtvā mama śāstrānusāriṇaḥ ॥
tatas tūṣṇīṃ vinītaś ca vedimadhye 'vasthāpayet ॥
[194]        tato māṃ sarvataḥ paśyet sarvadevair vivarjitam ॥
dyutimān matimāṃś caiva vidhimantreṇa sthāpayet ॥
punaḥ kartavyaṃ me bhūmi sthāpanaṃ cātiśobhanam ॥
yenāhaṃ cātra tiṣṭhāmi mantrapūtena saṃmatam ॥
kalaśāṃs tato gṛhītvā yathānyāyena pūritān ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo vedavedāṅgavarābhiṣikto
sarveṣu yajñeṣv api bhāgabhoktā ॥
ijyo yathā yajamānasya yo vai
mamānugrahāya tiṣṭhasva lokanātha ॥
varavara pavana manaḥ saṃvaraṇa, namaḥ puruṣottamāyeti sūktam ॥
evaṃ saṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā prahṛṣṭenāntarātmanā ॥
arcaprati yathānyāyaṃ yan mayā pūrvabhāṣitam ॥
arcitālaṅkṛtaṃ kṛtvā hṛṣṭapuṣṭamanās tathā ॥
śvetavastrāṇi me dadyāt priyaṃ ca mama bhūṣaṇam ॥
tato vastram upādāya jānubhyāṃ nipated bhuvi ॥
namo nārāyaṇety uktvā imaṃ mantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau bhavāṃś cāndrakiraṇaprakāśaḥ
[195]                śaṅkhasya kundasya ca varṇacoraḥ ॥
kṣaraudadhikaumudavarṇa deva
vastrāṇi gṛhṇīṣva ca lokanātha ॥
veṣaḥ suveṣaḥ sulabhadurlabha śreṣṭha suvarcā iti namo nārāyaṇeti sūktam ॥
tata etena mantreṇa kṛtvā ca vastrālaṅkṛtam ॥
tato me prāpaṇaṃ dadyāt kuṇḍale tāraṇe (?) śubhe ॥
datvā tu prāpaṇaṃ hy evam ācāryaḥ śāstrasaṃyutaḥ ॥
sarvalokahitārthāya śāntimantram udāharet ॥
mantraḥ ॥
oṃ yo 'sau brahmā devasya puraḥ śāntirudraya viṣṇoḥ ॥
śāntiḥ lokānāṃ ye mayā pūrvam uktāḥ ॥
acala cañcala -khevala- tarala- pracala- aravinda prabho te namaḥ
saṃsthāpito vāsudeva iti sūktam ॥
evaṃ śāntiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm ॥
pūjya bhāgavatāṃs tatra yathānyāyena sātvikān ॥
tataḥ sampūjanaṃ kṛtvā mama bhaktyā vyavasthitaḥ ॥
brāhmaṇān pūjayet tatra prayatnena ca bhojayet ॥
tataḥ sampūjya viprāṇāṃ gṛhya śāntyudakaṃ śubham ॥
kuryād abhyukṣaṇaṃ tatra mama karma sukhāvaham ॥
brāhmaṇān svajanāṃś caiva abhivādya kṛtāñjaliḥ ॥
[196]        śīghraṃ visarjayet tatra ye tathā samupāgatāḥ ॥
visarjya vacanaṃ sarvaṃ guruṃ tatra prapūjayet ॥
sa mānadānasatkārair ācchādadānakaiḥ ॥
guruṃ sampūjayed bhūmi brāhmāṃś ca mama priyān ॥
bhaktibhir bhaktiyuktāṃś ca taiś cālam atipūjitaḥ ॥
yāvanti jalabindūni mama gātreṣu yojayet ॥
tāvadvarṣasahasreṇa viṣṇulokeṣu tiṣṭhati ॥
ya etena vidhānena bhūmisaṃsthāpayen mama ॥
tāritāḥ svakulās tena mama karmasu saṃmitāḥ ॥
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre raupyārcāsthāpanaṃ mayā ॥
sauvarṇasya ca vakṣyāmi sthāpanaṃ bhūmi me priyam ॥
iti raupyārcāsthāpanam ॥
[197]
atha devīpratiṣṭhā ॥ 14 ॥
daśabhujādevīpratiṣṭhā ॥
[tatra] devīpurāṇe |
gaṅgā narmadā vindhyādrau ujjayiny atha vārbude ॥
himavanniṣadhe [?] droṇe sāṃnidhye tiṣṭhate śivā ॥
nadītīre caturmārge rathyāpitṛvaneṣu ca ॥
sthāpitā tu bhaved devī sarvakāmārthasiddhidā ॥
māraṇe śatruvargasya [?] pitṛsthāne sadārcitā ॥
ekaliṅge drume śaile gṛhe goṭhe trikaṇṭake ॥
pūjitā dravyaputrāyuḥ sukhadārogyadā bhavet ॥
sarvadā sarvagā devī mokṣadā pūjitā matam ॥
tathā |
hemarājatamṛddhārkṣī [?] śailacitrāyasāpi vā ॥
śaktiśūle sthitā devī sarvakāmaphalapradā ॥
yady asya āyudhaṃ proktaṃ tasmiṃs taṃ pūjayet tataḥ ॥
devī śaktyarcitā puṃsāṃ rājyāyuḥ sukhasaukhyadā ॥
yāmye brahmā bhavet koṭau raktavarṇo rajomayaḥ ॥
madhye rudro ṛbhuḥ śuklo vāme kṛṣṇatamo hariḥ ॥
vedayajñagrahā nāma lokeśāḥ sacarācarāḥ ॥
śūle sampūjite vatsa sarvaṃ bhavati pūjitaḥ ॥
[198]        vārkṣīṃ vā śailajāṃ vāpi ratnadhātumayīm api ॥
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena daśabāhuṃ trilocanām ॥
kārayed bhaktimān yas tu devīṃ śāstraviśāradaḥ ॥
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
rājatīm uttamāṅgena kabarī lambitena ca ॥
snigdhakuñcitabhāreṇa dhammillena sitena ca ॥
vyāmiśreṇa sitaiḥ puṣpair aliṅktayeva [?] saṃsthitām ॥
tamo nudanto vakreṇa tiraskṛtaniśākarām ॥
āyataiḥ karṇamaryādais taralā lokanirmitaiḥ ॥
bhrūkṣepād ullasatyakṣyai [?] ṛjujihvāvalokitaiḥ ॥
bhrūbhaṅgacāpadaṇḍena bhindantīṃ dṛṣṭisāyakaiḥ ॥
madhyonnatasamāyātām adhareṇa virājitām ॥
āraktavidrumābhena kiṃcitsa ... smitānanā ॥
dantamayūkhajyotsnābhir bhāsamānā taḍid yathā ॥
trireṣakandharā bhāti graiveyakavibhūṣitā ॥
kaṭhinastanabhāreṇa sasaktau tāv arandhrakau ॥
stanau samunnatau pīnau vakṣaḥ pīnonnataṃ śubham ॥
bhūṣitā stanamadhyena madhye ca trivalībhṛtā ॥
romarājī nitambārddham anaṅgāṅkurasūcitam ॥
keyūranāgabandhena hy aṅgadaiḥ ratnakāñcanaiḥ ॥
graiveyakakirīṭe ‘rddhe [?] avikena viśeṣakam ॥
[199]        virajitā tṛtīyena bhālake locanena ca ॥
rājantī pītavastreṇa sphuritāruṇareṇukā ॥
dvibhujādyā ca viṃśāṣṭāṃ yāvad dordaṇḍakalpanā [?] ॥
asikheṭakahastābhyāṃ gadādaṇḍena cāparau ॥
śaracāpe 'parābhyāṃ ca ṛṣṭikahuriṇau [?] parau ॥
śaṅkhaghaṇṭānvitau cāsyau [?] dhvajvajradharau parau ॥
paraśucakradharau cānyau sarvādarśinau parau ॥
śaktikuntadhṛtau cānyau halamusalinau parau ॥
paraśutomaramanyābhyāṃ ḍhakkāpaṇavinā parau ॥
tarjayanty apare nīrakṛtakolahalā pare ॥
abhayasvastike dvābhyām aṣṭāviṃśabhujā śivā ॥
śubhrapadmāsanasthā tu siṃhāsanam adhiṣṭhitā ॥
mahiṣaghnī śiracchedā naraṃ śastrograpāṇinā ॥
garjamānaṃ hataṃ mūrdhni nāgapāśena veṣṭinam ॥
upaghnantīṃ ripuṃ devī pūjanīyā purandara ॥
sthāpitā pūjitā śaka smaritā pāṭhināpi vā ॥
prayacchati śubhān kāmān manobhīṣṭān yajan naraḥ ॥
pakveṣṭe śailakāṣṭe vā mṛnmaye vāpi vāsava  ॥ ॥
śuklavāsāḥ śuciḥ snātvā kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ ॥
sarvasaṃjñojjhitaḥ pītastanmanābhāvabhāvitaḥ [?] ॥
puṣpagandhopahāraiś ca haviṣyānnair anekaśaḥ ॥
tilasarpiryavān huvā sarvamaṅgalamantritān ॥
[200]        vastrahemāmbusaṃvītaiḥ kalaśaiḥ snapayec chivām ॥
tatas tu śāstravijñaiś ca pratiṣṭhāṃ tatra kārayet ॥
devī śāstrārthatattvajñair mātṛmaṇḍalavedakaiḥ ॥
bhūtatantragṛhāvālagāruḍeṣu kṛtaśramaiḥ ॥
pratiṣṭhāpya śivāṃ tais tu yathāśaktyā tu dakṣayet ॥
pūjayed brāhmaṇān śakra kanyā bālāṃs tathaiva ca ॥
dīnāndhavikalān sarvān yathāśaktyā kṣamāpayet ॥
tatas te svastivācyaṃ tu maṅgalā prīyatāṃ mama ॥
sukhaṃ tiṣṭhantu rājāno gobrāhmaṇagaṇas tathā ॥
kṣatraviṭśūdravālebhyaḥ [?] sarvaśāntikarā bhavaḥ ॥
tathā stavena cānyena śivagītena toṣayet ॥
iti daśabhujā devīpratiṣṭhā ॥
atha mātṛṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā ॥
agastya uvāca ॥
pure vā yadi vā grāme nagare kheṭake 'pi vā ॥
mātaraḥ pūjanīyās tu dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalārthibhiḥ ॥
ekaliṅge drume śaile nadītīre vane 'pi vā ॥
[201]        pūjitāḥ sarvavidyānāṃ sādhakāya phalapradāḥ ॥
kālāntarapradā devyā sarvāḥ sarvatra pūjitāḥ ॥
kālāgnir iva paryantāṃ tāsāṃ vyāptisuputradāḥ ॥
nagaradvārapaurādivṛddhirājyasukhārthadāḥ ॥
gaṅgātīre 'thavā vindhye sarvakāmaphalapradāḥ ॥
vedaparvatalakṣmyādi kiṣkindhā arbudādiṣu ॥
svargadā mokṣadā vatsa niṣkāmā phalavāñchitā ॥
ete sthānāḥ samākhyātā samīhitaphalapradāḥ ॥
yāsāṃ kālāgniśivaparyantāṃ yāsāṃ vyāptir mahātmanām ॥
tā yatra yatra pūjyante tatraiva phaladāyikāḥ ॥
tadarthaṃ śubhaṛkṣāhe dārv ānīya tu buddhimān ॥
maṇimauktikavaiḍūryakāṣṭacandanacandanāḥ ॥
madhūkapārthabilvāmraśākaśiṃśipatindukāḥ ॥
śailapārthivahemotthā tāratāmrāḥ śubhapradāḥ ॥
tadvaidair ghaṭitā vatsa tadvaiś caiva pratiṣṭhitāḥ ॥
tāś cottarānanāḥ sthāpyāḥ sarvakāmaphalepsubhiḥ ॥
sarvāṃ śaileṣṭakāṣṭhotthaṃ grahaṃ vāstuvibhājitam ॥
valabhīmaṇḍapaṃ vatsa maṭhe vā sthāpanaṃ śubham ॥
gandhanaivedyadhūpena maṇimālyavibhūṣaṇaiḥ ॥
[202]        adhivāsanapūrvaṃ tu pratiṣṭhāpyās tu tadvidhaiḥ ॥
vedadhvanimahāghoṣaiḥ strīsaṅgītopaśobhitam ॥
kartavyaṃ sthāpanaṃ tāsāṃ bahuvāditravāditam ॥
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatraiva śubhamaṅgalaiḥ ॥
ākhyānair mātarā śāstrair rudrakrīḍākathiaḥ śubhaiḥ ॥
daśāvatārapūrvās tu yathāvācyā yathākramam ॥
evaṃ tūṣasi samprāpte baliṃ sandhyāsu dāpayet ॥
tathā mātṛgaṇaṃ pūjya devaṃ bhairavarūpiṇam ॥
strīsaṅghāḥ kanyakā viprāḥ pūjanīyāś ca śaktitaḥ ॥
maṭhaṃ ca kārayet tatra devyāḥ pūjārthasiddhaye ॥
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇaṃ sarvopakaraṇānvitam ॥
vāpīkūpataḍāgaṃ vā vāhikāvanaśobhitam  ॥ ॥
veśyātūryoparampannaṃ [-saṃpannam?] dhvajachatravibhūṣitam ॥
ghaṇṭādarpaṇadīpādyā deyā dravyānurūpataḥ ॥
ghaṭikā yantraṣaṣṭyādi dinasaṅkhyārthasiddhaye ॥
kartavyam ekam ekaṃ vā yathākālaṃ paricchadam ॥
anena vidhinā yas tu mātaraḥ sthāpayen naraḥ ॥
ihaiva pūjanīyā syān mṛto yāti parāṃ gatim ॥
ye tasya dāsakarmāntā dāsyo veśyādikā gṛhe ॥
te ‘pi yānti divaṃ vatsa kiṃ punas tasya bāndhavāḥ ॥
iti mātṛṇāṃ pratiṣṭhā ॥
[203]
atha catuḥṣaṣṭidevīrūpakam ॥
tathā dakṣiṇabhāge tu bhairavasthāpitaḥ pure ॥
kurute mahatīṃ pīḍām agnidāhaṃ sudāruṇam ॥
visūtrā mātaro yatra sthāpitā bhairavasya tu ॥
kaṣṭā paṅktiyutā vātha rurudāhas tadā bhavet ॥
tasya śāntyaiva kurvīta sammukhīṃ sarvamaṅgalām ॥
śriyaḥ kāryā viśeṣeṇa yogakṣemagṛhe śubhām ॥
keśavo 'py atha vā sthāpyaḥ śriyā sārddhaṃ surottama ॥
anena vidhinā śakra sthāpitāḥ sukhadā śubhāḥ ॥
tathā ॥
yat tat sarvadevādhidevatvaṃ liṅgarūpaṃ śivātmakam ॥
bhairavaṃ bhairavāsiddham avyaktaṃ parameśvaram ॥
kathaṃ tat kena vidhinā kasmin sthāne ca pūjayet ॥
etad veditum icchāmi kathayasva prasādataḥ ॥
avyaktaṃ yat paraṃ tejaḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtam ॥
liṅgaṃ tallayavā [?] teṣāṃ sarvaśāstreṣu kīrtitam ॥
taṃ kṛtvā liṅgarūpaṃ tu mama nātha surārcitam ॥
ātmabhāvānusāreṇa nadītīre niveśayet ॥
pure vā dakṣiṇe sthāne gaurī vātha catuṣpade ॥
dvārabandheṣu tatsthāne anyasmin vā manorame ॥
mahābhairavamantreṇa sthāpayed bhairavaṃ budhaḥ ॥
[204]        bhairavena tu talliṅgaṃ bhairavīṃ siddhim icchatā ॥
arcitaṃ ardhanārīśaṃ maṅgalā bhairavīyutam ॥
śivadūtyādividyābhiḥ kartavyaṃ parivāritam ॥
bhairavaṃ paramaṃ devaṃ sarvakāmaprasādhanam ॥
pūjitaṃ mantravidhinā sarvāvāptiphalāvaham ॥
dharmadaṃ cārthadaṃ śakra kāmadaṃ mokṣadaṃ nṛṇām ॥
śāntidaṃ devam āyuṣyaṃ rājyadaṃ sutadaṃ nṛṇām ॥
mahāsiddhiphalaṃ liṅgaṃ kūṭarūpaṃ parātparam ॥
jape home 'rcane vātha paraṃ vā nityasiddhidam ॥
nṛpāṇāṃ nṛpayoṣāṇāṃ kartavyaṃ sarvadā śubham ॥
liṅgavālāya gṛhiṇaṃ prasannaṃ samudāhṛtam ॥
gandhādiphalapuṣpādyaiḥ svādubhakṣyanivedanaiḥ ॥
maṇihemādivastrādyaiḥ pūjitaṃ sarvakāmadam ॥
śāntipuṣṭirasākājaiḥ stambhamāraṇasādanam ॥
uccāṭanaṃ mohanaṃ kuryāt kīlanaṃ parameśvaraḥ ॥
śuddharūpo yato devaḥ sthānabhāvaphalapradaḥ ॥
ādhārādheyarūpeṇa sādhayet tarhi taṃ nṛṇām ॥
utpātamakarakṣobhāv upasargopaśāntiṣu ॥
sthāpitaḥ pūjito devo bhayaṃ hanyān na saṃśayaḥ ॥
pratidevapurasthāne pratibhedeṣu vā nṛpaiḥ ॥
[205]        kṛte vā pūrvadevānāṃ prāsādānāṃ tathocchraye ॥
yad bhayaṃ nṛparāṣṭreṣu tac chamaṃ yāti niścayāt ॥
śambhoḥ sthānaṃ paraṃ śakra pure vā nagare kvacit ॥
ucchrāyaṃ labhate liṅgaṃ na cānyā pratimā kvacit ॥
śivā śyāmā madhyamā devī pretasaṃsthā kapālinī ॥
sā śūlakodarakṣāmā carcikā ripunāśinī ॥
caṇḍamuṇḍavināśāya pūjitā surasattamaiḥ ॥
madanukṛtisvarūpā tu kāmadā sthāpitā bhuvi ॥
pure vā nagare vāpi madho tadvārage 'pi vā ॥
nṛpageheṣu pūjyā sā sarvakāmapradāyikā ॥
ye yajanti sadā lokā śailīṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi ॥
te labhante 'kṣayān kośān yad abhīṣṭān surottama ॥
eṣā devī parā vidyā lakṣmīrūpā nagopari ॥
sarvakāmapradā proktā pāvake mahiṣopamā ॥
śivāsyā dakṣiṇāśastā cāmuṇḍā rākṣase śubhā ॥
paścime carcikā devī vāyavye mṛgagā śubhā ॥
uttare kṣemadā śastā maṅgalā parvaṇā śubhā ॥
uttare kṣemadā saṃsthā maṅgalāṃ pūrvadā śubhām ॥
sarvapāpa ... ……. madhyagaḥ ॥
pañcadevyaḥ prakartavyā yathābhāgaṃ vyavasthitāḥ ॥
[206]        mātaraḥ sarvataḥ śastāḥ śivo yāmye śubhāvahā ॥
paścime śasyate brahmā uttareṇa janārdanaḥ ॥
pūrvato bhāskaraḥ kāryo aiśānyāṃ sarvadevatāḥ ॥
caturmukhaḥ prakartavyaś caturmūrtiś ca madhyataḥ ॥
tathā ॥
ekaikā maṅgalā devī sā umā śaktirūpiṇī ॥
dvibhujādiprabhedena koṭibhedaṃ gatā punaḥ ॥
tathā caturvidhā loke kāmadā damanī purā ॥
hananī .... surā cānyā tathā ca mahiṣāsuraḥ ॥
khaḍgakheṭakarā devī pāśaśūladharā parā ॥
dhanurbāṇadharā cānyā ghaṇṭācakrakarā parā ॥
vajradaṇḍakarā devī daśabāhur athāparā ॥
kārtikā mudgaraṃ kuntaṃ kuṭhāraṃ tarjanī parā ॥
ḍamaruṃ vādayantī ca śaktihastā mahābalā ॥
padmābhayakarā devī darpaṇaṃ vimale kare ॥
evaṃvidhā surūpā tu siṃhasyopari saṃsthitā ॥
mahāmāyā prakartavyā siṃhayugmagatā parā ॥
padmāsanasthā varadā navayauvanabhāgyagā ॥
sarvābharaṇaśobhāḍhyā jayādyaiḥ parivāritā ॥
atha vā yudhyamānā tu mahiṣāsuraghātinī ॥
dhyātā prapūjitā nityaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradā ॥
yaḥ punar hemakāṣṭhādeḥ śailīṃ vā pratimāṃ śubhām ॥
[207]        kṛtvā pakveṣṭaśailādye prāsāde saṃniveśayet ॥
svamantravidhinā sicya vāpīkūpajalaṃ śubham ॥
ārāmapuṣpaśobhāḍhyaṃ devīm uddiśya tadvidhā ॥
tadā nimantraye nṝṇāṃ mātṛtantrārthabhāvinīm ॥
samastabhūtisambhāraṃ sarvopakaraṇair yutam ॥
kṛtvā maṭhottamaṃ vidvān devībhakteṣu vinyaset ॥
jalavahnitivāmaśvadānaṃ kanyāditarpaṇam ॥
yathā sampadyate śakra tathā kāryaṃ sadādarāt ॥
maṇiratnavitānaṃ ca ghaṇṭādyaṃ vādanāni ca ॥
āyudhāni vicitrāṇi deyāni vividhāni ca ॥
kalaśā hemapātrāṇi dhūpamālyakriyā vibhoḥ ॥
dīpāḥ sarvādarāt kāryā mahāphalaśubhāvahāḥ ॥
evaṃ kṛtvā samāpnoti vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ॥
phalaṃ yajñatapodānatīrthayātrādikaṃ ca yat ॥
iha loke mahākīrtiḥ paratra ca paraṃ padam ॥
śāśvataṃ cāvyayaṃ nityam agamyaṃ yat surādibhiḥ ॥
tat phalaṃ labhate śakra yatra devī parā śivā ॥
brahmovāca ॥
bhadrā vṛddhiḥ śivā śāntir āgneyādivyavasthitā ॥
pūjitavyā prayatnena ihaivārthaphalapradā ॥
[208]        yaṃ yaṃ kāmaṃ cintayitvā pūjayanti manīṣiṇaḥ ॥
taṃ taṃ dadāti devendra kṣemākṣemaphalaṃ nṛṇām ॥
mantrair vidyāvidhānena astraiḥ pāśupatādikaiḥ ॥
kṣemādivarṇanihitaiḥ pratyekaṃ phaladāḥ kriyāḥ ॥
rūpakaiś cānubhāvādi prādurbhāvai .... mādikaiḥ ॥
dānavāntakarīṃ devīṃ bahudhā pūjayec chivām ॥
maṅgalā śivabhedakā mahiṣāsuraghātinī ॥
devī cāmuṇḍarūpeṇa kṣemākṣemaphalapradā ॥
saumyā śubhāvahā divyā kriyābhedena siddhidā ॥
asurā jagatā raudrī samayā guhasambhavā ॥
kṣemapradā rūpaveśī mahāvidyā tu pāvakī ॥
ghoṇā raudrī tathā pāśā śrīghoṇā tu prakīrtitā ॥
vidyā vācakatvena rūpatvenāparājitāḥ ॥
vāruṇī damanī brāhmī gaurī pratyaṅgirā matā ॥
etāsāṃ navadā[-dhā?] bhinnā vāmadakṣiṇagāruḍāḥ ॥
kulamānasameṣaśavarādiṣu siddhidāḥ ॥
abhyarthanā mahābhāgā vaiparītye bhayāvahā ॥
pūjitāḥ sthāpitāḥ śakra na ca rāṣṭrabhayaṅkarāḥ  ॥ ॥
kulāni naiṣṭhikān siddhā kulasambhavavarjitāḥ ॥
kṣemādyā pūjanīyās tu sthāpanīyāḥ kathaṃcana  ॥ ॥
brahmovāca ॥
śruṇu śakra pravakṣyāmi catuḥṣaṣṭi prarūpakam ॥
[209]        mahābhāgyaviśeṣeṇa dānadharmasamanvitam ॥
pūjayed vijayāṃ devīṃ śūlapustākṣadhāriṇīm ॥
varadodyatāṃ siṃhasthāṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakām ॥
maṅgalāṃ pūjayed devīṃ sarvasiddhipradāyikām ॥
siṃhapadmāsanāṃ saumyāṃ jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇīm ॥
śūlākṣasūtrā varadām abhayaṃ dakṣiṇe kare ॥
bhadrāṃ tu pūjayed devīṃ bhadrāsanasamavasthitām ॥
nīlotpalakarāṃ devīṃ śūlākṣasūtradhāriṇīm ॥
dhṛtī tu pūjayed devīṃ [caṇḍāsana]vyavasthitām ॥
padmadarpaṇadhāriṇīṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
śāntiḥ śāntipradā kāryā padmāsanavyavasthitā ॥
akṣasūtradharā devī varadodyatapāṇikā ॥
śivā vṛṣāsanā kāryā trinetrā varapāṇikā ॥
ḍamarūragadhātrī ca triśūlā nirbhayabhadrā ॥
jaṭāmukuṭamarddhendu vāsukīkṛtakaṅkaṇam ॥
padmā padmāsanasthā tu pratihāropaśobhitā ॥
tuṣṭiṃ sampūjayed devīṃ kalaśopari saṃsthitām ॥
pāśāṅkuśakarāṃ saumyāṃ padmasvastikadhāriṇīm ॥
umā padmāsanā kāryā mṛgasiṃhaparivṛtā ॥
yogapaṭṭottarāsaṅgā kamaṇḍalukarā śubhā ॥
[210]        varadodyataśūlākṣajayādyaiḥ parivāritāḥ ॥
grahamālā virājantī patrakuṇḍaladhāriṇī ॥
śivārcanaratā devī puṣṭiḥ kāryā yathā śruṇu ॥
khaḍgahastā mahārūpā carmamudgaradhāriṇī ॥
aśvarūpā surūpā ca puṣṭir evaṃvidhā bhavet ॥
śivā padmāsanā kāryā padmaśrīphaladhāriṇī ॥
gajair gṛhītakalaśaiḥ snāpyamānā suśobhanā ॥
ṛddhiṃ pūjayate padmasthā varadābhayapāṇikām ॥
dānodyatakarāṃ devīṃ vyāharanti śubhāni ca ॥
vīṇāvādinī padmasthā ratiḥ kāryā suśobhanā ॥
dīptīḥ kha .... samā kāryā ki.... dhāriṇī ॥
siṃhāsanasthitā vāpi padmahastā suśobhanā ॥
nīlotpalasthitā kāntiḥ kalaśotpaladhāriṇī ॥
mālābhūṣitā devī sarvabharaṇacarcitā  ॥ ॥
paryaṅkodarasaṃsthā tu padmā kāyā suśobhanā ॥
śaṅkhapustakadhārī ca sraṅmālābharaṇojjvalā ॥
lakṣmīḥ padmasthitā kāryā padmaśrīphaladhāriṇī ॥
īśvarī vṛṣasaṃsthā tu jayamukuṭadhāriṇī ॥
candrārddhaśekharā devī triśūloragadhāriṇī ॥
kapālasaṃsthā bhaved vṛddhir bījapūrakadhāriṇī ॥
yogapaṭṭottarāsaṅgā ratnamālādharā śubhā ॥
[211]        śākrī gajāsanā kāryā vajrahastā suśobhanā ॥
jayāvatī siṃhasaṃsthā śaraśṛṅgadharī śubhā ॥
haṃsānanā bhaved brāhmī muñjamekhalabhūṣitā ॥
caturvaktrā surūpādyadaṇḍakāṣṭhakamaṇḍaluḥ ॥
akṣasūtraṃ ca bibhrāṇā suvahastākṣadhāriṇī ॥
yogapaṭṭakabaddhāṅgī savedodgiraṇānanā ॥
jayantī kuṇḍasaṃsthā tu kuntaśūlakarā śubhā ॥
khaḍgakheṭakarā kāryā varadābhayapāṇikā ॥
aparājitā tu kartavyā siṃhārūḍhā mahābalā ॥
pinākaśārṅgahastā tu khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇī ॥
trinetrā sujaṭājūṭā vāsukikṛtabhūṣaṇā ॥
pāśāṅkuśadharā devī ajitā makarāsanā ॥
rathasthā mānasī kāryā ghaṇṭāmudgaradhāriṇī ॥
vajrāṅkuśadharā devī sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ॥
phalanīlotpalakarā utsaṅge bālakānvitā ॥
māyā pāśāṅkuśā kāryā māyā cāmaradhāriṇī ॥
śūkarasthā mahāmāyā mohayantī carācaram ॥
mohinī mṛgam ārūḍhā dhvajaśūlākṣadhāriṇī ॥
veṇuhastā surāpātrā hārakeyūrabhūṣitā ॥
lālasā gajasaṃsthā tu varadarpaṇadhāriṇī ॥
[212]        nāvā śakaṭam ārūḍhā vimalā harṣavarcasā ॥
akṣasūtradhārī ca kamaṇḍalukarā śubhā ॥
gaurī padmāsanasthā tu varadābhayapāṇikā ॥
akṣasūtrakarā devī kamaṇḍalukarā śubhā ॥
hiraṇyasiṃham āsīnā varadābhayapāṇikā ॥
kauśikī kauśikārūḍhā triśūlamuṇḍadhāriṇī ॥
kapālakartikā hastā ghorarūpā mahābalā ॥
.... ghanasthānakartavyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ॥
vīṇāvādanaśīlā ca vījyamānā suśobhanā ॥
durgādigājamattārī [?] pṛṣṭhagā tripurasundarī ॥
pinākaśārṅgacarmāsi dhāriṇī mahiṣāsanā ॥
tacchīrṣasthamahiṣaiḥ subhaṭaiḥ parivāritā ॥
ratnakhaḍgavastreṇa naitraiś ca tarjitā … mahābalaiḥ ॥
veṣṭitā nāgapāśena kecid bhinnā gatāsavaḥ ॥
devī śūlagatā kāryā sarve te saṃmukhānanāḥ ॥
pādapadmāsane caivamakaṃ [?] siṃhaniveśitam ॥
evaṃ vidhena rūpeṇa durgā kāryā na cānyathā ॥
padmopari samāsīnā kriyā kāryā suśobhanā ॥
puṣpamālā virājantī prabhāmaṇḍalabhūṣitā ॥
śrutamekhaladhārī ca sarvamīhasamanvitā ॥
vratasthārundhatī kāryā puṣpodakakarā śubhā ॥
[213]        paṭṭe tu pūjayed devīṃ ghaṇṭāhastāṃ triśūlinīm ॥
varṇā surūpā kartavyā lambakarṇā suśobhanā ॥
kapālinī sadā kāryā kapālaśūladhāriṇī ॥
rudrarūpā tathā raudrī vṛṣasaṃsthā suśobhanā ॥
jaṭāmukuṭasārddhendu vāsukīkṛtakaṅkaṇā ॥
rathasthāṃ pūjayet kālīṃ kapālaśūladhāriṇīm ॥
māyūrī śikhi saṃsthā tu pāśaśaktidharā śubhā ॥
trinetrā vārakte kartavyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ॥
candrāsanā samārūḍhā padmasvastikadhāriṇī ॥
surūpāṃ pūjayed devīṃ śūlapaṭṭiśadhāriṇīm ॥
jaṭoragaśiraścandrabhūṣitāṃ śivarūpiṇīm ॥
vipulā śakra (?)nāśārthaṃ vajracakradhanurdharā ॥
aśvārūḍhā sadā pūjyā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā ॥
ambikāṃ śūlasūtrākṣadhāriṇīṃ pūjayet tadā ॥
padmāsanasthāṃ devendra carcikāṃ sāmprataṃ śruṇu ॥
pretārūḍhāṃ mahādevīṃ nāmābharaṇabhūṣitām ॥
ūrdhvakeśīṃ karālāṃ tu muṇḍakartikadhāriṇīm ॥
surapūjitā tu kartavyā gajārūḍhā mahābalā ॥
akṣāṅkuśadharā devī tathā vaivasvatīṃ śruṇu ॥
[214]        kapāladaṇḍahastā tu sūkarasthā yamāsanā ॥
kaumārī pūjyate śakramayūrāsanaśaktibhṛt ॥
tridaṇḍī bālarūpā ca raktamālyā sakukkuṭā ॥
māheśvarī vṛṣārūḍhā trinetrā śūladhāriṇī ॥
vīṇāvādanaśīlā ca hārakeyūrabhūṣitā ॥
śaṅkhacakradharā pūjyā vaiṣṇavī garuḍāsanā ॥
vanamālādharāṃ devīṃ mahālakṣmīṃ tathā śruṇu ॥
gandhārā śivaśūlā ca nṛtyamānā kapālinī ॥
saumyānanā susampūrṇā trinetrā muṇḍabhūṣitā ॥
tasyās tu mukhagā kāryā kārtikā vratasaṃsthitā ॥
vāmadakṣiṇako kāryau śūlotpalakarau narau ॥
siṃhapadmāsanā devī vṛṣapretāsanā tathā ॥
kauśikī haṃsayānena kapālaśūladhāriṇī ॥
śivadūtī prakartavyā yauvanābharaṇojjvalā ॥
śivavaktrāṅkuśadharā nirmāṃsā koṭarekṣaṇā ॥
pravālasadṛśau cauṣṭau vikāsā nāyakabandhanā ॥
mudrālaṅkṛtakarṇā ca vāmorukarasaṃsthitā ॥
pīṭhasaṃsthena yānena tathā ca vikṛtānanā ॥
cāmuṇḍā pūjyate devī ūrdhvakeśī bhayānanā ॥
pretāsanasthā nirmāṃsā kṣāmā khaṭvāṅgadhāriṇī ॥
kapālaśūlahastā ca kartikābharaṇojvalā ॥
[215]        śivā vajrāṅkuśamudrāṃ vastrai ca lambodarasaṃsthitā ॥
gajacarmāmbarā raudrā nānābharaṇabhūṣitā ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭes tu devīnāṃ rūpakaṃ kathitaṃ tava ॥
sāmprataṃ ca surādhyakṣa mahābhāgyaṃ vadāmy aham ॥
iti catuḥṣaṣṭidevīrūpakam ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭidevīsthāpanapūjanam ॥
brahmovāca ॥
yathā śivasya caivānyo vidyate śakrapūjane ॥
tathā cānyaṃ na paśyāmi catuḥṣaṣṭhestu pūjanāt ॥
suvarṇabhūmidānasya godānasya ca yat phalam ॥
tadādhikaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭi ....... smṛter iha ॥
aśvamedhasahasreṇa vājapeyaśatena ca ॥
yat phalaṃ labhate kartā phalaṃ tatsthāpane 'dhikam ॥
gaṅgāsnānasahasreṇa sarvatīrthaplavena ca ॥
yat phalaṃ labhate kartā dhāraṇāt tat phalaṃ labhet ॥
tasmāt kiṃ bahunā śakra bhāṣitena punaḥ punaḥ ॥
yadīcched vimalān bhogān nāthaṃ ca vipulaṃ yadi ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭes tu kartavyā sthāpanā surapūjita ॥
prāsāde maṇḍape gehe citre vastre ca lekhitā ॥
[216]        catuḥṣaṣṭir mahābhāga pūjitā rājyadā matā ॥
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ tāmrīṃ ratnajāṃ śailakāṣṭajām ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya samprāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya dvātriṃśat ṣoḍaśātha vā ॥
vasusaṅkhyam athāpīha sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam ॥
sarvagrahabhavāṃ pīḍāṃ karmajāṃ doṣajām api ॥
bhare kṛtvā na sandeha ekānte sthāpayed yadi ॥
mahālakṣmīṃ surādhyakṣa sarvāpatsu nibarhiṇīm  ॥ ॥
yathā śive parāśaktir catuḥṣaṣṭau tu tādṛśī ॥
ācāryaṃ ca viśeṣeṇa bhaktiḥ kāryā surottama ॥
saṅkrāntau grahaṇe caiva vyatīpāte dinakṣaye ॥
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ navamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tathaiva ca ॥
amāvāsyāṃ viśeṣeṇa pūjā kāryā surottama ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭyāṃ tu vijñāte tantrāṣṭakasu (?) pārage ॥
devyāvatārakuśale dānaṃ deyaṃ sukhārthibhiḥ ॥
caturvedasahasreṇa kṛtena yat phalaṃ labhet ॥
tat phalaṃ cādhikaṃ labhyaṃ tasyā kṛtvā pratiśrayam ॥
vājapeyasahasreṇa samyag iṣṭena yat phalam ॥
aśvamedham avāpnoti catuḥṣaṣṭau tad eva ca ॥
[217]        grahaṇe candrasūryābhyāṃ kurukṣetre ca yat phalam ॥
bhūmidānād avāpnoti yat phalaṃ pālane ‘dhikam ॥
kalau yuge mahāghore dharme ca tamasāvṛte ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭau tu kartavyā pūjā vākkāyakarmaṇā ॥
tathā ॥
narendreṇa viśeṣeṇa catuḥṣaṣṭhipratiṣṭhitā ॥
kartavyā rājyakāmena samyak te kathitaṃ mayā ॥
sthāpanaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi sarvakāmapradāyakam ॥
sarvakālaṃ prakartavyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ ॥
pūjā ca mahatī kāryā syān mahāphalakāṅkṣibhiḥ ॥
maṇihemaviśeṣāḍhyair vastraiḥ kīṭaphalodbhavaiḥ ॥
vividhaiḥ sitaraktaiś ca pītakṛṣṇaiḥ kramāt tathā ॥
gandhaiḥ sakarpūramadai ratnaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śubhaiḥ phalaiḥ ॥
kalyāṇādimahādhūpair dīpaiś candrodayādibhiḥ ॥
hemapātrasthitā kāryā karpāsā arthasiddhaye ॥
baligandhaiś carāsiddhiḥ pānapātraiḥ surottama ॥
kartavyaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ devī saṃsthāpane vidhiḥ ॥
dhvajārohe viśeṣeṇa vidhiḥ kāryaḥ phalārthibhiḥ ॥
[218]
atha dhvajārohaṇavidhiḥ ॥ 15 ॥
tatra devīpurāṇe ॥
dhvajadaṇḍaḥ śubhaḥ kārya āyāmapariṇāhataḥ ॥
daṇḍa …. dhvajaḥ kāryo bhūmer ūrdhvā ca lambitaḥ ॥
hemakiṅkiṇiśobhāḍhyo lāñchito vāhanādibhiḥ ॥
pratiṣṭhāvidhim āśritya homadānaṃ ca kārayet ॥
devīsampūjanaṃ kāryaṃ mantravidyāviśāradaiḥ ॥
catuḥṣaṣṭau tu vidyānām uddhāraṃ yas tu vindati ॥
sa guruḥ sthāpakaśreṣṭho vidyeśapadadāyakaḥ ॥
padamālādikair mantraiḥ sarvaṃ kāryaṃ yathāvidhi ॥
devīnāṃ havanaṃ pūjā nānyaiḥ kāryaṃ kadācana ॥
viphalaṃ tad bhavec chakra kartṛrāṣṭrabhayāvaham ॥
tasmāt suvidhim āśritya niveśe dhvajarohaṇam ॥
kṛtaṃ saphalatāṃ yāti kartṛrāṣṭrabhayādiṣu ॥
rakṣārthaṃ tu bhaved eko dvitīyo dhvajarohaṇe ॥
dharmadhvajas tṛtīyas tu caturthaḥ kāmiko dhvajaḥ ॥
yātrāyāṃ pañcamaḥ khyāto bahudhā parikīrtitaḥ ॥
anye ca bahavo bhedā ete yānti niveśane ॥
tathā |
devyo kaḥ kalaśaṃ siṃhaṃ dhvajāṅkaṃ dāpayec chubham ॥
[219]        pratiṣṭhā dviguṇaṃ sarvaṃ kalpanīyaṃ purā kṛtam ॥
yasmāt phalaṃ tu niṣpattau jāyate vidhipūrvakam ॥
tasmād dhvaje śilārohe mahādānaṃ pradāpayet ॥
saṅkarṇaṃ tu pratināde rakṣārthaṃ sthāpayed dhvajam ॥
svaliṅgakalaśe vātha vibhagne bhayadā khagāḥ ॥
kapotāḥ kauśikās tatra mahābhayakarā nṛṇām ॥
madavṛṣṭidā vatsa tataḥ …. kartavyo jayanor iha ॥
rakṣācihnaṃ tadā .... ॥
svavāhanāṃ … kaṃ kṛtvā parvādiṣu niveśayet ॥
kṣemārogyakaraṃ vatsa nṛpe rāṣṭrabhayaṃ samet ॥
subhikṣaṃ kṣemam ārogyaṃ nṛpasya ca janasya ca ॥
vāhanāṅkadhvajo deyo vicitraḥ kiṅkiṇīyutaḥ ॥
darpaṇaṃ cāmaraṃ ghaṇṭāṃ chatraṃ dadyāt tadūrdhvagam ॥
astrābhimantrito deyo dhvajo daṇḍān mahīm ataḥ ॥
gandhaiḥ phalādiratnaiś ca kalaśān sthāpayet purā ॥
tato dhvajaḥ pradātavyo dhyātvā kālaṃ śivāvidhim ॥
śive 'py evaṃ dhvajo deyo viṣṇau viṣṇupadaṃ nyaset ॥
citrādirakṣakān kṛtvā vāsudevaṃ sapañcakam ॥
[220]        dhvajaṃ khaḍgāṅkitaṃ dadyād viṣṇulokaphalapradam ॥
cakraṃ cānena vidhinā ropaṇīyaṃ śubhāvaham ॥
ihāyuḥ putradhanadaṃ dehānte bhavanaṃ svakam ॥
pañcaraṅgaṃ dhvajaṃ puṣpaṃ yo dadyāt parameśvari ॥
raṅgatantusamaṃ kālaṃ devaloke bhavet sukhī ॥
tathā |
ānandanṛpalokānāṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ mahāphalām ॥
na dhvajaḥ pradātavyo jayārthaṃ sukhasiddhidaḥ ॥
rekhādidaṇḍakaḥ kāryo yātrākāle svadiṅmukhaḥ ॥
mahādevīgaṇaṃ tatra gṛhāṣṭakasamanvitam ॥
kṛtvā tu labhate vatsa kāmakaṃ dvijasattamaḥ ॥
gobhūhiraṇyam aśvaikaṃ deyaṃ dānaṃ sukhārthibhiḥ ॥
pratiṣṭhagartasā .... dhvajāntā sarvakāmadā ॥
dhvajāḥ kāryās tu sarve 'pi nyūnam anyakṛtaṃ punaḥ ॥
tathā |
śakra uvāca ॥
devyā dhvajapramāṇaṃ ca daṇḍasya vidhilāñchanam ॥
dīyate ca yathā tāta tathā sarvaṃ bravīhi naḥ ॥
īśvara uvāca ॥
aṇḍajo maṇḍajaś cāpi dhvajaḥ keśavivarjitaḥ ॥
[221]        navaḥ samaś ca ślakṣṇaś ca prāsādadaṇḍam arcitam ॥
samaṃ sūkṣmabhṛtaṃ śubhraṃ śailaṃ vā dhātujaṃ tathā ॥
tasmin paṭṭe likhets siṃhaṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ॥
sacandrayā rocanayā lekhanyā dūrvayā likhet ॥
prāsādād dolamānaṃ kṣitivistārataḥ karam ॥
dhvajapaṭṭe tu kartavyā daśa yā dikṣu devatāḥ ॥
sarvavāhanasaṃsthābhyāṃ lakṣitāḥ sahajena ca ॥
kiṅkiṇī cāmaropetā ghaṇṭādarpaṇaśobhitāḥ ॥
hutaṃ homo mahāprājñaḥ sahakāradalānvitam ॥
mahāmaṅgalaśabdena devyāḥ kuryāt prapūjanam ॥
sugandhipuṣpanaivedyair yathāvibhavavistaram ॥
kanyakāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ bhojyāḥ śarkarādadhipāyasaiḥ ॥
bhūtānāṃ tu baliṃ datvā tathā tam uparohayet ॥
sarvaṃ kāmam avāpnoti vidyādharapatir bhavet ॥
modate vividhair bhogaiḥ sarvavidyārthapāragaiḥ ॥
atha vā haimaṃ raupyaṃ vā vārkṣaṃ pārthivaśailajam ॥
kārayen mṛgarājaṃ tu mahākarimadāpaham ॥
mahāgajanakhotkhātamuktāphalakadambakam ॥
evaṃvidhaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā navamyāṃ pūjayec chivām ॥
sopavāsaḥ śucir dakṣaḥ sarvasaṅgavivarjitaḥ ॥
kanyakāḥ pūjayitvā tu viprān vedavidas tathā ॥
[222]        devībhaktāḥ sadācārās trikālaṃ havane ratāḥ ॥
te yathāśaktitas toṣyā dhvajārohaṇakarmaṇi ॥
kanyā devī svayaṃ proktā kanyārūpā triśūlinī ॥
yāvat sākṣatayoniḥ syāt tāvad devī surārihan ॥
dvijo brahmā śivo viṣṇuḥ svakīyavratapālakāḥ ॥
pūjitais taiḥ sadaivātra sarvadevās tu pūjitāḥ ॥
dīnāndhakṛpaṇādīnām annaṃ deyaṃ svaśaktitaḥ ॥
yathā sarvagatāṃ devīṃ taduddiśyākṣayaṃ bhavet ॥
nānābhakṣākṣatadadhidūrvā utkārikānvitam ॥
baliṃ tu sarvabhūtebhyo datvā dikṣu nivedayet ॥
vajraghoṇaṃ tathā japtvā aṣṭaviṃśākṣarāpi vā ॥
siṃhastaṃbhe samāropya sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam ॥
vedadhvanimahāmantrakālikā carcikāpadam ॥
yasya siṃhaparadhyāne deyaṃ pūrvopakalpitam ॥
evaṃ saṃvastrasaṃvītaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ॥
devyā mahādhvajaṃ nyasya śeṣāṇām anuvinyaset ॥
brahmaviṣṇvindrarudrāṇāṃ somasūryadivaukasām ॥
dhvajadānaṃ mahādānaṃ sarvadānottamottamam ॥
yāvan na dīyate śakra dhvajaḥ prāsādamūrdhani ॥
tāvan na bhavati śreyaḥ prāsāde yad avāpyate ॥
dhvajaśūnyeṣu bhūtāni nāgagandharvarākṣasāḥ ॥
vidravanti mahātmāno nānābādhāḥ prakurvate ॥
tasmād devagṛhadvāre puraparvatapattane ॥
ucchritāṃ śāntikarmāya dhvajāḥ śukrasamāhitāḥ ॥
na hi cānyadhvajādānād uttamaṃ dṛśyate kvacit ॥
dānam iṣṭaṃ ca pūrta ca devyāṃ dīpas tathaiva ca ॥
anena vidhinā yas tu dhvajaṃ śakra niveśayet ॥
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti sa naraḥ śivatāṃ vrajet ॥
tasya darśanasaṃlāpād api pāparatā narāḥ ॥
vimuktāḥ sarvapāpebhyo divaṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ ॥
rājā cānena vidhinā devīlāñchanalāñchitaḥ ॥
śaṅkhacakravṛṣatārkṣyahaṃsabarhiṇavāraṇaiḥ ॥
sādaro bhaktim āsthāya dhvajayaṣṭiṃ samucchrayet ॥
na tasya saṃgare śaka vyādhayo na ca vairiṇaḥ ॥
na ca śastrakṛtā pīḍā bhaved dhvajasamucchrayāt ॥
tathā |
āsīl lubdho nirācāraḥ kaivarto matsyaghātakaḥ ॥
nepāle pacane śukra prayayau matsyabandhane ॥
sa ca vindhyāṭavīmadhye devīgeham apaśyata ॥
tasya jālaṃ prabhūtena śīrṇaṃ kālena bhārgavaḥ ॥
devyā gṛhāgrato vṛkṣe tasmiṃs tat samalambayat ॥
tato gṛhaṃ svakaṃ vatsa punas tatraiva kālataḥ ॥
[224]        āgatya karpaṭakhaṇḍaṃ ॥
datvāgataḥ punaḥ kālān mṛto ‘bhūt khaḍgādhipaḥ ॥
vidyādharapatiś citraḥ sarvavidyārthapāragaḥ ॥
tathā svabhavane devyā pūrvabhaktisamanvitaḥ ॥
dadau dhvajān sadā śubhrān kiṅkiṇīravakānvitān ॥
tenāsau karmaṇā pūrvasmaraṇād bhṛgusattama ॥
karmayogaṃ samāsthāya khaḍgarājas tv asāv abhūt ॥
anivāritaśaktiś ca sarvavidyādhareśvaraḥ ॥
tatra bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
nārada uvāca ॥
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi dhvajāropaṇam uttamam ॥
yad uktaṃ brahmaṇā puṇyaṃ tridaśādhipateḥ purā ॥
purā devāsure yuddhe yodhduṃ devair jayepsubhiḥ ॥
kṛtāny upari cihnāni vāhanāni dhvajāni tu ॥
lakṣmacihnadhvajaṃ ketur iti paryāyanāmabhiḥ ॥
kīrtitaḥ sa ca tasyeha pramāṇaṃ gadataḥ śruṇu ॥
dhvajo vaṃśasya kartavyo viśuddho ṛjur avraṇaḥ ॥
prāsādāyāmatulyas tu dhvajavaṃśaḥ pramāṇataḥ ॥
devāgārasya yā proktā mañjarī kalaśādayaḥ ॥
[225]        atha vā tatpramāṇas tu dhvajadaṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
antargṛhasya yā devī sūtrataḥ parikalpitā ॥
tasya vyāso bhaved vaṃśaḥ prāsādasya yadūttama ॥
atha vā mūlasūtreṇa vyāsas tasya gṛhasya ca ॥
prāsāda vyāsa ity ukta iha vai sa na saṃśayaḥ ॥
ketudaṇḍo bhaved vaṃśo na bhinno nānṛjus tathā ॥
dhanurdhvajo yugaṃ caiva nāvikā puruṣas tathā ॥
caturhastā bhavanty ete praśastāḥ kṛṣṇanandana ॥
aṣṭahastapramāṇas tu viṃśārddhasya pramāṇataḥ ॥
sāmānyo dhvajadaṇḍas tu sarvasādhāraṇo mataḥ ॥
uttamas tu dhvajo daṇḍaḥ smṛto hastāṃs tu ṣoḍaśa ॥
viṃśaddhastāt paraṃ daṇḍaṃ śakraketor na kārayet ॥
yugmahastas tu kartavyo dhvajadaṇḍo manīṣibhiḥ ॥
caturaṅgulavistīrṇaḥ suvrato [hy aṅgulopari ] ॥
nātisūkṣmo na ca sthūlaḥ na kāryonnataparva ca ॥
samaparvas tu kartavyaḥ sudṛḍhaḥ ślakṣṇa eva hi ॥
vakraḥ putravināśāya savraṇo 'rthavināśakaḥ ॥
rogado 'yugmahastas tu bhinne duḥkham anantakam ॥
[226]        karoti hāniṃ dharmasya hīno vāstupramāṇataḥ ॥
vaiṣamya samaparvā vai dadyāc chatrum adhonnataḥ ॥
jayo jayanto jaitreś ca śatruhantā jayāvahaḥ ॥
nandopanandanaś caiva indropendras tathaiva ca ॥
daśaite kīrtitā medā dhvajasyānandasammitāḥ ॥
dvihastas tu jayo daṇḍo jayanto dviguṇo mataḥ ॥
dvādaśārddhas tadā jaitraḥ śatruhantāṣṭa eva ca ॥
jayāvahas tu viṃśārddho nanda ādityasammitaḥ ॥
caturdaśopanandas tu indraḥ ṣoḍaśa ucyate ॥
upendro 'ṣṭādaśa prokto ānando viṃśatiḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
bhinno vai cakrasandhiśca na kāryo daṇḍa eva hi ॥
mūlasūtreṇa kartavyo vyāsato 'ntargṛhasya tu ॥
dhvajadaṇḍo mahābāhu atha vā vāstumānataḥ ॥
mañjarīmānato vāpi tadvad dhanaṃ tathā vibho ॥
patākā ca bhavet kāryā dhvajavaṃśāvalambinī ॥
devāgārasya śikharā tribhāgaparimārjanī ॥
sā coktā daśadhā vīramānato nāmatas tathā ॥
aṅkuraḥ pallavaś caiva skandhaḥ śākhā tathaiva ca ॥
[227]        patākā kadalī vīra ketur lakṣmo jayas tathā ॥
dhvajaś ca daśamaḥ proktaḥ sarvadevamayo nṛpa ॥
aṅkure dhvaṅgulaḥ proktaḥ pallavaś caturaṅgulaḥ ॥
skandhaḥ ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ proktaḥ śākhā cāṣṭāṅgulā matā ॥
ekādaśapatākā tu kadalī tu caturdaśa ॥
ketus tu ṣoḍaśaḥ prokto lakṣmāṣṭādaśa ucyate ॥
jayo viṃśati vai prokto vistārād aṅgulāni ca ॥
caturviṃśo dhvajaḥ proktaḥ patākā dvyaṅgulāni tu ॥
devāgārasya kuryāt prathamāṃśapramārjanam ॥
aṅkureti patākā sā vijñeyā yadunandana ॥
pallaveti dvitīyasya mārjanī parikīrtitā ॥
tribhāgamārjanī skandhaḥ śākhā vai pañcamasya tu ॥
ṣaṣṭhasyoktā patākā tu kadalī saptamasya tu ॥
aṣṭamasya tathā ketur lakṣmo vai navamasya tu ॥
tataś ca daśamasyokto jayanto yadunandana ॥
vṛṣasthānā ca mārjī ca dhvajas tu parikīrtitaḥ ॥
gajo meṣo 'tha mahiṣaḥ kabandhas tu ṛṣas tathā ॥
hariṇo 'tha naraś caiva śaraś ca narasattama ॥
sthānāny etāni cāṣṭau tu prayuktasya dhvajasya tu ॥
diśāṃ bhāgāt tu vai pūrvākrameṇa parikalpayet ॥
[228]        evaṃ daśāvidhāḥ proktāḥ patākās tatvadarśibhiḥ ॥
kartavyās tā yathā pūrvaṃ tac chṛṇuṣva narādhipa ॥
śuklavastramayī citrā sughaṇṭā sumanoramā ॥
nānā budbuda sampannā kiṅkiṇījālamaṇḍitā ॥
dhvajāgrāś caiva kartavyā devatāliṅgasūcakāḥ ॥
kāñcano vātha raupyo vā maṇiratnamayo 'tha vā ॥
raṅgakair likhito vātha taddāhanasamākṛtiḥ ॥
dhvajadaṇḍāgravinyastaḥ kartavyo yadunandana ॥
garutmāṃs tu dhvajo viṣṇor īśvarasya dhvajo vṛṣaḥ ॥
brahmaṇaḥ paṅkajaṃ kāryaṃ raveḥ dharmaḥ smṛto dhvajaḥ ॥
kuñjaro devarājasya yamasya mahiṣo dhvajaḥ ॥
haṃso jalādhipasyoktaḥ kuberasya naro dhvajaḥ ॥
baladevasya tālas tu kāmasya makaro dhvajaḥ ॥
siṃho dhvajas tu durgāyāḥ kīrtito yadunandana ॥
godhā dhvaja umādevyā revaṃtasya yaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
kacchapo varuṇasyokto vātasya hariṇo mataḥ ॥
pāvakasya tathā meṣa ākhur gaṇapateḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
brahmarṣīṇāṃ kuśaḥ prokta ity eṣā dhvajakalpanā ॥
yasya yo vāhanaḥ proktaḥ sa tasya dhvaja ucyate ॥
viṣṇor dhvajasya sauvarṇo daṇḍaḥ kāryo vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
[229]        patākā cāpi pītā syād garuḍasya samīpagā ॥
īśvarasya dhvajo daṇḍo rājato yadunandana ॥
patākā cāpi śuklā syād vṛṣabhasya samīpagā ॥
pitāmahadhvajo daṇḍaḥ smṛtas tāmramayo budhaiḥ ॥
padmavarṇā patākā syāt paṅkajasya samīpagā ॥
ādityasya ca sauvarṇo dhvajadaṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥
pañcavarṇapatākā syād dharmasyādho’grataḥ sthitā ॥
kiṅkiṇījālasaṃpannā nānābudbudasaṃmitā ॥
puṣpamālopasaṃpannā nānārājīvasaṃvṛtā ॥
indradhvajasya daṇḍo 'tha kāñcano yadunandana ॥
patākā bahuvarṇā syāt kuñjarasya samīpagā ॥
āyasaś cāpi daṇḍokto yamacihno vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥
patākā varṇataḥ kṛṣṇā mahiṣasya samīpagā ॥
jalādhipadhvajo daṇḍo rājataḥ parikīrtitaḥ ॥
patākā sarvataḥ śvetā ārā.... sya kathyate ॥
dhvaje cāpi kuberasya daṇḍo maṇimayaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
patākā cāpi raktā syān narapādasamīpagā ॥
baladevadhvajā daṇḍo rājato yadunandana ॥
patākā varṇataḥ śuklā tālasyādhogatā smṛtā ॥
kāmadhvaje trilohaḥ syād daṇḍo yadukulodvaha ॥
[230]        patākā rohiṇī tatra makarasya samīpagā ॥
mayūraṃ kārtikeyasya cihnaṃ lokeṣu gīyate ॥
trilohadaṇḍam ārūḍhaṃ bahuratnavibhūṣitam ॥
bahuvarṇakacitrā tu patākā kathitā budhaiḥ ॥
hasti[dantabhavaṃ] daṇḍaṃ tathā kuryāt gaṇapater nṛpa  ॥
tāmradaṇḍasamārūḍhaṃ suśuddhaṃ supratiṣṭhitam ॥
śuklā patākā kartavyā supramāṇā mahīpate ॥
mātṝṇāṃ cāpi kartavyo naikarūpo dhvajo budhaiḥ  ॥ ॥
patākābhir anekābhir bahuvarṇābhir anvitaḥ ॥
revantasyāpi kartavyo vaje vāji narādhipa ॥
raktā patākā tatrāpi kartavyā yadunandana ॥
cāmuṇḍāmandire kāryaḥ śiromālādhvajas tathā ॥
nīlā patākā kartavyā daṇḍo lohamayas tathā ॥
rītīmayaś ca mātṝṇāṃ revantasyāpi kārayet ॥
godhvajas tāmramayaḥ patākā gopasannibhā ॥
svarṇadaṇḍa ........ dhvajo meṣasamanvitaḥ ॥
patākābahuvarṇādyā kartavyā yadunandana ॥
aśmasāramayo daṇḍo dhvajo vānasya ucyate ॥
patākā kṛṣṇavarṇā tu hariṇasya samīpagā ॥
bhagavatyadhvaje daṇḍaḥ sarvaratnamayaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
[231]        patākā ca trivarṇā syāt siṃhasyādhogatā nṛpa ॥
evaṃvidham idaṃ kṛtvā dhvajaṃ lakṣaṇalakṣitam ॥
adhivāsya tato rājan tata āropayed budhaḥ ॥
tataḥ sarvauṣadhībhis tu snāpayitvā prayatnataḥ ॥
samālabhya ca badhnīyān madhye pratisaraṃ nṛpaḥ ॥
kalpayitvā śubhāṃ vedīṃ kalaśair upaśobhitām ॥
tasyāṃ vedyāṃ samāropya tāṃ rātrīm adhivāsayet ॥
nānākusumacitrāṃ tu srajaṃ tasyāvalambayet ॥
abhyarcya ca prayatnena dhūpam asya nivedayet ॥
balikarma tataḥ kuryāt kṛśarāmodakādibhiḥ ॥
palalollopikābhiś ca dadhipāyasasūpakaiḥ ॥
uddiśya lokapālebhyo baliṃ datvā ca pāyasaiḥ ॥
brāhmaṇān svasti vācyātha kṛtvā puṇyāhamaṅgalam ॥
vāditrakṛtanirghoṣaṃ veṣṭitaṃ navavāsasā ॥
śubhe lagne dine ṛkṣe dhvajam āropayed budhaḥ ॥
vinyasya svarṇakalaśaṃ śubhraṃ rājaṃ dhvajasya tu ॥
evam āropayed yas tu dhvajaṃ devālayopari ॥
śriyam ātanute nityaṃ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim ॥
asurā vastum icchanti dhvajahīne svarālaye ॥
tasmād devālayaṃ prājño dhvajahīnaṃ na kārayet ॥
[232]        mantras ca sthāpane prokto vidhānajñair dhvajasya tu ॥
ehy ehi Bhagavan deva devavāhana khecara ॥
śrīkara śrīnivāseśa jaitra jetropaśobhita ॥
vyomarūpa mahārūpa dharmātman namo 'stu te ॥
sāṃnidhyaṃ kuru daṇḍe ‘smin sākṣī ca dhruvatāṃ vraja ॥
kuru vṛddhiṃ sadā kartuḥ prāsādasyārkavallabha ॥
oṃ ehy ehi bhagavann īśvara vinirmita uparicara vāyumārgānusārin ॥
śrīnivāsa ripudhvaṃsa uparinilaya sarvadevamayāvyaya kuru sāṇnidhyaṃ śāntiṃ svastyayanaṃ mama bhavatu sarvavighnā apasarantu ॥
mantreṇānena rājendra śvabhre daṇḍaṃ niveśayet ॥
patākāṃ pūrvamantreṇa sthitvā pūrvamukho nṛpa ॥
kṣiped ūrdhvam athākāśaṃ prāsādaśikharād vibho ॥
patākāṃ tu tataḥ paśyet patantīṃ yadunandana ॥
prāsādapurato vīra patākā patate yadi ॥
indralokaṃ tadā kartur ādiśed yadunandana ॥
āgneyām agnilokaṃ tu yāmyāṃ yamasado bhavet ॥
nairṛtyāṃ nairitāṃ lokaṃ vāruṇe vāruṇaṃ vrajet ॥
yasya devasya yad veśma kṛtaṃ yadukulodvaha ॥
tasya lokam avāpnoti vṛṣaḥsthānaṃ gato yadi ॥
vāyavye vāyum āpnoti saumyāṃ ca somam āpnute ॥
aiśānyām īśam āpnoti kartā vai devaveśmani ॥
ya etat kārayed bhaktyā dhvajasyāropaṇaṃ raveḥ ॥
sahabhuktān parān bhāgān sūryaloke mahīyate ॥
tejasāṇḍajasaṃkāśaḥ kīrtyā cāmbujasaṃnibhaḥ ॥
dvijāṅkatulyaḥ prabhayā vikrameṇa ca gopateḥ ॥
atha jīrṇaliṅgoddhāraḥ ॥ 16 ॥
[tatra] bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
viṣṇur uvāca ॥
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sādhakānugrahaṃ bhuvi ॥
jīrṇaliṅgasya vidhivad uddhāraṃ śāstracoditam ॥
jīrṇaṃ kālena mahatā pracaled āsanād yadi ॥
dharaṇyāṃ vā mṛdutvena prāsādapatanena vā ॥
vāriṇā bhinnamūlaṃ vā dagdhaṃ vā kṣayam āgatam ॥
patitaṃ vā parāvṛttaṃ liṅgam āhur bhayānakam ॥
yathā śīrṇā narāḥ prāṇān saṃtyajanti tanukṣaye ॥
śivo jīrṇaṃ tathā liṅgaṃ saṃtyajed avicārataḥ ॥
yathā śivas tathā sūryo vyomam arcāṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥
vibhagno pūjayed yas tu bhojako bhāskarīṃ tanum ॥
viśeṣataḥ padmahīnāṃ sa yāti narakaṃ param ॥
anyāś ca vividhākārāḥ pratimāḥ śailadārujāḥ ॥
varjanīyāḥ prayatnena sādhakaiḥ śivaliṅgavat ॥
ātmanaḥ śivakāmena rājñas tad viśeṣataḥ ॥
mantreṇa liṅgam uddhārya yathāvad vidhipūrvakam ॥
brāhmaṇān śivabhaktāṃś ca bhojayitvā yathāvidhi ॥
[235]        prīyatāṃ śiva ity uktvā dakṣiṇābhiś ca pūjayet ॥
darbhāṃl lājāṃś ca gandhāṃś ca dhūpapuṣpabaliṃ punaḥ ॥
āhṛtya snāpayel liṅgaṃ jayāśair maṅgalais tathā ॥
śaṅkhavāditranirghoṣair vedagītasvanais tathā ॥
pragṛhya tāmrakalaśaṃ toyapūrṇam akutsitam ॥
sarvabījāni gandhāṃś ca dhānyaṃ caiva yathoditam ॥
sarvāṇi caiva ratnāni vṛṣabhaṃ ca hiraṇyakam ॥
prakṣipya kalaśe tasmin darbhaviṣṭarasaṃyutam ॥
anudvignaḥ śucir bhūtvā mantram etam udāharet ॥
jīrṇaliṅgaṃ parityajya maheśvara surādhipa ॥
pūrṇaiḥ sukalaśair devaṃ snāpayāmi vṛṣadhvajam ॥
ity uktvā mantravid evaṃ snāpayitvā samāhitaḥ ॥
sampūjya gandhamālyaiś ca baliṃ kṛtvā tu bhaktitaḥ ॥
vṛṣasya kakudā gṛhya śivaliṅgaṃ pracālayet ॥
godhāmarajjusūtreṇa samena sudṛḍhena ca ॥
dvijaḥ sampūjanaṃ gṛhya tal liṅgaṃ udake saṃnidhāpayet ॥
agnau ca taṃ kṣiped devaṃ ghṛtābhyaktas tataḥ param ॥
brāhmaṇān purataḥ kṛtvā mantrī saṃyamitendriyaḥ ॥
tatas tu 'gurave dadyātsthāpakamya yathocitam ॥
vṛṣabhaikādaśa gāś ca dadyād vatrayugāṣṭakam ॥
śayyāsanasuvarṇādibhūmiratnāni kuṇḍale ॥
[236]        tatas tu gurave dadyād gandhamālyapavitrakam ॥
ity evaṃ dhāturatnānāṃ śailajānāṃ samāsataḥ ॥
liṅgānāṃ jīrṇabhinnānāṃ parityāgavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ॥
devīpurāṇe ॥
brahmovāca ॥
purā devair yathā devāḥ sthāpitāḥ pūrvadharmibhiḥ ॥
te tathā pūjanīyās tu magarādyāḥ surottamāḥ ॥
yathā śivodbhavaṃ liṅgaṃ cālitaṃ bhayadaṃ bhavet  ॥ ॥
evaṃ sūryaharibrahmā cālitā bhayadāḥ smṛtāḥ ॥
sarvabhūmaṇḍalīkādyair ye devāḥ saṃniveśitāḥ ॥
te tathā pālanīyāś ca cālitās tv aśubhāvahāḥ ॥
devārcā naiva saṃcālyā mṛnmayāpi bhayāvahā ॥
hemād adoṣadā vatsa pūrvām uddhṛtya sthāpitā ॥
vṛddhikāmabhiyāśu jāsu sthitā sthitiḥ ॥
mahādurbhikṣajananī marakā vṛṣṭidā bhavet ॥
kartṛgotrasya bhayadā ripuvṛddhivivarddhinī ॥
śīrṇo devālayaḥ kālād yadā saṃcālayet kvacit ॥
bhairave bhittikūlādivilekhāc calitaḥ kvacit ॥
tadā liṅgavidhānena yāvat prāsādasaṃsthitiḥ ॥
niṣpattir bhavati sthāne tatra tāvan mahotsavaḥ ॥
kartavyaṃ pratyahaṃ deve pratiṣṭhāvat praveśanam ॥
[237]        nimne 'py evaṃ vidhiṃ kṛtvā prāsādaṃ kalpayet punaḥ ॥
śilānyāsādividhivad vidhiṃ kuryād yathocitam ॥
ityevaṃ bahuśāstrajñaiḥ khyāto 'yaṃ manujādibhiḥ ॥
liṅge hastāś ca kartavyāḥ pratimāyās tathocchrayaḥ ॥
atha vā siddhadevānāṃ nandyādivihitaukasām ॥
mahāpūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā yāgaṃ rudrakumāravat ॥
śivādyāḥ pūjayitvā tu prāsādaṃ kalpayet punaḥ  ॥ ॥
śilānyāsādividhivad vidhiṃ kṛtvā yathocitam ॥
tathā |
vasiṣṭha uvāca ॥
mātṝṃs tu bhairavaṃ durgāṃ śīrṇaveśmasamanvitām ॥
prāsādaṃ cālayitvā tu kuryād yas tu dvijottamaḥ ॥
pakveṣṭadāruśailaṃ vā tasya puṇyavidhiṃ śṛṇu ॥
śivabrahmendraviṣṇūnāṃ savituś ca dvijottama ॥
nāntaḥ śasyate mārgo mātṝṇāṃ bhairavasya ca ॥
durgāyāḥ sarvakālaṃ tu mātṝṇāṃ caiva cālanam ॥
navabhedāḥ samākhyātā mātṝṇām eta eva hi ॥
āsāṃ tu nāyikā devī cāmuṇḍā rurughātinī ॥
tasyās tu cālanaṃ kuryāt puṇyāhe vārabhen naraḥ ॥
īśikhā vajrapāṇir vā sadamanī vāmanā ॥
[238]        bālānanihitā cāsāṃ hṛdayā mātṛjā śivā ॥
tataḥ saṅgamatoyena snāpayitvā vinikṣipet ॥
paśuraktaṃ vimiśraṃ ca madyaṃ sadyaḥkṛtānvitam ॥
datvā dikṣu samastāsu cālyed ambikāṃ tataḥ ॥
adhipoṣyātha vā mantrī yadā cālayate śivām ॥
tadā kṣemaṃ vijānīyād rājā pāti vasundharām ॥
cālitā dakṣiṇāṃ neyā nottarāṃ tu diśaṃ nayet ॥
pūjyamānā sadā vatsa yāvat prāsādasiddhayaḥ ॥
niṣpanneṣu muhūrteṣu pratiṣṭhāvidhim ārabhet ॥
pratimāṃ tu yadā jīrṇāṃ pīṭhikāṃ vāpi cālayet ॥
hṛdayaṃ mohayitvā tu tadāsyāś cālanaṃ bhavet ॥
hemalāṅgūlakaṃ kṛtvā śivā cālyā vipaścitā ॥
bāṇarajjvā nibaddhā tu vṛṣasya kakudā dvija ॥
kṣīravṛkṣād adhastāt tu kṛtvā vācāṃ mahīṃ nyaset ॥
śīrṇāṃ mahāmbhasi kṣiptvā tadā cānyāṃ niveśayet ॥
pratiṣṭhāvidhim āśritya sarvaṃ kuryād dvijottamaḥ ॥
svena svena vidhānena mantraiś cāṅgasamudbhavaiḥ ॥
sthāpayed devatāṃ vatsa mātṝṇāṃ mātṛko vidhiḥ  ॥ ॥
śīrṇo devotthaprāsādo yaiḥ punaḥ saṃskṛto dvija ॥
aśocyās te mahātmāno dhūtapāpā mahādhiyaḥ ॥
mūlāc chataguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ prāpnuyāj jīrṇakārakāt ॥
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena jīrṇasyoddhāram ācaret ॥
[239]        śūnyo devālayo vatsa yasmin deśe 'pi tiṣṭhati ॥
bhayaṃ tatra vijānīyād durbhikṣāt taskarādibhiḥ ॥
jīrṇa gehaṃ yathā dehī tyaktvā cānyat samāśrayet ॥
prāsādaṃ devatājīrṇaṃ tyaktvānyatra vrajet tathā ॥
tasmin śūnye 'pi vā devā āśritā bhayadā nṝṇām ॥
udvāsayanti tat sthānaṃ kaliṃ kurvanti dāruṇam ॥
śocyās te bhayavinyastāḥ syur lokā nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥
gṛhopasṛṣṭā vidviṣṭā yānti nāśaṃ samantataḥ ॥
tasmād vatsa sa kāryaṃ pūjārthaṃ nānyathā nyaset ॥
devaṃ devyādayaś cāpi jīrṇena ca niveśayet ॥
yathā sadā bhavet pūjā tathā kāryaṃ vipaścitā ॥
kartā śatādhikaṃ maulaṃ prāpnuyād avicārayan ॥
rājā ṣaṣṭhaṃ samāpnoti prajā rāṣṭraṃ ca sidhyati ॥
mūlād aṣṭaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ jīrṇasaṃskārake bhavet ॥
tasmād anāthajīrṇeṣu kāryaṃ saṃskaraṇaṃ mune ॥
anāthā vā sanāthā vā pūjanīyāḥ sadā surāḥ ॥
viśeṣeṇa tu saṃpūrṇās te pūjyāḥ śataśo mune ॥
iti kṛtyakalpatarau pratiṣṭhākāṇḍaṃ sampūrṇam ॥
śubham astu ॥
iti śrīmahārājagovindacandrasaṃdhivigrahika-śrīlakṣmīdharabhaṭṭaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
pratiṣṭhākāṇḍaḥ sampūrṇaḥ ॥
